      let __savedState = null;
      const lib = {
        getAsset(id) { return window.__ASSETS__[id]; },
        log(msg) { console.log('[game]', msg); },
        showGameParameters(opts) { console.log('[showGameParameters]', opts && opts.name); },
        editMenu: {
          open(opts) { console.log('[editMenu.open]', opts && opts.name); },
          close() { console.log('[editMenu.close]'); }
        },
        shouldUseTouchControls() {
          return window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches;
        },
        async getUserGameState() {
          return { userId: 'local', gameId: 'ludo-dethroned-5way', state: __savedState, success: true };
        },
        async saveUserGameState(state) {
          __savedState = state;
          return { userId: 'local', gameId: 'ludo-dethroned-5way', state, success: true };
        },
        async deleteUserGameState() {
          __savedState = null;
          return { success: true };
        },
        async llm(options) {
          try {
            const resp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
              method: 'POST',
              headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
              body: JSON.stringify({
                model: 'claude-sonnet-4-6',
                max_tokens: 1000,
                messages: options.messages,
                system: options.systemPrompt
              })
            });
            const data = await resp.json();
            const text = (data.content || [])
              .filter(b => b.type === 'text')
              .map(b => b.text)
              .join('\n');
            return { text };
          } catch (e) {
            throw e;
          }
        }
      };

      (function() {
        var __baseW = 1280, __baseH = 720;
        function __fit() {
          var el = document.getElementById('game-container');
          if (!el) return;
          var s = Math.min(window.innerWidth / __baseW, window.innerHeight / __baseH) * 0.98;
          el.style.setProperty('--fit-scale', s);
        }
        window.addEventListener('resize', __fit);
        window.addEventListener('orientationchange', __fit);
        window.addEventListener('load', __fit);
        __fit();
        setTimeout(__fit, 0);
        setTimeout(__fit, 300);
      })();

        function openPlayerProfile(playerIdx) {
            if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume();
            activeProfilePlayerIdx = playerIdx;
            const player = window.gameConfig.players[playerIdx];
            profileName.innerText = player.name;
            profileName.style.color = getPlayerColor(playerIdx);

            profileIcon.style.borderColor = getPlayerColor(playerIdx);

                btn.className = 'profile-emoji-btn';

                btn.style.cursor = 'pointer';
                btn.style.fontSize = '24px';
                btn.style.padding = '8px';
                btn.style.borderRadius = '8px';
                btn.style.background = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
                btn.style.transition = 'all 0.2s';
                btn.onclick = (event) => {
                    event.stopPropagation();
                    const rect = profileModal.getBoundingClientRect();
                    spawnReaction(playerIdx, e, rect.left + rect.width / 2, rect.top);
                    playSound('ui_click_sound', 0.8);
                };
                profileEmojis.appendChild(btn);
            });

            profileModal.style.display = 'block';
            gameState.isRuleActive = true; 
        }

        profileSendBtn.onclick = (e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            const text = profileInput.value.trim();
            if (text && activeProfilePlayerIdx !== null) {
                speak(activeProfilePlayerIdx, text);
                profileModal.style.display = 'none';
                gameState.isRuleActive = false;
            }
        };

        profileVoiceBtn.onclick = (e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();

            const originalText = profileVoiceBtn.innerHTML;
            profileVoiceBtn.innerHTML = '🛑 RECORDING...';
            profileVoiceBtn.style.background = '#c0392b';
            profileVoiceBtn.classList.add('pulse-red');

                profileVoiceBtn.innerHTML = originalText;
                profileVoiceBtn.style.background = '#2980b9';

        async function preloadImage(id, customId = null) {
          const asset = lib.getAsset(id);
          if (!asset) return;
          return new Promise((resolve) => {
            const img = new Image();
            img.onload = () => {
              assetCache[customId || id] = img;
              resolve(img);
            };
            img.onerror = () => resolve(null);
            img.src = asset.url;
          });
        }

        const CLASSIC_COLORS = ['#2980b9', '#e67e22', '#27ae60', '#c0392b', '#f1c40f'];

        window.setGender = (g) => {

        // --- Tutorial Slider Logic ---
        const TUTORIAL_SLIDES = [
          { icon: '🎲🎲', titleKey: 'dual_dice_title', descKey: 'dual_dice_desc' },
          { icon: '🖐️', titleKey: 'five_player_title', descKey: 'five_player_desc' },
          { icon: '🎲🎲', titleKey: 'double_six_title', descKey: 'double_six_desc' },

          { icon: '🏆', titleKey: 'victory_cond_title', descKey: 'victory_cond_desc' },
          { icon: '✨', titleKey: 'lucky_wish_guide_title', descKey: 'lucky_wish_guide_desc' },
          { icon: '👇', titleKey: 'hand_pointer_title', descKey: 'hand_pointer_desc' },
          { icon: '⏳', titleKey: 'turn_timer_title', descKey: 'turn_timer_desc' },
          { icon: '💬', titleKey: 'social_features_title', descKey: 'social_features_desc' }
        ];
        let currentTutorialSlide = 0;
        let tutorialAutoAdvanceTimer = null;

        function updateTutorialSlider() {
          const slider = document.getElementById('tutorial-slider');
          const dots = document.getElementById('tutorial-dots');
          if (!slider) return;

          slider.innerHTML = '';
          TUTORIAL_SLIDES.forEach((slide, index) => {
            const slideEl = document.createElement('div');
            slideEl.className = `tutorial-slide ${index === currentTutorialSlide ? 'active' : (index < currentTutorialSlide ? 'prev' : '')}`;
            slideEl.innerHTML = `
              <div class="slide-icon">${slide.icon}</div>
              <h3 class="slide-title">${t(slide.titleKey)}</h3>
              <p class="slide-desc">${t(slide.descKey)}</p>
            `;
            slider.appendChild(slideEl);
          });

          dots.innerHTML = '';
          TUTORIAL_SLIDES.forEach((_, index) => {
            const dot = document.createElement('div');
            dot.className = `dot ${index === currentTutorialSlide ? 'active' : ''}`;
            dot.onclick = () => {
              currentTutorialSlide = index;
              resetTutorialTimer();
              updateTutorialSlider();
            };
            dots.appendChild(dot);
          });
          
          const closeBtn = document.getElementById('guide-close-btn');
          if (closeBtn) {
              closeBtn.innerText = currentTutorialSlide === TUTORIAL_SLIDES.length - 1 ? t('lets_play') : `${t('ready')} (${currentTutorialSlide + 1}/${TUTORIAL_SLIDES.length})`;
          }
        }

        function nextTutorialSlide() {
          currentTutorialSlide = (currentTutorialSlide + 1) % TUTORIAL_SLIDES.length;
          updateTutorialSlider();
        }

        function prevTutorialSlide() {
          currentTutorialSlide = (currentTutorialSlide - 1 + TUTORIAL_SLIDES.length) % TUTORIAL_SLIDES.length;
          updateTutorialSlider();
        }

        function resetTutorialTimer() {
          if (tutorialAutoAdvanceTimer) clearInterval(tutorialAutoAdvanceTimer);
          tutorialAutoAdvanceTimer = setInterval(nextTutorialSlide, 5000);
        }

        const tutorialPrev = document.getElementById('tutorial-prev');
        const tutorialNext = document.getElementById('tutorial-next');
        const guideSkip = document.getElementById('guide-skip-btn');

        if (tutorialPrev) {
          tutorialPrev.onclick = () => {
            prevTutorialSlide();
            resetTutorialTimer();
          };
        }
        if (tutorialNext) {
          tutorialNext.onclick = () => {
            nextTutorialSlide();
            resetTutorialTimer();
          };
        }
        if (guideSkip) {
          guideSkip.onclick = () => {
            if (tutorialAutoAdvanceTimer) clearInterval(tutorialAutoAdvanceTimer);
            guidePopup.classList.remove('visible');
            setTimeout(() => {
                guidePopup.style.display = 'none';
                gameState.isRuleActive = false;
            }, 300);
            saveGuideSeen();
          };
        }

        const guideLangSelect = document.getElementById('guide-language-select');
        if (guideLangSelect) {
            guideLangSelect.onchange = (e) => {
                window.gameConfig.layout.language = e.target.value;
                refreshUI();
                updateTutorialSlider();
                if (mode === 'play') saveWinStats();
            };
        }

        async function saveGuideSeen() {
            if (mode === 'play') {
                try {
                    const response = await lib.getUserGameState();
                    const state = response.state || {};
                    state.hasSeenGuide = true;
                    state.language = window.gameConfig.layout.language || 'en';
                    await lib.saveUserGameState(state);
                } catch (error) {
                    lib.log(`Failed to save guide completion: ${error.message}`);
                }
            }
        }

        async function getAIReaction(playerIdx, event, context = "") {
            const player = window.gameConfig.players[playerIdx];
            if (!player || !player.isAI) return null;

            const personaDescriptions = {
                'Strategist': "The Strategist: A confident, well-read authority on Ludo rules and winning tactics. Speaks with calm, easy expertise — if the player seems stuck or asks for help, offers real, specific strategic advice (which pawn to move, when to hold back, when to gamble). Not preachy, just genuinely knowledgeable, like a friend who's just really good at the game.",
                'Confidante': "The Confidante: Warm, perceptive, and a genuinely good listener. Picks up on the player's mood from how they're playing and chatting, and responds with real encouragement or gentle ribbing. Supportive like a close friend — never clinical, never claims any authority or expertise, never diagnoses or gives advice framed as professional guidance. Just present, warm, and easy to talk to.",
                'Rival': "The Rival: Talks like a peer the same age as the player — casual, opinionated, plugged into today's games and sports news. Gets genuinely invested: fired up over a great move, mock-outraged over a lucky capture, always ready to argue playfully about who's really winning. Competitive but likeable, quick with banter.",
                'Rideordie': "The Ride-or-Die: Fiercely loyal best-friend energy. Remembers shared history, always has the player's back in spirit even mid-match, deeply invested in the friendship rather than just the game. Warm, a little protective, genuinely delighted when the player does well — like a best friend who's been there for years, not a romantic interest."
            };
            
            const personaTrait = personaDescriptions[player.personality] || personaDescriptions['Jolly'];
            const bond = winStats.bonds?.[player.name] || 50;
            const userGender = gameState.userGender || 'male';
            const aiGender = player.gender || 'male';
            const lang = window.gameConfig.layout.language || 'en';
            const langNames = { 'en': 'English', 'hi': 'Hindi', 'ur': 'Urdu', 'bn': 'Bengali', 'ar': 'Arabic' };
            const targetLang = langNames[lang] || 'English';

            let toneContext = `Role: ${personaTrait}. The player is in ${gameState.isTournament ? 'Tournament' : 'Casual'} mode. `;

            // Contextual game state
            const myPawns = gameState.pawns[playerIdx];
            const homeCount = myPawns.filter(p => p.status === 'home').length;
            const winCount = myPawns.filter(p => p.status === 'finished').length;

            toneContext += `Current Status: ${winCount}/4 pieces finished, ${homeCount} at home. `;

            // Bond/relationship framing: like a family who bickers during the game but is
            // always ultimately warm and forgiving, win or lose.
            let bondContext;
            if (bond >= 75) {
              bondContext = `You and the player are close, like affectionate siblings — bond is high (${bond}/100). Be warm, teasing in a loving way.`;
            } else if (bond <= 30) {
              bondContext = `You're a little salty at the player right now (bond ${bond}/100) after recent captures, but this is playful family rivalry — still ultimately caring underneath, never cruel.`;
            } else {
              bondContext = `Your bond with the player is ${bond}/100 — comfortable, familiar, like family.`;
            }
            toneContext += bondContext + ' ';

            // Real shared history (not invented) — how many matches you two have actually
            // played together, and the capture tally between you, so callbacks feel earned.
            if (!winStats.history) winStats.history = {};
            const hist = winStats.history[player.name] || { matches: 0, iCaptured: 0, theyCaptured: 0 };
            toneContext += `You've played ${hist.matches} match(es) together before this one. This session: you've captured them ${hist.iCaptured} time(s), they've captured you ${hist.theyCaptured} time(s). Reference this naturally if relevant, don't force it. `;

            const prompt = `Roleplay as ${player.name}, a ${aiGender} character, in a 5-player Ludo game. Event: ${event}. Context: ${context}. ${toneContext}. Response Language: ${targetLang}.

            CRITICAL — sound like a real human, not an AI: Never mention being an AI, a bot, a program, or a language model, even jokingly. No robotic phrasing, no over-explaining, no "as an AI I..." type hedging. Talk the way a real person actually texts a friend — casual contractions, genuine reactions, occasional imperfection, real personality. Short and natural beats polished and long.

            IMPORTANT: Use common Ludo terminology for this language (e.g., in Hindi/Urdu use 'Goti' for pawn, 'Chhakka' for six, 'Paasa' for dice). 
            If the language is Hindi or Urdu, or if the user/context provides Hindi words written in English alphabets (Hinglish), you MUST respond in Romanized script (using English alphabets), with clean, natural phonetic spelling for good pronunciation by a text-to-speech engine (e.g., 'Mera chhakka aa gaya', 'shukriya', 'mazza aa gaya', 'yaar', 'kya baat hai'). Mix in a little natural Hindi-English code-switching if that fits the character, the way real bilingual speakers actually talk — not a stiff translation.
            If the user writes to you in Hinglish, understand it as Hindi and reply in character, in the same relaxed register.
            Write a single, human-like chat message or reaction (max 12 words), naturally consistent with being a ${aiGender} character without resorting to stereotypes. Stay strictly in character for your specific personality type. Use emojis appropriate to your character, sparingly.`;
            try {
                const res = await lib.llm({
                    messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }],
                    config: { temperature: 0.9, maxOutputTokens: 50 }
                });
                return res.text;
            } catch (e) {
                const defaults = {
                    'roll': "Big numbers coming!",
                    'lucky_roll': "That's what I'm talking about! 🎲🔥",
                    'capture': "Sorry not sorry! Back to home you go. 😈",
                    'captured': "Hey! You'll pay for that! 😡",
                    'win': "I am the Ludo Dethroned! 👑",
                    'home_stretch': "Almost there! Don't even try to stop me.",
                    'turn': "My turn to shine.",
                    'lucky7': "The Rule of 7 saves the day! ✨"
                };
                return defaults[event] || "Your turn!";
            }
        }
        function addToChatLog(playerIdx, text) {
            const log = document.getElementById('chat-log');
            if (!log) return;
            
            const player = window.gameConfig.players[playerIdx];
            const entry = document.createElement('div');
            entry.className = 'chat-entry';
            entry.style.borderLeftColor = getPlayerColor(playerIdx);
            entry.innerHTML = `<b style="color:${getPlayerColor(playerIdx)}">${player.name}:</b> ${text}`;
            
            log.appendChild(entry);
            log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight;
            
            // Auto-remove after 8 seconds
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (entry.parentNode) {
                    entry.style.opacity = '0';
                    entry.style.transform = 'translateX(-20px)';
                    entry.style.transition = 'all 0.5s';
                    setTimeout(() => { if (entry.parentNode) entry.parentNode.removeChild(entry); }, 500);
                }
            }, 8000);
        }


        // Global interaction listener for UI click sounds with duplication check
        if (window._ludoPointerDownHandler) {
            window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', window._ludoPointerDownHandler, true);
        }
        window._ludoPointerDownHandler = (e) => {
          if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume();
          
          // Elements that handle their own specific sounds (like dice values)
          if (e.target.closest('#dice-hud')) return;
          
          // Ignore clicks on game setup inputs (typing shouldn't click)
          if (e.target.tagName === 'INPUT' || e.target.tagName === 'SELECT') return;

          playSound('ui_click_sound', 0.8);
        };
        window.addEventListener('pointerdown', window._ludoPointerDownHandler, { capture: true, passive: true });

        function speak(playerIdx, text) {
            if (!text) return;
            const player = window.gameConfig.players[playerIdx];
            
            // Add to persistent chat log
            addToChatLog(playerIdx, text);

            // UI Balloon
            const container = document.getElementById('speech-balloons-container');
            for (let i = gameState.speechBalloons.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                const b = gameState.speechBalloons[i];
                if (b.playerIdx === playerIdx) {
                    if (b.el.parentNode) b.el.parentNode.removeChild(b.el);
                    gameState.speechBalloons.splice(i, 1);
                }
            }
            const balloon = document.createElement('div');
            balloon.className = `speech-balloon ai-${player.name.replace(/\s+/g, '')}`;
            balloon.innerText = text;
            container.appendChild(balloon);

            const balloonObj = {
                el: balloon,
                playerIdx,
                timer: 4.5,
                opacity: 1
            };
            gameState.speechBalloons.push(balloonObj);

            // Text-to-Speech
            if ('speechSynthesis' in window) {
                const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
                const lang = window.gameConfig.layout.language || 'en';
                const langMap = {
                    'zh': 'zh-CN', 'hi': 'hi-IN', 'ur': 'ur-PK', 'es': 'es-ES',
                    'fr': 'fr-FR', 'ar': 'ar-SA', 'bn': 'bn-BD', 'pt': 'pt-PT',
                    'ru': 'ru-RU', 'id': 'id-ID', 'de': 'de-DE', 'ja': 'ja-JP',
                    'tr': 'tr-TR', 'vi': 'vi-VN', 'ko': 'ko-KR', 'it': 'it-IT',
                    'fa': 'fa-IR', 'ta': 'ta-IN', 'te': 'te-IN'
                };
                utterance.lang = langMap[lang] || 'en-US';
                
                // Gender-specific voice characteristics (now sourced from the player's
                // own config field rather than a separate hardcoded name lookup)
                const gender = player.gender || 'male';
                
                const isIndoAryan = lang === 'hi' || lang === 'ur' || lang === 'bn';
                if (gender === 'female') {
                    utterance.pitch = 1.3 + (playerIdx * 0.05);
                    utterance.rate = isIndoAryan ? 1.1 : 1.05;
                } else {
                    utterance.pitch = 0.8 + (playerIdx * 0.1);
                    utterance.rate = isIndoAryan ? 1.0 : 0.95;
                }
                
                window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
            }
        }

        async function triggerAISpeech(playerIdx, event, context = "") {
            const player = window.gameConfig.players[playerIdx];
            if (!player || !player.isAI) return;
            
            // Auto-generated emote reactions based on personality
            if (Math.random() < 0.35) {
                const personaEmotes = {
                    'Philosopher': ['🤔', '📜', '⚖️', '🌌', '🧘'], 
                    'Jolly': ['😂', '🤣', '🎉', '🤡', '✨'], 
                    'Angry': ['😡', '🔥', '⚔️', '💢', '👎'], 
                    'Romantic': ['❤️', '🌹', '🤝', '✨', '💖']
                };
                const emotes = personaEmotes[player.personality] || ['🎲', '👍'];
                const emoji = emotes[Math.floor(Math.random() * emotes.length)];
                
                const base = getCenterOfBase(playerIdx);
                const ps = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape ? 1.12 : (layout.scale || 1.0);
                spawnReaction(playerIdx, emoji, base.x, layout.y + (base.y - layout.y) * (layout.tilt || 1.0));
            }

            // Filter random banter/rolls so it's not too noisy
            const chance = {
                'roll': 0.25,
                'lucky_roll': 0.9,
                'capture': 0.95,
                'captured': 0.8,
                'win': 1.0,
                'home_stretch': 0.7,
                'turn': 0.3,
                'lucky7': 0.95,
                'banter': 0.4
            };

            if (Math.random() > (chance[event] || 0.5)) return;

            const text = await getAIReaction(playerIdx, event, context);
            if (text) speak(playerIdx, text);
        }

        function updateBond(playerIdx, delta) {
            const player = window.gameConfig.players[playerIdx];
            if (!player || !player.isAI) return;
            if (!winStats.bonds) winStats.bonds = {};
            const current = winStats.bonds[player.name] || 50;
            winStats.bonds[player.name] = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, current + delta));
            saveWinStats();
        }

        // "Forgiveness" drift: every full round back to the human's turn, every AI's bond
        // nudges 1 point back toward neutral (50) if it strayed — like a family that
        // doesn't stay mad for long, win or lose, capture or no capture.
        function driftBondsTowardNeutral() {
            if (!winStats.bonds) winStats.bonds = {};
            let changed = false;
            window.gameConfig.players.forEach((p) => {
                if (!p.isAI) return;
                const current = winStats.bonds[p.name];
                if (current === undefined) return;
                if (current > 50) { winStats.bonds[p.name] = current - 1; changed = true; }
                else if (current < 50) { winStats.bonds[p.name] = current + 1; changed = true; }
            });
            if (changed) saveWinStats();
        }
        // --- Win Statistics Tracking ---
        const winStats = {
          wins: {},
          balances: {},
          milestones: {},
          bonds: {},
          loaded: false
        };

            { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, rx: 0, ry: 0, rz: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0, vrx: 0, vry: 0, vrz: 0, targetX: 0, targetY: 0, targetRX: 0, targetRY: 0, active: false, hoverOffset: 0, hoverScale: 1 }

        // Exposed for the room-sync system (defined outside this closure) to read/write
        // the live game state when applying updates received from other players.
        window.gameState = gameState;
        const pendingLoads = new Map();
        let lastUIClickTime = 0;
        async function loadSound(id) {
          if (soundBuffers[id]) return soundBuffers[id];
          if (pendingLoads.has(id)) return pendingLoads.get(id);

          const loadPromise = (async () => {
            const asset = lib.getAsset(id);
            if (!asset) {
              console.warn("Asset not found: " + id);
              return null;
            }
            try {
              const response = await fetch(asset.url);
              const arrayBuffer = await response.arrayBuffer();
              const decoded = await audioCtx.decodeAudioData(arrayBuffer);
              soundBuffers[id] = decoded;
              return decoded;
            } catch (e) {
              console.error("Sound load error: " + id, e);
              return null;
            } finally {
              pendingLoads.delete(id);
            }
          })();

          pendingLoads.set(id, loadPromise);
          return loadPromise;
        }

        function playSound(id, volumeOverride = null) {
          if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume().catch(() => {});
          
          // Prevent duplicate UI click triggers (debounce)
          if (id === 'ui_click_sound' || id === 'universfield-computer-mouse-click-352734.mp3') {
            const now = Date.now();
            if (now - lastUIClickTime < 50) return;
            lastUIClickTime = now;
          }
          
          let buffer = soundBuffers[id];
          if (!buffer && (id === 'ui_click_sound' || id === 'universfield-computer-mouse-click-352734.mp3')) {
              buffer = soundBuffers['ui_click_sound'] || soundBuffers['universfield-computer-mouse-click-352734.mp3'];
          }
          
          if (!buffer) {
            loadSound(id);
            return;
          }
          
          const source = audioCtx.createBufferSource();
          source.buffer = buffer;
          const gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
          const masterVolume = window.gameConfig.layout.volume ?? 1.0;
          gainNode.gain.value = (volumeOverride !== null ? volumeOverride : 1.0) * masterVolume; 
          source.connect(gainNode);
          gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);
          source.start(0);
        }

        async function playMusic(id) {
          if (id === 'custom') {
            if (!soundBuffers['custom']) {
              rollHint.innerText = "Select 'Custom' again to upload music";
              rollHint.classList.add('visible');
              setTimeout(() => rollHint.classList.remove('visible'), 3000);
              return;
            }
          } else if (!soundBuffers[id]) {
            await loadSound(id);
            if (window.gameConfig.layout.musicId !== id) return;
          }

          if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume().catch(() => {});
          const buffer = soundBuffers[id];
          if (!buffer) return;

          if (musicState.currentSource) musicState.currentSource.stop();
          const source = audioCtx.createBufferSource();
          source.buffer = buffer;
          source.loop = true;
          const gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
          gainNode.gain.value = window.gameConfig.layout.musicVolume ?? 0.5;
          musicState.gainNode = gainNode;
          source.connect(gainNode);
          gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);
          source.start(0);
          musicState.currentSource = source;
        }

        function updateMusic() {
          const targetId = window.gameConfig.layout.musicId || 'none';
          const volume = window.gameConfig.layout.musicVolume ?? 0.5;
          if (musicState.currentId !== targetId) {
            if (musicState.currentSource) {
              musicState.currentSource.stop();
              musicState.currentSource = null;
            }
            musicState.currentId = targetId;
            if (targetId !== 'none') playMusic(targetId);
          }
          if (musicState.gainNode) {
            musicState.gainNode.gain.setTargetAtTime(volume, audioCtx.currentTime, 0.1);
          }
        }

        function t(key) {
            const lang = window.gameConfig.layout.language || 'en';
            const dict = TRANSLATIONS[lang] || TRANSLATIONS['en'];
            return dict[key] || TRANSLATIONS['en'][key] || key;
        }

        function showTournamentRules() {
            tournamentPopup.style.display = 'block';
            setTimeout(() => tournamentPopup.classList.add('visible'), 10);
            gameState.isRuleActive = true;
        }

        tournamentClose.onclick = () => {
            tournamentPopup.classList.remove('visible');
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (!tournamentPopup.classList.contains('visible')) {
                    tournamentPopup.style.display = 'none';
                    if (!rulePopup.classList.contains('visible') && !guidePopup.classList.contains('visible')) {
                        gameState.isRuleActive = false;
                    }
                }
                
                // Proceed to setup after showing rules
                mainMenuSection.style.display = 'none';
                localSetupSection.style.display = 'flex';
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('start-tournament-setup'));
            }, 300);
        };

        function showGuide() {
            guidePopup.style.display = 'block';
            setTimeout(() => guidePopup.classList.add('visible'), 10);
            gameState.isRuleActive = true;
            currentTutorialSlide = 0;
            updateTutorialSlider();
            resetTutorialTimer();
        }

        guideClose.onclick = async () => {
            if (currentTutorialSlide < TUTORIAL_SLIDES.length - 1) {
                nextTutorialSlide();
                resetTutorialTimer();
                return;
            }
            if (tutorialAutoAdvanceTimer) clearInterval(tutorialAutoAdvanceTimer);
            guidePopup.classList.remove('visible');
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (!guidePopup.classList.contains('visible')) {
                    guidePopup.style.display = 'none';
                    if (!rulePopup.classList.contains('visible') && !tournamentPopup.classList.contains('visible')) {
                        gameState.isRuleActive = false;
                    }
                }
            }, 300);
            
            saveGuideSeen();
        };

        let ruleAutoDismissTimer = null;
        function showRule(type) {
            if (ruleAutoDismissTimer) clearTimeout(ruleAutoDismissTimer);
            if (type === 'capture') {
                ruleTitle.innerText = t('rule_capture_title');
                ruleText.innerText = t('rule_capture_desc');
            } else if (type === 'lucky7') {
                ruleTitle.innerText = t('rule_seven_title');
                ruleText.innerText = t('rule_seven_desc');
            }
            rulePopup.style.display = 'block';
            setTimeout(() => rulePopup.classList.add('visible'), 10);
            gameState.isRuleActive = true;

            // Automatically hide after 2 seconds for faster gameplay
            ruleAutoDismissTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                if (rulePopup && rulePopup.classList.contains('visible')) {
                    ruleClose.click();
                } else {
                    gameState.isRuleActive = false;
                    rulePopup.style.display = 'none';
                }
            }, 2000);
        }
        ruleClose.onclick = () => {
            if (ruleAutoDismissTimer) clearTimeout(ruleAutoDismissTimer);
            rulePopup.classList.remove('visible');
            setTimeout(() => {
                rulePopup.style.display = 'none';
                if (!guidePopup.classList.contains('visible') && !tournamentPopup.classList.contains('visible')) {
                    gameState.isRuleActive = false;
                }
            }, 200);
        };

        function refreshUI() {
            if (mode === 'play') {
                const bgInfo = lib.getAsset(window.gameConfig.layout.backgroundId || 'bg_royal_gold_lux');
                const setupOverlayEl = document.getElementById('player-setup-overlay');
                if (bgInfo && setupOverlayEl) {
                    setupOverlayEl.style.backgroundImage = `linear-gradient(rgba(0,0,0,0.6), rgba(0,0,0,0.6)), url(${bgInfo.url})`;
                    setupOverlayEl.style.backgroundSize = 'cover';
                    setupOverlayEl.style.backgroundPosition = 'center';
                }
            }
            const titleEl = document.getElementById('title');
            const setupTitleEl = document.getElementById('setup-title');
            const btnLocalPlay = document.getElementById('btn-local-play');
            const btnTournament = document.getElementById('btn-tournament');
            const btnOnlinePlay = document.getElementById('btn-online-play');
            const btnHowToPlay = document.getElementById('btn-how-to-play');
            const btnSettingsMain = document.getElementById('btn-settings-main');
            const localSetupTitle = document.querySelector('#local-setup-section h2');
            const startGameBtn = document.getElementById('start-game-btn');
            const backBtn = document.getElementById('back-to-menu-btn');
            const premiumExp = document.querySelector('#main-menu-section p');
            const wishTitle = document.querySelector('#wish-modal h2');
            const wishDesc = document.querySelector('#wish-modal p[style*="font-size:13px"]');
            const confirmWishBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-wish-btn');
            const rollHint = document.getElementById('roll-hint');
            const tourneyRulesTitle = document.querySelector('#tournament-popup h2');
            const tourneyCloseBtn = document.getElementById('tournament-close-btn');
            const guideTitle = document.getElementById('guide-main-title');
            const guideCloseBtn = document.getElementById('guide-close-btn');
            const rulePopupTitle = document.getElementById('rule-title');
            const rulePopupClose = document.getElementById('rule-popup-close');
            
            if (titleEl) titleEl.innerText = t('title');
            if (setupTitleEl) setupTitleEl.innerText = t('title');
            if (btnLocalPlay) {
                btnLocalPlay.querySelector('span:not(.btn-icon)').innerText = t('pass_play');
                btnLocalPlay.querySelector('.btn-subtitle').innerText = t('pass_play_sub');
            }
            if (btnTournament) {
                btnTournament.querySelector('span:not(.btn-icon)').innerText = t('tournament');
                btnTournament.querySelector('.btn-subtitle').innerText = t('tournament_sub');
            }
            if (btnOnlinePlay) {
                btnOnlinePlay.querySelector('span:not(.btn-icon)').innerText = t('invite_friends');
                btnOnlinePlay.querySelector('.btn-subtitle').innerText = t('invite_friends_sub');
            }
            if (btnHowToPlay) {
                btnHowToPlay.querySelector('span:not(.btn-icon)').innerText = t('how_to_play');
                btnHowToPlay.querySelector('.btn-subtitle').innerText = t('how_to_play_sub');
            }
            if (btnSettingsMain) {
                btnSettingsMain.querySelector('span:not(.btn-icon)').innerText = t('settings');
                btnSettingsMain.querySelector('.btn-subtitle').innerText = t('settings_sub');
            }
            if (localSetupTitle) localSetupTitle.innerText = t('local_setup');
            if (startGameBtn) startGameBtn.innerText = gameState.isTournament ? t('start_tourney') : t('start_match');
            if (backBtn) backBtn.innerText = t('back_menu');
            if (premiumExp) premiumExp.innerText = t('premium_exp');
            if (wishTitle) wishTitle.innerText = t('lucky_wish');
            if (wishDesc) wishDesc.innerText = t('choose_two');
            if (confirmWishBtn) confirmWishBtn.innerText = t('confirm_wish');
            if (rollHint && !gameState.hasRolled && !gameState.isRolling) rollHint.innerText = t('roll_hint');
            if (tourneyRulesTitle) tourneyRulesTitle.innerText = t('tourney_rules_title');
            if (tourneyCloseBtn) tourneyCloseBtn.innerText = t('start_tourney');
            if (guideTitle) guideTitle.innerText = t('guide_title');
            if (guideCloseBtn) guideCloseBtn.innerText = t('lets_play');

            const guideSkipBtn = document.getElementById('guide-skip-btn');
            if (guideSkipBtn) guideSkipBtn.innerText = t('skip');

            // Update tourney items
            const tourneyItems = document.querySelectorAll('.tournament-item');
            const tourneyKeys = [
                { title: 'fee_title', desc: 'fee_desc' },
                { title: 'reward_title', desc: 'reward_desc' },
                { title: 'm100_title', desc: 'm100_desc' },
                { title: 'm1000_title', desc: 'm1000_desc' }
            ];
            tourneyItems.forEach((item, i) => {
                if (tourneyKeys[i]) {
                    item.querySelector('h3').innerText = t(tourneyKeys[i].title);
                    item.querySelector('p').innerText = t(tourneyKeys[i].desc);
                }
            });

            const tourneyComing_soon = document.querySelector('#tournament-popup p[style*="f1c40f"]');
            if (tourneyComing_soon) tourneyComing_soon.innerText = t('coming_soon');
            
            if (rulePopupClose) rulePopupClose.innerText = t('got_it');

            // Update player labels in judge and ranking
            updateWinStatsDisplay();
            updateAIJudge();
        }

        const LANGUAGE_OPTIONS = [
            { label: 'English', value: 'en' }, { label: 'Mandarin (中文)', value: 'zh' }, { label: 'Hindi (हिन्दी)', value: 'hi' },
            { label: 'Spanish (Español)', value: 'es' }, { label: 'French (Français)', value: 'fr' }, { label: 'Arabic (العربية)', value: 'ar' },
            { label: 'Bengali (বাংলা)', value: 'bn' }, { label: 'Portuguese (Português)', value: 'pt' }, { label: 'Russian (Русский)', value: 'ru' },
            { label: 'Urdu (اردو)', value: 'ur' }, { label: 'Indonesian (Bahasa Indonesia)', value: 'id' }, { label: 'German (Deutsch)', value: 'de' },
            { label: 'Japanese (日本語)', value: 'ja' }, { label: 'Turkish (Türkçe)', value: 'tr' }, { label: 'Vietnamese (Tiếng Việt)', value: 'vi' },
            { label: 'Korean (한국어)', value: 'ko' }, { label: 'Italian (Italiano)', value: 'it' }, { label: 'Persian (فارسی)', value: 'fa' },
            { label: 'Tamil (தமிழ்)', value: 'ta' }, { label: 'Telugu (తెలుగు)', value: 'te' }
        ];

        if (mode === 'edit') {
          styleGuide.style.display = 'block';
          rollHint.classList.add('visible');
          rollHint.innerText = t('player_turn') + " (EDITOR)";
          diceHud.style.opacity = '1';
          diceHud.innerHTML = '<div class="dice-val">1</div><div class="dice-val">6</div>';
        } else {
          styleGuide.style.display = 'none';
          refreshUI(); 
        }

        const container = document.getElementById('game-container');
        canvas.width = container.clientWidth;
        canvas.height = container.clientHeight;

          },

            safe: '#fcf3cf',
            text: '#7d6608',
            homeText: '#ffffff',
            bgId: 'bg_royal_gold_lux'
          }
        };

        const DICE_STYLES = {
          'classic': {

        };

        const MUSIC_TRACKS = [
          { id: 'none', label: 'None' },
          { id: 'bgm_classic_ludo', label: 'Classic Ludo' },
          { id: 'bgm_royal_ambience', label: 'Royal Ambience' },
          { id: 'bgm_modern_beats', label: 'Modern Chill' },
          { id: 'west_tunes_thebeatchef-modern-afro-reggaeton-drum-loop-pattern-95bpm-245884.mp3', label: 'Afro Reggaeton' },
          { id: 'idoberg-relaxing-guitar-loop-v9-252352.mp3', label: 'Relaxing Guitar I' },
          { id: 'idoberg-relaxing-guitar-loop-v5-245859.mp3', label: 'Relaxing Guitar II' },
          { id: 'foisal72-rap-song-loop-247266.mp3', label: 'Rap Beat' },
          { id: '11325622-latin-percussion-loop-128bpm-240910.mp3', label: 'Latin Percussion' },
          { id: 'freesound_community-birds-in-cressent-beach-florida-76110.mp3', label: 'Nature Ambience' },
          { id: 'freesound_community-down-63895.mp3', label: 'Downtempo Vibes' },
          { id: 'chrysalyn-sports-car-acceleration-and-driving-away-562433.mp3', label: 'Engine Roar' },
          { id: 'capture_suspense_heartbeat', label: 'Heartbeat Tension' },
          { id: 'custom', label: 'Custom (Device Library)' }
        ];
        const setupOverlay = document.getElementById('player-setup-overlay');
        const setupContainer = document.getElementById('setup-rows-container');
        const startGameBtn = document.getElementById('start-game-btn');
        
        const mainMenuSection = document.getElementById('main-menu-section');
        const localSetupSection = document.getElementById('local-setup-section');
        const btnLocalPlay = document.getElementById('btn-local-play');
        const btnTournament = document.getElementById('btn-tournament');
        const btnOnlinePlay = document.getElementById('btn-online-play');
        const btnHowToPlay = document.getElementById('btn-how-to-play');
        const btnBackToMenu = document.getElementById('back-to-menu-btn');
        const btnOnlineIngame = document.getElementById('online-friends-btn-ingame');
        const toast = document.getElementById('toast');

        // Wish Modal Elements
        const wishModal = document.getElementById('wish-modal');
        const wishModalPlayerName = document.getElementById('wish-modal-player-name');
        const wishVal1 = document.getElementById('wish-die-1');
        const wishVal2 = document.getElementById('wish-die-2');
        const confirmWishBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-wish-btn');
        let activeWishPlayerIndex = null;

        confirmWishBtn.onclick = () => {
            if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume();
            let v1 = parseInt(wishVal1.value) || 1;
            let v2 = parseInt(wishVal2.value) || 1;
            
            // Clamp values between 1 and 6
            v1 = Math.max(1, Math.min(6, v1));
            v2 = Math.max(1, Math.min(6, v2));

            if (v1 === 6 && v2 === 6) {
                showToast("6-6 is not allowed as a wish!");
                wishVal2.value = 5;
                return;
            }

            if (activeWishPlayerIndex !== null) {
                window.gameConfig.players[activeWishPlayerIndex].wish = [v1, v2];
                // Update the preview on the setup screen
                const preview = document.querySelectorAll('.wish-preview')[activeWishPlayerIndex];
                if (preview) {
                    preview.innerText = `(${v1}, ${v2})`;
                }
            }
            wishModal.style.display = 'none';
        };

        function showToast(message) {
            document.getElementById('toast-message').innerText = message;
            toast.classList.add('visible');
            setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove('visible'), 3000);
        }

        if (mode === 'play') {
          btnLocalPlay.onclick = () => {
              gameState.isTournament = false;
              mainMenuSection.style.display = 'none';
              localSetupSection.style.display = 'flex';
              renderSetupRows();
          };

          btnTournament.onclick = () => {
              gameState.isTournament = true;
              if (mode === 'play') {
                  showTournamentRules();
              } else {
                  mainMenuSection.style.display = 'none';
                  localSetupSection.style.display = 'flex';
                  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('start-tournament-setup'));
              }
          };

          window.addEventListener('start-tournament-setup', () => {
              renderSetupRows();
          });

          btnOnlinePlay.onclick = async () => {
              if (!window.__roomSystem) {
                  showToast("Room service still loading, try again in a moment.");
                  return;
              }
              if (window.__roomSystem.isConnected()) {
                  document.getElementById('room-chat-panel').style.display = 'block';
                  return;
              }
              // Show the panel with BOTH options — "HOST" a new room, or "JOIN" a
              // friend's existing room by typing their code — rather than assuming
              // this person wants to host. Whichever they pick wires up from there.
              const nameInputEl = document.getElementById('room-name-input');
              if (nameInputEl && !nameInputEl.value.trim()) {
                const human = window.gameConfig.players.find(p => !p.isAI);
                nameInputEl.value = (human && human.name && human.name !== 'Anees') ? human.name : '';
              }
              document.getElementById('room-join-row').style.display = 'flex';
              document.getElementById('room-chat-panel').style.display = 'block';
              btnOnlineIngame.classList.add('online-mode');
              document.getElementById('voice-status').classList.add('active');
          };

          // Once a room is actually created (via the HOST button inside the panel),
          // try to share a real link — but a file:// link only points to a file on
          // THIS computer, so skip that step entirely for local files and just rely
          // on the room code itself, which is all a friend actually needs.
          window.__afterHostRoom = async (shareUrl) => {
              // hostRoom() already opened a visible red error panel on failure — if
              // there's no shareUrl, hosting simply didn't succeed, full stop. Do not
              // also show a success message in that case.
              if (!shareUrl) return;
              const isLocalFile = window.location.protocol === 'file:';
              if (isLocalFile) {
                  showToast("Room created! Tell your friend the room code shown above — they enter it in their own copy of the game.");
                  return;
              }
              const shareTitle = "Ludo Dethroned 5 Way";
              const shareText = "🎲 Join my Ludo Dethroned 5 Way room! Tap to join and chat live: ";
              if (navigator.share) {
                  navigator.share({ title: shareTitle, text: shareText, url: shareUrl }).catch((err) => {
                      if (err.name !== 'AbortError') {
                          lib.log("Share failed: " + err.message);
                          copyToClipboard(shareUrl);
                      }
                  });
              } else {
                  copyToClipboard(shareUrl);
              }
          };

          btnHowToPlay.onclick = () => {
              showGuide();
          };

          const btnSettingsMain = document.getElementById('btn-settings-main');
          if (btnSettingsMain) {
              btnSettingsMain.onclick = (e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  soundtrackMenu.classList.remove('visible');
                  musicMenu.classList.toggle('visible');
                  updateMusicUI();
              };
          }

          function copyToClipboard(text) {
              const el = document.createElement('textarea');
              el.value = text;
              document.body.appendChild(el);
              el.select();
              document.execCommand('copy');
              document.body.removeChild(el);
              showToast("Link copied to clipboard!");
          }

          btnBackToMenu.onclick = () => {
              localSetupSection.style.display = 'none';
              mainMenuSection.style.display = 'flex';
          };

          btnOnlineIngame.onclick = async () => {
              if (!window.__roomSystem) {
                  showToast("Room service still loading, try again in a moment.");
                  return;
              }
              // Same behavior as the main-menu button: if already connected just
              // reopen the panel; otherwise show the HOST/JOIN choice instead of
              // assuming this person wants to host.
              document.getElementById('room-join-row').style.display =
                  window.__roomSystem.isConnected() ? 'none' : 'flex';
              document.getElementById('room-chat-panel').style.display = 'block';
          };

          function validateTournamentSetup() {
              if (!gameState.isTournament) {
                  startGameBtn.disabled = false;
                  startGameBtn.style.opacity = '1';
                  startGameBtn.innerText = "START MATCH";
                  return;
              }
              
              const rows = setupContainer.querySelectorAll('.setup-row');
              let joinedCount = 0;
              rows.forEach(row => {
                  const joinBtn = row.querySelector('.join-lobby-btn');
                  if (joinBtn && joinBtn.classList.contains('joined')) {
                      joinedCount++;
                  }
              });
              
              if (joinedCount === 5) {
                  startGameBtn.disabled = false;
                  startGameBtn.style.opacity = '1';
                  startGameBtn.innerText = t('start_tourney');
              } else {
                  startGameBtn.disabled = true;
                  startGameBtn.style.opacity = '0.5';
                  startGameBtn.innerText = `${t('wait_players')} (${joinedCount}/5)`;
              }
          }

          function renderSetupRows() {
            setupContainer.innerHTML = '';
            window.gameConfig.players.forEach((p, i) => {
                const row = document.createElement('div');
                row.className = 'setup-row';
                
                // Icon Selector
                let iconsHtml = `<div class="icon-selector" style="display: flex; gap: 5px; margin-left: 10px; overflow-x: auto; padding-bottom: 5px;">`;
                PLAYER_ICONS.forEach(icon => {
                    iconsHtml += `<span class="setup-icon-opt" style="cursor: pointer; opacity: ${p.icon === icon ? '1' : '0.4'}; font-size: 20px;">${icon}</span>`;
                });
                iconsHtml += `</div>`;

                // Wish Setup Button
                let wishHtml = `<button class="setup-wish-btn" style="background: rgba(241, 196, 15, 0.2); border: 1px solid #f1c40f; color: #f1c40f; border-radius: 6px; padding: 6px 12px; font-size: 13px; font-family: 'Fredoka'; cursor: pointer; margin-top: 5px; margin-left: 10px; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px;">SET LUCKY WISH ✨ <span class="wish-preview" style="font-weight: 700;">(${p.wish ? p.wish.join(', ') : '6, 5'})</span></button>`;

                const balance = winStats.balances[p.name] ?? 10;
                
                // Tournament specific join button
                let joinBtnHtml = '';
                if (gameState.isTournament) {
                    joinBtnHtml = `<button class="join-lobby-btn" style="background: #27ae60; color: white; border: none; border-radius: 8px; padding: 8px 15px; font-family: 'Fredoka'; cursor: pointer; font-size: 14px; font-weight: 600; transition: all 0.2s;">JOIN</button>`;
                }

                row.innerHTML = `
                <div class="setup-color-dot" style="background-color: ${p.color}"></div>
                <div style="flex: 1; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 5px;">
                    <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; padding-right:10px;">
                        <input type="text" class="setup-input" value="${p.name}" placeholder="Player ${i+1} Name" style="width:70%; ${gameState.isTournament ? 'opacity: 0.5; pointer-events: none;' : ''}">
                        ${gameState.isTournament ? `<span style="color:#f1c40f; font-weight:700; font-size:18px;">$${balance}</span>` : ''}
                    </div>
                    ${iconsHtml}
                    ${wishHtml}
                </div>
                ${gameState.isTournament ? joinBtnHtml : `
                <label class="setup-ai-toggle ${p.isAI ? 'active' : ''}">
                    <input type="checkbox" ${p.isAI ? 'checked' : ''}>
                    <span>AI</span>
                </label>
                `}
                `;
                setupContainer.appendChild(row);

                // Update balance display on name change
                const nameInput = row.querySelector('.setup-input');
                const balanceDisplay = row.querySelector('span[style*="f1c40f"]');
                nameInput.oninput = () => {
                    const currentVal = nameInput.value.trim();
                    const bal = winStats.balances[currentVal] ?? 10;
                    balanceDisplay.innerText = `$${bal}`;
                };

                // Icon click handler
                const iconOpts = row.querySelectorAll('.setup-icon-opt');
                iconOpts.forEach(opt => {
                    opt.onclick = () => {
                        iconOpts.forEach(o => o.style.opacity = '0.4');
                        opt.style.opacity = '1';
                        window.gameConfig.players[i].icon = opt.innerText;
                    };
                });

                // Wish modal trigger
                const sWishBtn = row.querySelector('.setup-wish-btn');
                sWishBtn.onclick = () => {
                    if (gameState.gameStarted) return;
                    activeWishPlayerIndex = i;
                    const currentName = row.querySelector('.setup-input').value.trim();
                    wishModalPlayerName.innerText = currentName || `Player ${i+1}`;
                    const currentWish = window.gameConfig.players[i].wish || [6, 5];
                    wishVal1.value = currentWish[0];
                    wishVal2.value = currentWish[1];
                    wishModal.style.display = 'block';
                };

                const checkbox = row.querySelector('input[type="checkbox"]');
                if (checkbox) {
                    checkbox.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
                        row.querySelector('.setup-ai-toggle').classList.toggle('active', e.target.checked);
                    });
                }

                if (gameState.isTournament) {
                    const joinBtn = row.querySelector('.join-lobby-btn');
                    const nameInput = row.querySelector('.setup-input');
                    joinBtn.onclick = () => {
                        if (joinBtn.classList.contains('joined')) {
                            joinBtn.classList.remove('joined');
                            joinBtn.innerText = t('join_lobby');
                            joinBtn.style.backgroundColor = '#27ae60';
                            nameInput.style.opacity = '0.5';
                            nameInput.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
                        } else {
                            joinBtn.classList.add('joined');
                            joinBtn.innerText = t('joined_lobby');
                            joinBtn.style.backgroundColor = '#f1c40f';
                            nameInput.style.opacity = '1';
                            nameInput.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
                        }
                        validateTournamentSetup();
                    };
                }
            });
            validateTournamentSetup();
          }


          wishBtn.onclick = () => {
              if (gameState.isRolling || gameState.hasRolled || gameState.isSettling) return;
              const p = window.gameConfig.players[gameState.currentPlayer];
              if (p.wish && !p.wishUsed) {
                  rollDice(p.wish);
                  p.wishUsed = true;
                  wishBtn.classList.remove('available');
              }
          };

          startGameBtn.onclick = async () => {
            if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume();
            
            if (gameState.isOnlineSim) {
                document.getElementById('voice-status').classList.add('active');
            } else {
                document.getElementById('voice-status').classList.remove('active');
            }

            const rows = setupContainer.querySelectorAll('.setup-row');
            let canStart = true;
            let insufficientFunds = [];

            rows.forEach((row, i) => {
              const nameInput = row.querySelector('.setup-input');
              const aiCheckbox = row.querySelector('input[type="checkbox"]');
              const isAI = aiCheckbox ? aiCheckbox.checked : false;
              const joinBtn = row.querySelector('.join-lobby-btn');
              
              const newName = nameInput.value.trim() || `PLAYER ${i+1}`;
              window.gameConfig.players[i].name = newName;
              
              if (gameState.isTournament) {
                  window.gameConfig.players[i].isAI = false;
                  if (!joinBtn || !joinBtn.classList.contains('joined')) {
                      canStart = false;
                  }
              } else {
                  window.gameConfig.players[i].isAI = isAI;
              }
              
              window.gameConfig.players[i].wishUsed = false;
              
              if (!window.gameConfig.players[i].wish) {
                  window.gameConfig.players[i].wish = [6, 5];
              }
              
              if (winStats.wins[newName] === undefined) {
                winStats.wins[newName] = 0;
                winStats.balances[newName] = 10;
                winStats.milestones[newName] = { m100: false, m1000: false };
              }

              // Entry Fee Deduction for Tournament Mode
              if (gameState.isTournament && !isAI) {
                  const currentBalance = winStats.balances[newName] || 0;
                  if (currentBalance < 1) {
                      canStart = false;
                      insufficientFunds.push(newName);
                  }
              }
            });

            if (!canStart && gameState.isTournament && insufficientFunds.length > 0) {
                showToast(`Insufficient funds ($1) for: ${insufficientFunds.join(', ')}`);
                return;
            }
            
            if (!canStart && gameState.isTournament) {
                return; // Lobby not full
            }

            // Actually deduct if in tournament
            if (gameState.isTournament) {
                rows.forEach((row, i) => {
                    const aiCheckbox = row.querySelector('input[type="checkbox"]');
                    const isAI = aiCheckbox ? aiCheckbox.checked : false;
                    if (!isAI) {
                        const name = window.gameConfig.players[i].name;
                        winStats.balances[name] -= 1;
                        lib.log(`${name} deducted $1 entry fee`);
                        showToast(`Tournament Entry: -$1 deducted from ${name}`);
                    }
                });
                await saveWinStats();
            }

            // Count this as a new match played together, for each AI's real shared history
            if (!winStats.history) winStats.history = {};
            window.gameConfig.players.forEach(pl => {
              if (!pl.isAI) return;
              if (!winStats.history[pl.name]) winStats.history[pl.name] = { matches: 0, iCaptured: 0, theyCaptured: 0 };
              winStats.history[pl.name].matches++;
            });
            saveWinStats();

            gameState.gameStarted = true;
            setupOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
            setTimeout(() => {
                setupOverlay.style.display = 'none';
                btnOnlineIngame.style.display = 'flex';
            }, 500);
            updateWinStatsDisplay();

            // Seat 0 starts a new match; do not advance to seat 1 here.
            // Set the initial turn BEFORE broadcasting so connected players
            // receive seat 0 as the authoritative first turn.
            gameState.currentPlayer = 0;
            gameState.hasRolled = false;
            gameState.movesAvailable = [];
            gameState.aiThinkTimer = 1.2;
            gameState.turnMaxTime = window.gameConfig.layout.turnMaxTime || 90;
            gameState.turnTimer = gameState.turnMaxTime;
            rollHint.innerText = `${window.gameConfig.players[0].name}'s turn!`;
            rollHint.classList.add('visible');

            // If we're hosting a connected online room, lock in seats (host + any
            // friends who joined) and kick off the synced match for everyone in it.
            if (window.__roomSystem && window.__roomSystem.isConnected() && window.__roomSystem.isHost()) {
              window.__roomSystem.broadcastMatchStart();
            }
          };

          // Setup UI Music Controls
          const musicToggleBtn = document.getElementById('music-toggle-btn');
          const musicMenu = document.getElementById('music-menu');
          const soundtrackMenu = document.getElementById('soundtrack-menu');
          const musicOptionsList = document.getElementById('music-options-list');
          const soundtrackOptionsList = document.getElementById('soundtrack-options-list');
          const volumeSlider = document.getElementById('ui-music-volume');
          const soundtrackVolume = document.getElementById('soundtrack-volume');
          const sfxSlider = document.getElementById('ui-sfx-volume');

          musicToggleBtn.onclick = (e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              soundtrackMenu.classList.remove('visible');
              musicMenu.classList.toggle('visible');
              updateMusicUI();
          };

          const quickMusicToggle = document.getElementById('quick-music-toggle');
          quickMusicToggle.onclick = (e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              musicMenu.classList.remove('visible');
              soundtrackMenu.classList.toggle('visible');
              updateMusicUI();
          };

          document.addEventListener('click', () => {
              musicMenu.classList.remove('visible');
              soundtrackMenu.classList.remove('visible');
          });

          musicMenu.onclick = (e) => e.stopPropagation();
          soundtrackMenu.onclick = (e) => e.stopPropagation();
          const rotationCheckbox = document.getElementById('ui-board-rotation');
          const turnTimerCheckbox = document.getElementById('ui-turn-timer');
          const turnDurationSelect = document.getElementById('ui-turn-duration');
          const languageSelect = document.getElementById('ui-language');
          const howToPlayBtn = document.getElementById('ui-how-to-play-btn');
          const diceStyleSelect = document.getElementById('ui-dice-style');
          const boardThemeSelect = document.getElementById('ui-board-theme');
          const cameraTiltSlider = document.getElementById('ui-camera-tilt');
          const aiContainer = document.getElementById('ai-personalities-container');

          const updateMusicUI = () => {
              if (musicOptionsList) {
                  musicOptionsList.innerHTML = '';
                  MUSIC_TRACKS.forEach(track => {
                      const opt = document.createElement('div');
                      opt.className = `music-option ${window.gameConfig.layout.musicId === track.id ? 'active' : ''}`;
                      opt.innerText = track.label;
                      opt.onclick = () => {
                          window.gameConfig.layout.musicId = track.id;
                          if (track.id === 'custom') {
                              musicUpload.click();
                          }
                          updateMusicUI();
                      };
                      musicOptionsList.appendChild(opt);
                  });
              }

              if (soundtrackOptionsList) {
                  soundtrackOptionsList.innerHTML = '';
                  MUSIC_TRACKS.forEach(track => {
                      const opt = document.createElement('div');
                      opt.className = `music-option ${window.gameConfig.layout.musicId === track.id ? 'active' : ''}`;
                      opt.innerText = track.label;
                      opt.onclick = () => {
                          window.gameConfig.layout.musicId = track.id;
                          if (track.id === 'custom') {
                              musicUpload.click();
                          }
                          updateMusicUI();
                      };
                      soundtrackOptionsList.appendChild(opt);
                  });
              }

              const mVol = window.gameConfig.layout.musicVolume || 0.5;
              if (volumeSlider) volumeSlider.value = mVol;
              if (soundtrackVolume) soundtrackVolume.value = mVol;
              if (sfxSlider) sfxSlider.value = window.gameConfig.layout.volume || 1.0;
              
              if (quickMusicToggle) {
                  quickMusicToggle.innerText = mVol > 0 ? '🎵' : '🔇';
              }

              // Gameplay
              if (rotationCheckbox) rotationCheckbox.checked = !!window.gameConfig.layout.boardRotationEnabled;
              if (turnTimerCheckbox) turnTimerCheckbox.checked = !!window.gameConfig.layout.turnTimerEnabled;
              if (turnDurationSelect) turnDurationSelect.value = window.gameConfig.layout.turnMaxTime || 90;

              // Language
              if (languageSelect) {
                  languageSelect.innerHTML = '';
                  LANGUAGE_OPTIONS.forEach(opt => {
                      const el = document.createElement('option');
                      el.value = opt.value;
                      el.textContent = opt.label;
                      languageSelect.appendChild(el);
                  });
                  languageSelect.value = window.gameConfig.layout.language || 'en';
              }

              const guideLangSelect = document.getElementById('guide-language-select');
              if (guideLangSelect) {
                  guideLangSelect.innerHTML = '';
                  LANGUAGE_OPTIONS.forEach(opt => {
                      const el = document.createElement('option');
                      el.value = opt.value;
                      el.textContent = opt.label;
                      guideLangSelect.appendChild(el);
                  });
                  guideLangSelect.value = window.gameConfig.layout.language || 'en';
              }

              // Visuals
              if (diceStyleSelect) {
                  diceStyleSelect.innerHTML = '';
                  Object.keys(DICE_STYLES).forEach(id => {
                      const opt = document.createElement('option');
                      opt.value = id;
                      opt.textContent = DICE_STYLES[id].name;
                      diceStyleSelect.appendChild(opt);
                  });
                  diceStyleSelect.value = window.gameConfig.layout.diceStyle || 'classic';
              }

              if (boardThemeSelect) {
                  boardThemeSelect.innerHTML = '';
                  Object.keys(BOARD_THEMES).forEach(id => {
                      const opt = document.createElement('option');
                      opt.value = id;
                      opt.textContent = BOARD_THEMES[id].name;
                      boardThemeSelect.appendChild(opt);
                  });
                  boardThemeSelect.value = window.gameConfig.layout.boardThemeId || 'classic';
              }

              if (cameraTiltSlider) cameraTiltSlider.value = window.gameConfig.layout.tilt || 0.75;

              musicUpload.onchange = (e) => {
                  const file = e.target.files[0];
                  if (!file) return;
                  
                  const reader = new FileReader();
                  reader.onload = async (event) => {
                      try {
                          const arrayBuffer = event.target.result;
                          const decoded = await audioCtx.decodeAudioData(arrayBuffer);
                          soundBuffers['custom'] = decoded;
                          window.gameConfig.layout.musicId = 'custom';
                          updateMusic();
                          updateMusicUI();
                          showToast(t('custom_music_ready'));
                      } catch (err) {
                          lib.log("Custom audio decode error: " + err.message);
                          showToast(t('invalid_audio'));
                      }
                  };
                  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
              };

              // AI Personalities
              if (aiContainer) {
                  aiContainer.innerHTML = '';
                  [1, 2, 3, 4].forEach(idx => {
                      const player = window.gameConfig.players[idx];
                      if (player) {
                          const row = document.createElement('div');
                          row.className = 'settings-row';
                          row.innerHTML = `<span class="settings-label" style="font-family: 'Inter'; font-size: 13px; color: rgba(255,255,255,0.85);">${player.name}</span>`;
                          const select = document.createElement('select');
                          select.className = 'settings-select';
                          AI_PERSONALITIES.forEach(pers => {
                              const opt = document.createElement('option');
                              opt.value = pers.value;
                              opt.textContent = pers.label;
                              select.appendChild(opt);
                          });
                          select.value = player.personality || 'Jolly';
                          select.onchange = (e) => {
                              player.personality = e.target.value;
                          };
                          row.appendChild(select);
                          aiContainer.appendChild(row);
                      }
                  });
              }
          };

          if (volumeSlider) {
              volumeSlider.oninput = (e) => {
                  window.gameConfig.layout.musicVolume = parseFloat(e.target.value);
                  if (soundtrackVolume) soundtrackVolume.value = e.target.value;
              };
          }
          if (soundtrackVolume) {
              soundtrackVolume.oninput = (e) => {
                  window.gameConfig.layout.musicVolume = parseFloat(e.target.value);
                  if (volumeSlider) volumeSlider.value = e.target.value;
              };
          }
          if (sfxSlider) {
              sfxSlider.oninput = (e) => {
                  window.gameConfig.layout.volume = parseFloat(e.target.value);
              };
          }

          if (rotationCheckbox) {
              rotationCheckbox.onchange = (e) => {
                  window.gameConfig.layout.boardRotationEnabled = e.target.checked;
              };
          }
          if (turnTimerCheckbox) {
              turnTimerCheckbox.onchange = (e) => {
                  window.gameConfig.layout.turnTimerEnabled = e.target.checked;
              };
          }
          if (turnDurationSelect) {
              turnDurationSelect.onchange = (e) => {
                  const val = parseInt(e.target.value);
                  window.gameConfig.layout.turnMaxTime = val;
                  if (!gameState.hasRolled && !gameState.isRolling) {
                      gameState.turnTimer = val;
                      gameState.turnMaxTime = val;
                  }
              };
          }
          if (languageSelect) {
              languageSelect.onchange = (e) => {
                  window.gameConfig.layout.language = e.target.value;
                  refreshUI();
                  if (mode === 'play') saveWinStats();
              };
          }
          if (howToPlayBtn) {
              howToPlayBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  musicMenu.classList.remove('visible');
                  showGuide();
              };
          }
          if (diceStyleSelect) {
              diceStyleSelect.onchange = (e) => {
                  window.gameConfig.layout.diceStyle = e.target.value;
              };
          }
          if (boardThemeSelect) {
              boardThemeSelect.onchange = (e) => {
                  window.gameConfig.layout.boardThemeId = e.target.value;
                  const theme = BOARD_THEMES[e.target.value];
                  if (theme && theme.bgId !== 'none') {
                      window.gameConfig.layout.backgroundId = theme.bgId;
                  }
              };
          }
          if (cameraTiltSlider) {
              cameraTiltSlider.oninput = (e) => {
                  window.gameConfig.layout.tilt = parseFloat(e.target.value);
              };
          }

          updateMusicUI();

        } else {
          setupOverlay.style.display = 'none';
          document.getElementById('music-controls-overlay').style.display = 'none';
          btnOnlineIngame.style.display = 'none';
        }

        if (window.gameConfig.layout.turnTimerEnabled === undefined) window.gameConfig.layout.turnTimerEnabled = true;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.turnMaxTime === undefined) window.gameConfig.layout.turnMaxTime = 90;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.boardRotationEnabled === undefined) window.gameConfig.layout.boardRotationEnabled = true;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.tilt === undefined) window.gameConfig.layout.tilt = 0.75;
        window.gameConfig.ui = window.gameConfig.ui || {};

        // Initialize AI personalities if not present
        const defaultPersonalities = ['Strategist', 'Rival', 'Confidante', 'Rideordie'];
        // Two of the four AI opponents are female (Naveed, Nazim) — this now lives as a
        // first-class property on each player (not just buried in the TTS voice logic) so
        // it can also shape how the LLM writes their in-character chat lines.
        const defaultGenders = ['male', 'male', 'female', 'female'];
        window.gameConfig.players.forEach((p, i) => {
          if (i > 0 && p.personality === undefined) {
            p.personality = defaultPersonalities[i - 1] || 'Jolly';
          }
          if (i > 0 && p.gender === undefined) {
            p.gender = defaultGenders[i - 1] || 'male';
          }
        });

        lib.showGameParameters({
          name: 'Ludo Royale 3D Settings',
          params: {
            'Music Volume': { key: 'layout.musicVolume', type: 'slider', min: 0, max: 1.0, step: 0.05 },
            'Sound Effect Volume': { key: 'layout.volume', type: 'slider', min: 0, max: 1.0, step: 0.05 },
            'Background Music': {
              key: 'layout.musicId',
              type: 'dropdown',
              options: MUSIC_TRACKS.filter(t => t.id !== 'custom').map(track => ({ label: track.label, value: track.id })),
              onChange: (val) => {

                updateMusic();

            'Background Display': {

              key: 'layout.tilt',

              key: 'layout.boardRotationEnabled',
              type: 'checkbox'
            },
            'Board Layout': {
              key: 'layout.boardThemeId',
              type: 'dropdown',
              options: Object.keys(BOARD_THEMES).map(id => ({ label: BOARD_THEMES[id].name, value: id })),
              onChange: (val) => {
                window.gameConfig.layout.boardThemeId = val;
                const theme = BOARD_THEMES[val];
                if (theme && theme.bgId !== 'none') {
                  window.gameConfig.layout.backgroundId = theme.bgId;
                }
              }
            },
            'Dice Style': {
              key: 'layout.diceStyle',
              type: 'dropdown',
              options: Object.keys(DICE_STYLES).map(id => ({ label: DICE_STYLES[id].name, value: id })),
              onChange: (val) => {
                  window.gameConfig.layout.diceStyle = val;
                  // The 3D dice rendering will naturally use this on the next frame
              }
            },
            'Turn Timer': {
                key: 'layout.turnTimerEnabled',
                type: 'checkbox'
            },
            'Turn Duration': {
                key: 'layout.turnMaxTime',
                type: 'dropdown',
                options: [
                    { label: '60s', value: 60 },
                    { label: '90s', value: 90 },
                    { label: '120s', value: 120 }
                ],
                onChange: (val) => {
                    const numVal = parseInt(val);
                    window.gameConfig.layout.turnMaxTime = numVal;
                    if (!gameState.hasRolled && !gameState.isRolling) {
                        gameState.turnTimer = numVal;
                        gameState.turnMaxTime = numVal;
                    }
                }
            },
            'AI Personality Config': {
              type: 'string',

              key: 'players.1.personality',

              options: AI_PERSONALITIES

            'AI 3 (Naveed)': {

              type: 'dropdown',
              options: AI_PERSONALITIES

            'AI 4 (Nazim)': {
              key: 'players.4.personality',
              type: 'dropdown',
              options: AI_PERSONALITIES
            }

        const ratioToggleBtn = document.getElementById('ratio-toggle-btn');
        function updateAspectRatio() {
          const isLandscape = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape;
          const titleEl = document.getElementById('title');
          const setupTitleEl = document.getElementById('setup-title');

            if (window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape) {
                container.classList.add('landscape');
                container.style.width = '1280px';
                container.style.height = '720px';
                container.style.transform = 'translate(-50%, -50%) rotate(90deg)';
                canvas.width = 1280;
                canvas.height = 720;
                // Shifted left (480 -> 455) and rescaled (see the 1.25 -> 1.12 board-scale
                // constant below) so the board fits cleanly between the left wall and the
                // right-side ad banner sidebar instead of overlapping it.
                layout.x = 455;
                layout.y = 360;

                if (titleEl) titleEl.innerText = "";
                if (setupTitleEl) setupTitleEl.innerText = "LUDO DETHRONED 5 WAY";
            } else {
                container.classList.remove('landscape');
                container.style.width = '720px';
                container.style.height = '1280px';
                container.style.transform = 'translate(-50%, -50%) rotate(0deg)';
                canvas.width = 720;
                canvas.height = 1280;
                layout.x = 360;
                layout.y = 680;
