                if (titleEl) titleEl.innerText = "LUDO DETHRONED 5 WAY";
                if (setupTitleEl) setupTitleEl.innerText = "LUDO DETHRONED 5 WAY";
            }
        }

        if (ratioToggleBtn) {
            ratioToggleBtn.onclick = (e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
                document.documentElement.requestFullscreen().catch(() => {});
              } else {
                document.exitFullscreen().catch(() => {});
              }
            };
        }
        document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', () => {
          window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize'));
        });

        window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape = true;

        updateAspectRatio();
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.rotationDelay === undefined)
          window.gameConfig.layout.rotationDelay = 60;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.pawnSpeed === undefined)
          window.gameConfig.layout.pawnSpeed = 0.05;

        if (window.gameConfig.layout.diceScale === undefined)
          window.gameConfig.layout.diceScale = 1.0;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.volume === undefined)
          window.gameConfig.layout.volume = 1.0;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.backgroundId === undefined)
          window.gameConfig.layout.backgroundId = 'none';
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.boardThemeId === undefined)
          window.gameConfig.layout.boardThemeId = 'classic';
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.tournamentBoardThemeId === undefined)
          window.gameConfig.layout.tournamentBoardThemeId = 'led';

        if (window.gameConfig.layout.musicVolume === undefined)
          window.gameConfig.layout.musicVolume = 0.5;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.musicId === undefined)
          window.gameConfig.layout.musicId = 'none';
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.diceStyle === undefined)
          window.gameConfig.layout.diceStyle = 'classic';

        if (window.gameConfig.layout.rollHintX === undefined) window.gameConfig.layout.rollHintX = 360;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.rollHintY === undefined) window.gameConfig.layout.rollHintY = 1060;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudX === undefined) window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudX = 360;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudY === undefined) window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudY = 1160;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerX === undefined) window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerX = 360;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerY === undefined) window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerY = 900;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerWidth === undefined) window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerWidth = 600;
        if (window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerHeight === undefined) window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerHeight = 150;

        if (window.gameConfig.layout.showSquareNumbers === undefined) window.gameConfig.layout.showSquareNumbers = true;

        window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
          // Re-derive canvas resolution / board anchor from the current mode instead of
          // blindly using clientWidth/clientHeight, which fought with updateAspectRatio()
          // (e.g. on fullscreen toggle) and reset the board's landscape positioning.
          updateAspectRatio();
        });

        gameState.rotationOffset = layout.rotation || 0;

        function updateDiceHud() {
          if (!diceHud) return;
          if (gameState.isRolling || !gameState.hasRolled || gameState.movesAvailable.length === 0) {
            if (!gameState.isRolling) {
                diceHud.style.opacity = '0';
                diceHud.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
            }
            return;
          }
          
          diceHud.style.opacity = '1';
          diceHud.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
          
          const ds = layout.diceScale || 1.0;
          const spacing = 60 * ds;
          const hudWidth = window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudSize || 80;
          // Calculate gap to match canvas dice spacing (spacing * 2 is center-to-center distance)
          const targetGap = (spacing * 2) - hudWidth;
          diceHud.style.gap = targetGap + 'px';

          const playerColor = getPlayerColor(gameState.currentPlayer);
          diceHud.innerHTML = '';
          
          // Check if this is the bonus 7-roll scenario (either natural 6-6 or Rule of 7 granted 6-6)
          // The HUD displays gameState.movesAvailable which includes the bonus 6-6 from Luck 7
          const isBonusRoll = gameState.movesAvailable.length === 2 && 
                              gameState.movesAvailable[0] === 6 && 
                              gameState.movesAvailable[1] === 6;
          
          gameState.movesAvailable.forEach((v, i) => {
              const isSelected = gameState.movesAvailable.length > 1 && gameState.selectedMoveIndex === i;
              const dv = document.createElement('div');
              dv.className = 'dice-val';
              dv.innerText = v;
              
              // Bonus roll styling: golden glow
              if (isBonusRoll) {
                  dv.style.backgroundColor = '#FFD700';
                  dv.style.color = '#1a1a1a';
                  dv.style.borderColor = '#FFA500';
                  dv.style.boxShadow = '0 0 30px #FFD700, 0 8px 25px rgba(0,0,0,0.8)';
                  dv.style.fontWeight = '900';
              } else {
                  dv.style.borderColor = isSelected ? '#ffffff' : playerColor;
                  dv.style.boxShadow = isSelected ? '0 0 20px #ffffff' : '0 8px 25px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)';
              }
              
              dv.style.transform = isSelected ? 'scale(1.1)' : 'scale(1)';
              dv.style.cursor = 'pointer';
              dv.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
              
          dv.onpointerdown = (e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume();
              playSound('ui_click_sound', 0.8);
              gameState.selectedMoveIndex = i;
              updateDiceHud();
              rollHint.innerText = t('pawn_select');
          };
              diceHud.appendChild(dv);
          });
        }

        // --- Pawn Initialization ---
        function initPawns() {
          gameState.pawns = [];
          for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < 5; pIdx++) {
            const playerPawns = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
              playerPawns.push({
                playerIdx: pIdx,
                pawnIdx: i,
                status: 'home',
                pos: -1,
                displayPos: -1,
                renderX: 0,
                renderY: 0,
              });
            }
            gameState.pawns.push(playerPawns);
          }
        }
        initPawns();

        // Initialize defaults for all players in winStats
        const allPossibleNames = [...PLAYER_NAMES, ...window.gameConfig.players.map(p => p.name)];
        allPossibleNames.forEach(name => {
          if (name && winStats.wins[name] === undefined) {
            winStats.wins[name] = 0;
            winStats.balances[name] = 10; 
            winStats.milestones[name] = { m100: false, m1000: false };
          }
        });

        // Load saved game state
        async function loadWinStats() {
          try {
            const response = await lib.getUserGameState();
            if (response.state) {
              // Load language
              if (response.state.language) {
                window.gameConfig.layout.language = response.state.language;
                refreshUI();
              }
              // Load win statistics
              if (response.state.winStats) {
                Object.keys(response.state.winStats).forEach(name => {
                  winStats.wins[name] = response.state.winStats[name] || 0;
                });
              }
              // Load balances
              if (response.state.balances) {
                Object.keys(response.state.balances).forEach(name => {
                  winStats.balances[name] = response.state.balances[name] ?? 10;
                });
              }
              // Load milestones
              if (response.state.milestones) {
                Object.keys(response.state.milestones).forEach(name => {
                  winStats.milestones[name] = response.state.milestones[name] || { m100: false, m1000: false };
                });
              }
              // Load bonds
              if (response.state.bonds) {
                winStats.bonds = response.state.bonds;
              }
              // Load shared-history (matches played together, capture tallies)
              if (response.state.history) {
                winStats.history = response.state.history;
              }
              // Load user gender
              if (response.state.userGender) {
                window.setGender(response.state.userGender);
              }
              
              // Check if user has seen the guide
              if (!response.state.hasSeenGuide) {
                // New user - show the guide after a brief delay
                setTimeout(() => {
                  showGuide();
                }, 500);
                lib.log('First-time user detected, showing guide');
              } else {
                lib.log('Returning user, guide already seen');
              }
            } else {
              // No saved state at all - new user
              lib.log('No saved state found, starting fresh');
              setTimeout(() => {
                showGuide();
              }, 500);
            }
            winStats.loaded = true;
            updateWinStatsDisplay();
          } catch (error) {
            lib.log(`Failed to load game state: ${error.message}`);
            winStats.loaded = true;
            updateWinStatsDisplay();
            // Still show guide for new users even if load failed
            setTimeout(() => {
              showGuide();
            }, 500);
          }
        }

        async function updatePlayerWin(playerName) {
          // Track any name that wins
          winStats.wins[playerName] = (winStats.wins[playerName] || 0) + 1;
          
          // Reward fulfillment logic
          if (gameState.isTournament) {
            // Normal Win Reward: +$2
            winStats.balances[playerName] = (winStats.balances[playerName] || 0) + 2;
            lib.log(`${playerName} awarded $2 for tournament win`);
            showToast(`${playerName} awarded $2 for the win! 💰`);

            // Milestone Bonuses
            const wins = winStats.wins[playerName];
            const ms = winStats.milestones[playerName] || { m100: false, m1000: false };
            
            if (wins >= 100 && !ms.m100) {
              winStats.balances[playerName] += 100;
              ms.m100 = true;
              showToast(`${playerName} reached 100 wins! Bonus $100 credited! 🏆`);
              lib.log(`${playerName} bonus $100 credited for 100 wins`);
            }
            
            if (wins >= 1000 && !ms.m1000) {
              winStats.balances[playerName] += 1000;
              ms.m1000 = true;
              showToast(`${playerName} reached 1,000 wins! Bonus $1,000 credited! 👑`);
              lib.log(`${playerName} bonus $1,000 credited for 1,000 wins`);
            }
            winStats.milestones[playerName] = ms;
          }
          
          lib.log(`${playerName} wins updated: ${winStats.wins[playerName]}`);
          
          updateWinStatsDisplay();
          await saveWinStats();
        }

        // Save win statistics and balances
        async function saveWinStats() {
          try {
            const response = await lib.getUserGameState();
            const state = response.state || {};
            
            // Update stats while preserving other saved data
            state.winStats = winStats.wins;
            state.balances = winStats.balances;
            state.milestones = winStats.milestones;
            state.bonds = winStats.bonds;
            state.history = winStats.history;
            state.userGender = gameState.userGender;
            state.language = window.gameConfig.layout.language || 'en';
            
            await lib.saveUserGameState(state);
            lib.log('Game state saved successfully');
          } catch (error) {
            lib.log(`Failed to save game state: ${error.message}`);
          }
        }

        // Reset all win statistics and balances
        async function resetWinStats() {
          try {
            const response = await lib.getUserGameState();
            const state = response.state || {};
            
            // Reset everything
            winStats.wins = {};
            winStats.balances = {};
            winStats.milestones = {};
            PLAYER_NAMES.forEach(name => {
              winStats.wins[name] = 0;
              winStats.balances[name] = 10;
              winStats.milestones[name] = { m100: false, m1000: false };
            });
            state.winStats = winStats.wins;
            state.balances = winStats.balances;
            state.milestones = winStats.milestones;
            
            updateWinStatsDisplay();
            await lib.saveUserGameState(state);
            lib.log('Game state reset');
          } catch (error) {
            lib.log(`Failed to reset game state: ${error.message}`);
          }
        }

        // Update the win statistics display
        function updateWinStatsDisplay() {
          // Get all names that have ever won or are in current game
          const allNames = new Set([
            ...window.gameConfig.players.map(p => p.name),
            ...Object.keys(winStats.wins)
          ]);

        const sorted = Array.from(allNames).map(name => {
            const pIdx = window.gameConfig.players.findIndex(p => p.name === name);
            const color = pIdx !== -1 ? getPlayerColor(pIdx) : '#444';
            return {
              name,
              wins: winStats.wins[name] || 0,
              balance: winStats.balances[name] ?? 10,
              color,
              isCurrent: pIdx !== -1
            };
          }).sort((a, b) => {
            if (b.wins !== a.wins) return b.wins - a.wins;
            if (a.isCurrent !== b.isCurrent) return b.isCurrent ? 1 : -1;
            return 0;
          });

          const topThree = sorted.slice(0, 3);

          for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            const seg = document.getElementById(`rank-${i}`);
            if (seg) {
              const player = topThree[i];
              if (player) {
                seg.style.display = 'flex';
                seg.style.backgroundColor = player.color;
                const balanceHtml = gameState.isTournament ? `<span class="rank-balance">${t('bal')}${player.balance}</span>` : '';
                seg.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="rank-badge">#${i + 1}</span>
                    <span class="rank-name">${player.name}</span>
                    <div class="rank-stats-row">
                      <span class="rank-wins">${t('win')}:${player.wins}</span>
                      ${balanceHtml}
                    </div>
                `;
              } else {
                seg.style.display = 'none';
              }
            }
          }
        }

        // Load statistics on game start
        if (mode === 'play') {
          loadWinStats();
        } else {
          // In edit mode, just show the current stats without loading
          winStats.loaded = true;
          updateWinStatsDisplay();
        }

        gameState.reactions = [];

        function spawnReaction(playerIdx, emoji, startX, startY) {
          gameState.reactions.push({
            emoji,
            x: startX,
            y: startY,
            playerIdx,
            t: 0,
            scale: 1,
            opacity: 1
          });
        }

        function drawReactions() {
          const ps = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape ? 1.12 : (layout.scale || 1.0);
          for (let i = gameState.reactions.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const r = gameState.reactions[i];
            r.t += 0.015;
            if (r.t >= 1) {
              gameState.reactions.splice(i, 1);
              continue;
            }

            const target = getCenterOfBase(r.playerIdx);
            const targetTiltY = layout.y + (target.y - layout.y) * (layout.tilt || 1.0);
            // Parabolic path in world space
            const ease = 1 - Math.pow(1 - r.t, 2);
            const currentX = r.x + (target.x - r.x) * ease;
            const currentY = r.y + (targetTiltY - r.y) * ease - Math.sin(r.t * Math.PI) * 200 * ps;
            
            r.scale = 0.5 + Math.sin(r.t * Math.PI) * 1.5;
            r.opacity = 1 - Math.pow(r.t, 3);

            ctx.save();
            ctx.globalAlpha = r.opacity;
            ctx.font = `bold ${40 * ps * r.scale}px Inter`;
            ctx.textAlign = 'center';
            ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
            ctx.shadowBlur = 15;
            ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)';
            ctx.fillText(r.emoji, currentX, currentY);
            ctx.restore();
          }
        }

        function getCurrentRotation() {
          return mode === 'play'
            ? gameState.rotationOffset
            : window.gameConfig.layout.rotation || 0;
        }

        function getArmAngle(i) {
          const baseAngle = i * 72 - 90;
          const adj = window.gameConfig.players[i]?.angleAdj || 0;
          const rot = getCurrentRotation();
          return (baseAngle + adj + rot) * (Math.PI / 180);
        }

        function getArmLocalToWorld(armIndex, lx, ly, ox = 0, oy = 0) {
          const p = window.gameConfig.players[armIndex];
          const angle = getArmAngle(armIndex);
          const s = Math.sin(angle);
          const c = Math.cos(angle);
          const effectiveScale = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape ? 1.12 : (layout.scale || 1.0);

          // Apply offsets in local coordinate system
          const totalLX = lx + (p.radiusAdj || 0) + ox;
          const totalLY = ly + oy;

          const rx = (totalLX * c - totalLY * s) * effectiveScale;
          const ry = (totalLX * s + totalLY * c) * effectiveScale;

          return { x: layout.x + rx, y: layout.y + ry };
        }

        function getSpaceCoords(armIndex, col, row) {
          const p = window.gameConfig.players[armIndex];
          const ss = layout.spaceSize;
          const wr = layout.wingRadius + (p?.radiusAdj || 0);
          const hScale = p?.spaceHeightScale ?? 1.0;
          const wScale = p?.widthAdj ?? 1.0;
          let lx = wr + (row + 0.5) * ss * hScale;
          let ly = (col - 1) * ss * wScale;

          let ox = p.xAdj || 0;
          let oy = p.yAdj || 0;

          if (col === 1) {
            lx += p.winPathXAdj || 0;
            ly += p.winPathYAdj || 0;
          }

          return getArmLocalToWorld(armIndex, lx, ly, ox, oy);
        }

        function getCenterOfPath(i) {
          return getSpaceCoords(i, 1, 2.5);
        }

        function getCenterOfBase(i) {
          const p = window.gameConfig.players[i];
          const ss = layout.spaceSize;
          const wr = layout.wingRadius;
          const dist = wr + 8.5 * ss;

          // Use arm's local coordinate system including the arm's own xAdj/yAdj
          // PLUS its own baseXAdj/baseYAdj.
          // This ensures the base follows the path when the path is moved
          // and correctly rotates with the board.
          const ox = (p.xAdj || 0) + (p.baseXAdj || 0);
          const oy = (p.yAdj || 0) + (p.baseYAdj || 0);

          return getArmLocalToWorld(i, dist, 0, ox, oy);
        }

        function getBasePawnPos(pIdx, iIdx) {
          const p = window.gameConfig.players[pIdx];
          const ss = layout.spaceSize;
          const bc = getCenterOfBase(pIdx);
          const armAngle = getArmAngle(pIdx);
          const baseRot = armAngle + (p.baseRotation || 0) * (Math.PI / 180);
          const s = Math.sin(baseRot);
          const c = Math.cos(baseRot);

          // These offsets define the pawn positions within the home base triangle.
          // They are spread out to fit the larger hexagonal board's base.
          const offsets = [
            { lx: -ss * 1.375, ly: -ss * 1.788 },
            { lx: -ss * 1.375, ly: ss * 1.788 },
            { lx: ss * 0.115, ly: -ss * 0.715 },
            { lx: ss * 0.115, ly: ss * 0.715 },
          ];

          const off = offsets[iIdx];
          const rx = (off.lx * c - off.ly * s) * layout.scale;
          const ry = (off.lx * s + off.ly * c) * layout.scale;
          return { x: bc.x + rx, y: bc.y + ry };
        }
        function getPawnPath(playerIndex) {
          const path = [];
          // Start from r=4 of c=2 of player's arm (the right side entry square)
          for (let r = 4; r >= 0; r--) path.push({ arm: playerIndex, c: 2, r });

          // Visit the other 4 arms in clockwise order
          for (let i = 1; i < 5; i++) {
            const arm = (playerIndex + i) % 5;
            for (let r = 0; r < 6; r++) path.push({ arm, c: 0, r });
            path.push({ arm, c: 1, r: 5 });
            for (let r = 5; r >= 0; r--) path.push({ arm, c: 2, r });
          }

          // Visit the remaining squares of the starting arm to complete the loop
          for (let r = 0; r < 6; r++) path.push({ arm: playerIndex, c: 0, r });
          path.push({ arm: playerIndex, c: 1, r: 5 });
          path.push({ arm: playerIndex, c: 2, r: 5 });

          // Home path
          for (let r = 0; r < 6; r++) {
            path.push({ arm: playerIndex, c: 1, r });
          }
          return path;
        }

        function applySplitSacrifice(pIdx, movingPawnIdx, startPos, possibleCaptures, usedDieIdx) {
            if (startPos === -1) return false;
            if (!possibleCaptures || !Array.isArray(possibleCaptures) || possibleCaptures.length === 0) return false;
            
            // Only apply if the pawn is part of a capture that involves combined dice numbers
            const isCombinedCaptureStep = possibleCaptures.some(c => 
                c && c.pawnIdx === movingPawnIdx && 
                Array.isArray(c.diceIndices) &&
                c.diceIndices.length > 1 && 
                c.diceIndices[0] === usedDieIdx
            );
            
            if (!isCombinedCaptureStep) return false;

            const myPawns = gameState.pawns[pIdx];
            const path = getPawnPath(pIdx);
            const startSpace = path[startPos];
            const isSafe = (startSpace.c === 2 && startSpace.r === 4) || (startSpace.c === 0 && startSpace.r === 3);
            if (isSafe) return false;

            // Find partners still at startPos
            let partners = [];
            myPawns.forEach((p, i) => {
                if (i !== movingPawnIdx && p.status === 'on-path' && p.pos === startPos) {
                    partners.push(i);
                }
            });

            // If there's exactly one partner left and NO one else (meaning it was a double stack and now it's alone)
            if (partners.length === 1) {
                const partnerIdx = partners[0];
                myPawns[partnerIdx].status = 'home';
                myPawns[partnerIdx].pos = -1;
                lib.log(`Stack split sacrifice: Pawn ${partnerIdx} sent home`);
                playSound('01KZ3W7PP9XZQ20XX5V8S0MJXW'); // Vinyl stop sound for penalty
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        function rollDice(forcedDice = null) {
          gameState.hasRolled = false;
          gameState.isRolling = true;
          gameState.isSettling = false;
          
          playSound('freesound_community-dice-95077.mp3');

          gameState.isRolling = true;
          gameState.rollAnimTime = 60; // Longer animation for 3D travel
          gameState.rollStartedAt = Date.now();
          gameState.settleStartedAt = 0;
          
          if (forcedDice) {
              gameState.dice = [...forcedDice];
          } else {
              gameState.dice = [Math.floor(Math.random() * 6) + 1, Math.floor(Math.random() * 6) + 1];
          }
          
          rollHint.innerText = 'Rolling...';

          if (gameState.dice[0] === 6 && gameState.dice[1] === 6) {
              triggerAISpeech(gameState.currentPlayer, 'lucky_roll', "I rolled a double six!");
          } else {
              triggerAISpeech(gameState.currentPlayer, 'roll', `Rolling for ${gameState.dice[0]} and ${gameState.dice[1]}`);
          }

          // Initialize 3D dice positions and velocities
          gameState.dice3D.forEach((d, i) => {
            d.active = true;
            // Start from bottom or sides
            d.x = layout.x + (i === 0 ? -200 : 200);
            d.y = layout.y + 400;
            d.z = 100;
            
            // Target the center with some offset
            const spacing = 40 * (layout.diceScale || 1.0);
            d.targetX = layout.x + (i === 0 ? -spacing : spacing);
            d.targetY = layout.y + (i === 0 ? -spacing/2 : spacing/2);
            
            // Initial velocity to jump towards center
            d.vx = (d.targetX - d.x) / 60;
            d.vy = (d.targetY - d.y) / 60;
            d.vz = 5;
            
            // Random initial rotation
            d.rx = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            d.ry = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            d.rz = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            
            // High rotation velocity
            d.vrx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
            d.vry = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
            d.vrz = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;

            // Target rotation for the final value
            // 1: rx=0, ry=0; 6: rx=PI, ry=0; 2: rx=-PI/2, ry=0; 5: rx=PI/2, ry=0; 3: rx=0, ry=-PI/2; 4: rx=0, ry=PI/2
            const val = gameState.dice[i];
            d.targetRX = 0;
            d.targetRY = 0;
            if (val === 6) d.targetRX = Math.PI;
            else if (val === 2) d.targetRX = -Math.PI / 2;
            else if (val === 5) d.targetRX = Math.PI / 2;
            else if (val === 3) d.targetRY = Math.PI / 2;
            else if (val === 4) d.targetRY = -Math.PI / 2;
            
            // Add many full rotations to target
            d.targetRX += Math.PI * 4 * (Math.random() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1);
            d.targetRY += Math.PI * 4 * (Math.random() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1);
            d.targetRZ = Math.PI * 4 * (Math.random() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1);
          });

          triggerAISpeech(gameState.currentPlayer, 'roll');
        }

        function arePawnsSettled() {
          for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < 5; pIdx++) {
            for (let iIdx = 0; iIdx < 4; iIdx++) {
              const pawn = gameState.pawns[pIdx][iIdx];
              if (pawn.renderX === 0) continue;
              let target;
              if (pawn.pos === -1) target = getBasePawnPos(pIdx, iIdx);
              else {
                const path = getPawnPath(pIdx);
                const pData = path[Math.min(path.length - 1, Math.round(pawn.pos))];
                target = getSpaceCoords(pData.arm, pData.c, pData.r);
              }
              // Increased tolerance from 2.0 to 5.0 for more robust settling detection
              if (Math.hypot(target.x - pawn.renderX, target.y - pawn.renderY) > 5.0) {
                return false;
              }
            }
          }
          return true;
        }
        function nextTurn() {
          if (mode === 'edit') return;
          // In an online room, an invited device must never advance another
          // player's turn locally. It waits for the host's room snapshot instead.
          if (window.__roomSystem && window.__roomSystem.isConnected() &&
              !window.__roomSystem.isHost() &&
              !window.__roomSystem.isMySeat(gameState.currentPlayer)) return;
          gameState.pendingNextTurn = false;
          gameState.isRuleActive = false; // Safety: Clear rule lock when transitioning turns
          if (gameState.extraTurnEarned) {
            gameState.extraTurnEarned = false;
            // Persistence of lucky7UsedThisTurn ensures bonus only once per turn sequence (series of extra turns)
          } else {
            gameState.currentPlayer = (gameState.currentPlayer + 1) % 5;
            gameState.lucky7UsedThisTurn = false;
          }
          gameState.hasRolled = false;
          gameState.isRolling = false;
          gameState.isSettling = false;
          gameState.pendingNextTurn = false;
          gameState.pendingNextTurnStartedAt = 0;
          gameState.movesAvailable = [];
          gameState.dice = [0, 0];
          gameState.selectedMoveIndex = null;
          gameState.isWaitingToPass = false;
          gameState.aiThinkTimer = 1.2;
          gameState.turnMaxTime = window.gameConfig.layout.turnMaxTime || 90;
          gameState.turnTimer = gameState.turnMaxTime;
          // Reset consecutive 6 counter for new player when entering their turn fresh
          gameState.consecutiveSixes[gameState.currentPlayer] = 0;
          
          updateDiceHud();
          const p = window.gameConfig.players[gameState.currentPlayer];
          rollHint.innerText = `${p.name}'s turn!`;
          rollHint.classList.add('visible');
          if (!p.isAI) {
            playSound('universfield-new-notification-043-493471.mp3', 0.6);
            // A full round has come back to the human player — let any bruised
            // AI feelings from captures start softening, like family does.
            driftBondsTowardNeutral();
          }
          
          if (window.gameConfig.layout.boardRotationEnabled) {
              const desiredRotation = 180 - gameState.currentPlayer * 72;
              let diff = desiredRotation - gameState.rotationOffset;
              while (diff > 180) diff -= 360;
              while (diff < -180) diff += 360;
              gameState.targetRotation = gameState.rotationOffset + diff;
              playSound('soundreality-whoosh-large-sub-384631.mp3');
          }

          // Deactivate dice so they return to bottom for the next player
          gameState.dice3D.forEach(d => {
            d.active = false;
          });

          triggerAISpeech(gameState.currentPlayer, 'turn');

          // Share the new board state with everyone else in the room, if we're
          // the one whose action just caused this turn transition.
          if (window.__roomSystem && window.__roomSystem.isConnected()) {
            window.__roomSystem.broadcastState();
          }
        }

        function canAnyPawnMove(pIdx, moves) {
          return gameState.pawns[pIdx].some((p, i) => moves.some((m) => canMovePawn(pIdx, i, m)));
        }

        // Helper: returns true if pawn is in the player's colored home path (safe home stretch)
        function isPawnInColoredHomePath(pIdx, pawn) {
          if (!pawn || pawn.status !== 'on-path') return false;
          const p = Number(pIdx) || 0;
          const path = (typeof getPawnPath === 'function') ? getPawnPath(p) : null;
          if (!path || pawn.pos === undefined || pawn.pos < 0) return false;
          const homeStretchStart = Math.max(0, path.length - 6);
          if (pawn.pos < homeStretchStart) return false;
          const sq = path[pawn.pos];
          return !!(sq && sq.c === 1 && sq.arm === p);
        }

        // Check if player meets the Lucky 7 condition:
        // "it happen only when:
        //  - 4 tokens at base home
        //  - or 3 base home and one is home (finished) or colored home path
        //  - or 2 at base home and 2 are home (finished) or colored home path
        //  - or 1 at base home and 3 are home (finished) or colored home path"
        // (i.e. strictly 1 to 4 tokens at base home, all remaining tokens finished or in colored home path, and ZERO tokens on the open track)
        function checkSevenRollConditions(pIdx) {
          const p = Number(pIdx);
          if (!gameState.pawns || !gameState.pawns[p]) return false;
          const pawns = gameState.pawns[p];

          let baseHomeCount = 0;
          let finishedHomeCount = 0;
          let coloredPathCount = 0;
          let openTrackCount = 0;

          pawns.forEach(pawn => {
            if (pawn.status === 'home') {
              baseHomeCount++;
            } else if (pawn.status === 'finished') {
              finishedHomeCount++;
            } else if (pawn.status === 'on-path') {
              if (isPawnInColoredHomePath(p, pawn)) {
                coloredPathCount++;
              } else {
                openTrackCount++;
              }
            } else {
              openTrackCount++;
            }
          });

          // Must have ZERO tokens out on the open/contested common track
          if (openTrackCount > 0) return false;

          // Must have 1, 2, 3, or 4 tokens at base home, and ALL remaining tokens home (finished) or in colored home path
          const safeOrFinished = finishedHomeCount + coloredPathCount;
          return (baseHomeCount >= 1 && baseHomeCount <= 4) && (baseHomeCount + safeOrFinished === 4);
        }

        function checkAndApplyLucky7(pIdx, dice) {
          if (!dice || dice.length < 2) return false;
          const p = Number(pIdx);
          const d1 = Number(dice[0]) || 0;
          const d2 = Number(dice[1]) || 0;
          if (d1 + d2 !== 7) return false; // Any combination totaling 7: 1+6, 2+5, 3+4, 4+3, 5+2, 6+1
          if (gameState.lucky7UsedThisTurn) return false;

          // Only happens under the exact specified token position conditions
          if (checkSevenRollConditions(p)) {
            gameState.lucky7UsedThisTurn = true;
            gameState.extraTurnEarned = true;
            gameState.movesAvailable = [6, 6]; // Total 7 becomes 6 + 6!
            gameState.dice = [6, 6]; // 3D dice also transform to 6 + 6!
            showRule('lucky7');
            if (typeof playSevenRollSound === 'function') playSevenRollSound();
            if (typeof triggerAISpeech === 'function') {
              triggerAISpeech(p, 'lucky7', "The Rule of 7 gave me double sixes!");
            }
            return true;
          }
          return false;
        }

        // Play the 7-roll congratulation sound
        async function playSevenRollSound() {
          playSound('congratulation_granted_6_6');
          lib.log('7-roll congratulation sound triggered');
        }

        function isEntrySquareThreatened(pIdx) {
          // Check if the entry square (where pawns deploy) is under threat from opponent pawns
          const path = getPawnPath(pIdx);
          const entrySpace = path[0]; // Entry square is at position 0
          
          // Check all opponent pawns to see if they can reach this entry square
          for (let oIdx = 0; oIdx < 5; oIdx++) {
            if (oIdx === pIdx) continue; // Skip self
            
            for (const otherPawn of gameState.pawns[oIdx]) {
              if (otherPawn.status !== 'on-path') continue; // Only on-path pawns can capture
              
              const otherPath = getPawnPath(oIdx);
              const otherSpace = otherPath[otherPawn.pos];
              
              // Check if opponent pawn is on the entry square already
              if (otherSpace.arm === entrySpace.arm && 
                  otherSpace.c === entrySpace.c && 
                  otherSpace.r === entrySpace.r) {
                return true; // Entry square is occupied - threatened
              }
              
              // Check all possible moves for opponent pawns
              for (let dieValue = 1; dieValue <= 6; dieValue++) {
                const potentialPos = otherPawn.pos + dieValue;
                if (potentialPos < otherPath.length) {
                  const potentialSpace = otherPath[potentialPos];
                  if (potentialSpace.arm === entrySpace.arm && 
                      potentialSpace.c === entrySpace.c && 
                      potentialSpace.r === entrySpace.r) {
                    return true; // Opponent can capture the entry square
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          }
          
          return false; // Entry square is safe
        }

        function canMovePawn(pIdx, pawnIdx, steps) {
          const pawn = gameState.pawns[pIdx][pawnIdx];
          const path = getPawnPath(pIdx);
          if (pawn.status === 'home') return steps === 6;
          if (pawn.status === 'finished') return false;
          if (pawn.pos + steps >= path.length) return false;
          return true;
        }

        function wouldMoveCapture(pIdx, pawnIdx, steps) {
          if (!canMovePawn(pIdx, pawnIdx, steps)) return false;
          
          const pawn = gameState.pawns[pIdx][pawnIdx];
          const path = getPawnPath(pIdx);
          let targetPos = (pawn.status === 'home') ? 0 : pawn.pos + steps;
          
          if (targetPos >= path.length) return false;
          
          const targetSpace = path[targetPos];
          const isSafe = (targetSpace.c === 2 && targetSpace.r === 4) || (targetSpace.c === 0 && targetSpace.r === 3);
          if (isSafe) return false;

          // Stack Protection: Count how many pawns I will have there
          let myCount = 1; 
          gameState.pawns[pIdx].forEach((p, i) => {
              if (i === pawnIdx) return;
              if (p.status === 'on-path') {
                  const myPath = getPawnPath(pIdx);
                  const mySpace = myPath[p.pos];
                  if (mySpace.arm === targetSpace.arm && mySpace.c === targetSpace.c && mySpace.r === targetSpace.r) {
                      myCount++;
                  }
              }
          });

          for (let oIdx = 0; oIdx < 5; oIdx++) {
            if (oIdx === pIdx) continue;
            const otherPath = getPawnPath(oIdx);
            let otherCount = 0;
            gameState.pawns[oIdx].forEach(op => {
              if (op.status === 'on-path') {
                const otherSpace = otherPath[op.pos];
                if (otherSpace.arm === targetSpace.arm && otherSpace.c === targetSpace.c && otherSpace.r === targetSpace.r) {
                  otherCount++;
                }
              }
            });

            if (otherCount > 0 && myCount >= otherCount) return true;
          }
          return false;
        }

        function getPossibleCaptures(pIdx, availableMoves) {
          const captures = []; // { pawnIdx, diceIndices: [] }
          const playerPawns = gameState.pawns[pIdx];
          const path = getPawnPath(pIdx);

          const checkCaptureAtPos = (pos, movingPawnIdx) => {
            if (pos < 0 || pos >= path.length) return false;
            const targetSpace = path[pos];
            const isSafe = (targetSpace.c === 2 && targetSpace.r === 4) || (targetSpace.c === 0 && targetSpace.r === 3);
            if (isSafe) return false;

            // Stack Protection
            let myCount = 1;
            playerPawns.forEach((p, i) => {
                if (i === movingPawnIdx) return;
                if (p.status === 'on-path') {
                    const myPath = getPawnPath(pIdx);
                    const mySpace = myPath[p.pos];
                    if (mySpace.arm === targetSpace.arm && mySpace.c === targetSpace.c && mySpace.r === targetSpace.r) {
                        myCount++;
                    }
                }
            });

            for (let oIdx = 0; oIdx < 5; oIdx++) {
              if (oIdx === pIdx) continue;
              const otherPath = getPawnPath(oIdx);
              let otherCount = 0;
              gameState.pawns[oIdx].forEach(op => {
                  if (op.status === 'on-path') {
                      const otherSpace = otherPath[op.pos];
                      if (otherSpace.arm === targetSpace.arm && otherSpace.c === targetSpace.c && otherSpace.r === targetSpace.r) {
                          otherCount++;
                      }
                  }
              });

              if (otherCount > 0 && myCount >= otherCount) return true;
            }
            return false;
          };

          playerPawns.forEach((pawn, pI) => {
            if (pawn.status === 'finished') return;

            // Single dice
            availableMoves.forEach((mv, mvIdx) => {
              if (!canMovePawn(pIdx, pI, mv)) return;
              let targetPos = (pawn.status === 'home') ? 0 : (pawn.pos + mv);
              if (checkCaptureAtPos(targetPos, pI)) {
                captures.push({ pawnIdx: pI, diceIndices: [mvIdx] });
              }
            });

            // Combinations
            if (availableMoves.length === 2) {
              // Die 0 then Die 1
              if (canMovePawn(pIdx, pI, availableMoves[0])) {
                let pos1 = (pawn.status === 'home') ? 0 : (pawn.pos + availableMoves[0]);
                if (pos1 < path.length) {
                   const steps2 = availableMoves[1];
                   if (pos1 + steps2 < path.length) {
                     if (checkCaptureAtPos(pos1 + steps2, pI)) {
                       captures.push({ pawnIdx: pI, diceIndices: [0, 1] });
                     }
                   }
                }
              }
              // Die 1 then Die 0
              if (canMovePawn(pIdx, pI, availableMoves[1])) {
                let pos1 = (pawn.status === 'home') ? 0 : (pawn.pos + availableMoves[1]);
                if (pos1 < path.length) {
                   const steps2 = availableMoves[0];
                   if (pos1 + steps2 < path.length) {
                     if (checkCaptureAtPos(pos1 + steps2, pI)) {
                       captures.push({ pawnIdx: pI, diceIndices: [1, 0] });
                     }
                   }
                }
              }
            }
          });
          return captures;
        }

        function movePawn(pIdx, pawnIdx, steps) {
          if (!canMovePawn(pIdx, pawnIdx, steps)) return false;
          const pawn = gameState.pawns[pIdx][pawnIdx];
          const path = getPawnPath(pIdx);
          if (pawn.status === 'home') {
            pawn.status = 'on-path';
            pawn.pos = 0;
            playSound('chrysalyn-sports-car-acceleration-and-driving-away-562433.mp3', 0.5);
          } else {
            pawn.pos += steps;
            playSound('chrysalyn-sports-car-acceleration-and-driving-away-562433.mp3', 0.5);
            if (pawn.pos === path.length - 1) {
              pawn.status = 'finished';
              gameState.extraTurnEarned = true;
              if (window.gameConfig.players[pIdx].isAI) updateBond(pIdx, 2);
            } else if (pawn.pos > path.length - 10) {
              triggerAISpeech(pIdx, 'home_stretch', "I am so close to winning!");
            }
          }
          
          // Check for victory after any move (new rule: win if all 4 are on home stretch)
          checkForWin(pIdx);

          if (pawn.status === 'on-path') {
              const currentSpace = path[pawn.pos];
              const isSafe = (currentSpace.c === 2 && currentSpace.r === 4) || (currentSpace.c === 0 && currentSpace.r === 3);
              if (!isSafe) {
                  // Stack Protection Rule: Count current player pawns on target square
                  let myCount = 0;
                  gameState.pawns[pIdx].forEach(p => {
                      if (p.status === 'on-path') {
                          const myPath = getPawnPath(pIdx);
                          const mySpace = myPath[p.pos];
                          if (mySpace.arm === currentSpace.arm && mySpace.c === currentSpace.c && mySpace.r === currentSpace.r) {
                              myCount++;
                          }
                      }
                  });

                  gameState.pawns.forEach((otherPlayerPawns, otherPIdx) => {
                      if (otherPIdx === pIdx) return;
                      
                      // Count other player's pawns on this square
                      let otherCount = 0;
                      otherPlayerPawns.forEach(p => {
                          if (p.status === 'on-path') {
                              const oPath = getPawnPath(otherPIdx);
                              const oSpace = oPath[p.pos];
                              if (oSpace.arm === currentSpace.arm && oSpace.c === currentSpace.c && oSpace.r === currentSpace.r) {
                                  otherCount++;
                              }
                          }
                      });

                      // Only capture if myCount >= otherCount
                      if (otherCount > 0 && myCount >= otherCount) {
                          otherPlayerPawns.forEach((otherPawn) => {
                              if (otherPawn.status === 'on-path') {
                                  const otherPath = getPawnPath(otherPIdx);
                                  const otherSpace = otherPath[otherPawn.pos];
                                  if (otherSpace.arm === currentSpace.arm && otherSpace.c === currentSpace.c && otherSpace.r === currentSpace.r) {
                                      otherPawn.status = 'home';
                                      otherPawn.pos = -1;
                                      gameState.extraTurnEarned = true;
                                      playSound('01KZ3W7PP9XZQ20XX5V8S0MJXW');

                                      if (window.gameConfig.players[otherPIdx].isAI) updateBond(otherPIdx, -2);
                                      if (window.gameConfig.players[pIdx].isAI) updateBond(pIdx, 1);

                                      // Real capture tally between the human and each AI, for
                                      // genuine (not invented) shared-history callbacks in chat.
                                      if (!winStats.history) winStats.history = {};
                                      const humanIdx = window.gameConfig.players.findIndex(pl => !pl.isAI);
                                      if (pIdx === humanIdx && window.gameConfig.players[otherPIdx].isAI) {
                                        // Human captured this AI -> from the AI's own POV, "they captured me"
                                        const name = window.gameConfig.players[otherPIdx].name;
                                        if (!winStats.history[name]) winStats.history[name] = { matches: 0, iCaptured: 0, theyCaptured: 0 };
                                        winStats.history[name].theyCaptured++;
                                      } else if (otherPIdx === humanIdx && window.gameConfig.players[pIdx].isAI) {
                                        // This AI captured the human -> from the AI's own POV, "I captured them"
                                        const name = window.gameConfig.players[pIdx].name;
                                        if (!winStats.history[name]) winStats.history[name] = { matches: 0, iCaptured: 0, theyCaptured: 0 };
                                        winStats.history[name].iCaptured++;
                                      }
                                      saveWinStats();

                                      const capturerName = window.gameConfig.players[pIdx].name;
                                      const victimName = window.gameConfig.players[otherPIdx].name;
                                      triggerAISpeech(pIdx, 'capture', `I just knocked out ${victimName}!`);
                                      triggerAISpeech(otherPIdx, 'captured', `${capturerName} just knocked me out!`);
                                  }
                              }
                          });
                      }
                  });
              }
          }
          return true;
        }

        // Check if a player has won the game
        function checkForWin(pIdx) {
          const playerPawns = gameState.pawns[pIdx];
          const path = getPawnPath(pIdx);
          const homePathStartIndex = path.length - 6; // Last 6 squares including finish
          
          const winConditionMet = playerPawns.every(p => {
            return p.status === 'finished' || (p.status === 'on-path' && p.pos >= homePathStartIndex);
          });
          
          if (winConditionMet) {
            const playerName = window.gameConfig.players[pIdx].name;
            const playerColor = getPlayerColor(pIdx);
            
            // Update win statistics
            if (mode === 'play' && winStats.loaded) {
              updatePlayerWin(playerName);
            }
            
            triggerAISpeech(pIdx, 'win');
            for(let i=0; i<5; i++) {
                if(i !== pIdx) triggerAISpeech(i, 'captured', `${playerName} has won the match.`);
            }
            
            // Show victory message
            rollHint.innerHTML = `🎉 <span style="color: ${getPlayerColor(pIdx)}">${playerName}</span> ${t('win_text')} 🎉`;
            rollHint.classList.add('visible');
            
            // Reset game after a delay
            setTimeout(() => {
              initPawns();
              gameState.gameStarted = false;
              gameState.currentPlayer = 0;
              gameState.hasRolled = false;
              gameState.movesAvailable = [];
              gameState.isWaitingToPass = false;
              gameState.extraTurnEarned = false;
              rollHint.classList.remove('visible');
              nextTurn();
            }, 4000);
          }
        }

        function getCenterOfWinHome(i) {
          const wr = layout.wingRadius;
          const effectiveScale = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape ? 1.12 : (layout.scale || 1.0);
          const r_outer =
            (wr / Math.cos(Math.PI / 5) + (layout.centerRadiusAdj || 0)) * effectiveScale;
          const rot = getCurrentRotation();
          const centerAngleDeg = i * 72 - 90 + (layout.centerRotation || 0) + rot;
          const angleStart = ((centerAngleDeg - 36) * Math.PI) / 180;
          const angleEnd = ((centerAngleDeg + 36) * Math.PI) / 180;

          const p1 = {
            x: layout.x + Math.cos(angleStart) * r_outer,
            y: layout.y + Math.sin(angleStart) * r_outer,
          };
          const p2 = {
            x: layout.x + Math.cos(angleEnd) * r_outer,
            y: layout.y + Math.sin(angleEnd) * r_outer,
          };
          const pCenter = { x: layout.x, y: layout.y };

          const p = window.gameConfig.players[i];
          const wx = (p.winXAdj || 0) * effectiveScale;
          const wy = (p.winYAdj || 0) * effectiveScale;

          return { x: (p1.x + p2.x + pCenter.x) / 3 + wx, y: (p1.y + p2.y + pCenter.y) / 3 + wy };
        }

        function drawBoard() {
          const ss = layout.spaceSize;
          const wr = layout.wingRadius;
          const ps = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape ? 1.12 : (layout.scale || 1.0);
          let themeId = window.gameConfig.layout.boardThemeId || 'classic';
          if (mode === 'play' && gameState.isTournament) {
            themeId = window.gameConfig.layout.tournamentBoardThemeId || 'neon';
          }
          const theme = BOARD_THEMES[themeId] || BOARD_THEMES.classic;

          // Performance: Calculate pulse once per frame
          const now = Date.now();
          const pulseValue = Math.sin(now / 200);
          const basePulse = Math.sin(now / 150);
          
          // Clear gradient cache periodically to avoid memory leaks if scales change
          if (gameState.frameCount % 60 === 0) {
              gameState.gradientCache = {};
          }

          for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            const playerColor = getPlayerColor(i);
            const p = window.gameConfig.players[i];
            const armAngle = getArmAngle(i);
            const sw = ss * (p.widthAdj || 1.0) * ps;
            const sh = ss * (p.spaceHeightScale || 1.0) * ps;

            for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) {
              for (let r = 0; r < 6; r++) {
                const pos = getSpaceCoords(i, c, r);
                ctx.save();
                ctx.translate(pos.x, pos.y);
                ctx.rotate(armAngle);

                let trackFill = theme.track;
                ctx.fillStyle = (c === 1 && r < 5) || (c === 2 && r === 4) ? playerColor : trackFill;

                const isSafe = (c === 2 && r === 4) || (c === 0 && r === 3);
                if (isSafe && c !== 1) {
                    ctx.fillStyle = gameState.isTournament ? playerColor : theme.safe;
                }
                if (c === 2 && r === 4) ctx.fillStyle = playerColor;
                
                // Tournament mode: minimal shadows
                if (gameState.isTournament && themeId !== 'classic') {
                    if (gameState.frameCount % 3 === 0) {
                        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
                        ctx.shadowBlur = 8 * ps; 
                        ctx.shadowColor = playerColor;
                        ctx.lineWidth = 3 * ps;
                    } else {
                        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
                        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                        ctx.lineWidth = 3 * ps;
                    }
                } else {
                    ctx.strokeStyle = theme.border; // This will be black for classic theme
                    ctx.lineWidth = 3 * ps;
                    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                }

                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.rect(-sw / 2, -sh / 2, sw, sh);
                ctx.fill();
                ctx.stroke();

                if (isSafe) {
                  ctx.shadowBlur = 0; 
                  ctx.fillStyle = '#000000';
                  const starFontSize = (gameState.isTournament || mode === 'play') ? Math.floor(36 * ps) : Math.floor(22 * ps);
                  ctx.font = `bold ${starFontSize}px Inter`;
                  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
                  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
                  
                  if (gameState.isTournament || mode === 'play') {
                    ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
                    ctx.lineWidth = 4 * ps;
                    ctx.strokeText('★', 0, 0);
                    ctx.fillText('★', 0, 0);
                  } else {
                    ctx.strokeStyle = theme.border;
                    ctx.lineWidth = 2 * ps;
                    ctx.strokeText('☆', 0, 8 * ps);
                    ctx.fillText('☆', 0, 8 * ps);
                  }
                }

                if (window.gameConfig.layout.showSquareNumbers) {
                  ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                  ctx.save();
                  ctx.rotate(-armAngle);
                  const isColored = (c === 1 && r < 5) || (c === 2 && r === 4);
                  ctx.fillStyle = isColored ? '#ffffff' : theme.text;
                  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.85;
                  ctx.font = `bold ${Math.floor(16 * ps)}px Inter`;
                  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
                  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
                  ctx.shadowBlur = 0;

                  const sqNum = i * 18 + c * 6 + r + 1;
                  ctx.fillText(sqNum.toString(), 0, 0);
                  ctx.restore();
                }

                if (c === 2 && r === 4) {
                  ctx.save();
                  ctx.fillStyle = playerColor;
                  ctx.font = `bold ${Math.floor(28 * ps)}px Inter`;
                  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
                  ctx.fillText('↑', 0, 10 * ps);
                  ctx.restore();
                }
                ctx.restore();
              }
            }
          }
          for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            const playerColor = getPlayerColor(i);
            const p = window.gameConfig.players[i];
            const armAngle = getArmAngle(i);
            ctx.save();
            const baseCenter = getCenterOfBase(i);
            const baseWidth = 6 * ss * ps * (p.baseWidthScale || 1.0);
            const baseHeight = 5 * ss * ps * (p.baseHeightScale || 1.0);
            ctx.translate(baseCenter.x, baseCenter.y);
            const totalBaseRot = armAngle + ((p.baseRotation || 0) * Math.PI) / 180;
            ctx.rotate(totalBaseRot);

            ctx.globalAlpha = p.baseOpacity ?? 1.0;
            ctx.fillStyle = playerColor;
            
            // Tournament: minimal shadow (only every 6 frames for performance)
            if (gameState.isTournament && gameState.frameCount % 6 === 0) {
                ctx.shadowBlur = 12 * ps;
                ctx.shadowColor = playerColor;
            } else {
                ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
            }

            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(baseHeight / 2, 0);
            ctx.lineTo(-baseHeight / 2, -baseWidth / 2);
            ctx.lineTo(-baseHeight / 2, baseWidth / 2);
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.fill();
            if (gameState.isTournament && themeId !== 'classic') {
                ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
                ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                ctx.lineWidth = 3 * ps;
            } else {
                ctx.strokeStyle = theme.border;
                ctx.lineWidth = 4 * ps;
                ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
            }
            ctx.stroke();

            ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
            ctx.fillStyle = gameState.isTournament ? '#ffffff' : theme.track;
            const inset = 0.7;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo((baseHeight / 2) * inset, 0);
            ctx.lineTo((-baseHeight / 2) * inset, (-baseWidth / 2) * inset);
            ctx.lineTo((-baseHeight / 2) * inset, (baseWidth / 2) * inset);
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.stroke();
            
            const plateWidth = baseWidth * 0.95; 
            const plateHeight = 40 * ps;
            const plateX = -baseHeight / 2 - plateHeight / 2 - 15 * ps;
            const plateY = 0;

            ctx.save();
            ctx.translate(plateX, plateY);
            ctx.rotate(Math.PI / 2);

            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.roundRect(-plateWidth / 2 + 4, -plateHeight / 2 + 4, plateWidth, plateHeight, 8 * ps);
            ctx.fill();

            ctx.fillStyle = theme.track;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.roundRect(-plateWidth / 2 - 2, -plateHeight / 2 - 2, plateWidth + 4, plateHeight + 4, 10 * ps);
            ctx.fill();

            ctx.fillStyle = playerColor;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.roundRect(-plateWidth / 2, -plateHeight / 2, plateWidth, plateHeight, 8 * ps);
            ctx.fill();

            ctx.fillStyle = gameState.isTournament ? '#000000' : '#ffffff';
            let fontSize = 28 * ps;
            ctx.font = `bold ${fontSize}px Fredoka`;
            
            const name = p.name.toUpperCase();
            const textMetrics = ctx.measureText(name);
            if (textMetrics.width > plateWidth * 0.8) {
                fontSize *= (plateWidth * 0.8) / textMetrics.width;
                ctx.font = `bold ${fontSize}px Fredoka`;
            }

            ctx.textAlign = 'center';
            ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
            ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8)';
            ctx.shadowBlur = 2;
            ctx.shadowOffsetX = 1;
            ctx.shadowOffsetY = 1;
            ctx.fillText(name, 0, 0);
            ctx.restore();
            ctx.restore();
          }
          for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            const playerColor = getPlayerColor(i);
            const p = window.gameConfig.players[i];
            const rot = getCurrentRotation();
            const centerAngleDeg = i * 72 - 90 + (layout.centerRotation || 0) + rot;
            const aStart = ((centerAngleDeg - 36) * Math.PI) / 180;
            const aEnd = ((centerAngleDeg + 36) * Math.PI) / 180;
            const r_pent = (wr / Math.cos(Math.PI / 5) + (layout.centerRadiusAdj || 0)) * ps;

            const wx = (p.winXAdj || 0) * ps;
            const wy = (p.winYAdj || 0) * ps;

            ctx.fillStyle = playerColor;
            
            // Tournament: minimal shadow (only every 6 frames)
            if (gameState.isTournament && gameState.frameCount % 6 === 0) {
                ctx.shadowBlur = 12 * ps;
                ctx.shadowColor = playerColor;
            } else {
                ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
            }

            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(layout.x + wx, layout.y + wy);
            ctx.lineTo(
              layout.x + Math.cos(aStart) * r_pent + wx,
              layout.y + Math.sin(aStart) * r_pent + wy
            );
            ctx.lineTo(
              layout.x + Math.cos(aEnd) * r_pent + wx,
              layout.y + Math.sin(aEnd) * r_pent + wy
            );
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.fill();
            if (gameState.isTournament && themeId !== 'classic') {
                ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
                ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
                ctx.lineWidth = 3 * ps;
            } else {
                ctx.strokeStyle = themeId === 'classic' ? '#000000' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)';
                ctx.lineWidth = 3 * ps;
                ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
            }
            ctx.stroke();
          }

          ctx.save();
          ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
          ctx.translate(layout.x, layout.y);
          ctx.rotate(((getCurrentRotation() + (layout.centerRotation || 0)) * Math.PI) / 180);
          ctx.fillStyle = themeId === 'neon' ? '#ffffff' : theme.homeText;
          ctx.font = `bold ${Math.floor(28 * ps)}px Fredoka`;
          ctx.textAlign = 'center';
          ctx.shadowBlur = 5;
          ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)';
          ctx.fillText('HOME', 0, 10 * ps);
          ctx.restore();
        }

        function rotate3D(p, rx, ry, rz) {
          let y = p.y * Math.cos(rx) - p.z * Math.sin(rx);
          let z = p.y * Math.sin(rx) + p.z * Math.cos(rx);
          p.y = y;
          p.z = z;
          let x = p.x * Math.cos(ry) + p.z * Math.sin(ry);
          z = -p.x * Math.sin(ry) + p.z * Math.cos(ry);
          p.x = x;
          p.z = z;
          x = p.x * Math.cos(rz) - p.y * Math.sin(rz);
          y = p.x * Math.sin(rz) + p.y * Math.cos(rz);
          p.x = x;
          p.y = y;
          return p;
        }

        function drawRoundedPolygon(ctx, points, radius) {
          if (points.length < 3) return;
          ctx.beginPath();
          for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i++) {
            const p1 = points[(i + points.length - 1) % points.length];
            const p2 = points[i];
            const p3 = points[(i + 1) % points.length];

            const dx1 = p2.x - p1.x;
            const dy1 = p2.y - p1.y;
            const len1 = Math.sqrt(dx1 * dx1 + dy1 * dy1);

            const dx2 = p2.x - p3.x;
            const dy2 = p2.y - p3.y;
            const len2 = Math.sqrt(dx2 * dx2 + dy2 * dy2);

            const r = Math.min(radius, len1 / 2, len2 / 2);

            const x1 = p2.x - (dx1 / len1) * r;
            const y1 = p2.y - (dy1 / len1) * r;
            const x2 = p2.x - (dx2 / len2) * r;
            const y2 = p2.y - (dy2 / len2) * r;

            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);
            else ctx.lineTo(x1, y1);
            ctx.quadraticCurveTo(p2.x, p2.y, x2, y2);
          }
          ctx.closePath();
        }

        function drawDiceDots(ctx, value, vertices, indices, ps) {
          const styleId = window.gameConfig.layout.diceStyle || 'classic';
          const style = DICE_STYLES[styleId] || DICE_STYLES.classic;
          ctx.save();
          ctx.fillStyle = style.dot;
          if (style.glow) {
            ctx.shadowColor = style.glow;
            ctx.shadowBlur = 8 * ps;
          }
          let cx = 0, cy = 0;
          indices.forEach((idx) => {
            cx += vertices[idx].x;
            cy += vertices[idx].y;
          });
          cx /= 4;
          cy /= 4;

          const v0 = vertices[indices[0]];
          const v1 = vertices[indices[1]];
          const v3 = vertices[indices[3]];

          const ux = (v1.x - v0.x) / 2;
          const uy = (v1.y - v0.y) / 2;
          const vx = (v3.x - v0.x) / 2;
          const vy = (v3.y - v0.y) / 2;

          const dotRadius = 4.5 * ps;
          const drawDot = (px, py) => {
            const dx = cx + px * ux + py * vx;
            const dy = cy + px * uy + py * vy;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(dx, dy, dotRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
            
            // Subtle highlight
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(dx - dotRadius*0.3, dy - dotRadius*0.3, dotRadius * 0.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.fillStyle = style.dot;
          };

          if (value === 1) drawDot(0, 0);
          if (value === 2) {
            drawDot(-0.5, -0.5);
            drawDot(0.5, 0.5);
          }
          if (value === 3) {
            drawDot(-0.5, -0.5);
            drawDot(0, 0);
            drawDot(0.5, 0.5);
          }
          if (value === 4) {
            drawDot(-0.5, -0.5);
            drawDot(0.5, -0.5);
            drawDot(-0.5, 0.5);
            drawDot(0.5, 0.5);
          }
          if (value === 5) {
            drawDot(-0.5, -0.5);
            drawDot(0.5, -0.5);
            drawDot(-0.5, 0.5);
            drawDot(0.5, 0.5);
            drawDot(0, 0);
          }
          if (value === 6) {
            drawDot(-0.5, -0.5);
            drawDot(0.5, -0.5);
            drawDot(-0.5, 0.5);
            drawDot(0.5, 0.5);
            drawDot(-0.5, 0);
            drawDot(0.5, 0);
          }
          ctx.restore();
        }

        function drawDice() {
          const ps = 1.0; // Detached from Global Scale for fixed UI positioning
          const ds = layout.diceScale || 1.0;

          gameState.dice3D.forEach((d, i) => {
            let drawX = d.x;
            let drawY = d.y;
            let drawZ = d.z;
            let rx = d.rx;
            let ry = d.ry;
            let rz = d.rz;

            const selectionScale = d.hoverScale || 1;
            const selectionZOffset = (d.hoverOffset || 0) * ps;

            if (!d.active) {
              const spacing = 60 * ps * ds;
              const isLandscape = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape;
              // Shifted left to match board alignment
              drawX = isLandscape ? (830 + (i === 0 ? -spacing : spacing)) : (layout.x + (i === 0 ? -spacing : spacing));
              if (isLandscape) {
                  drawY = 610;
              } else {
                  let yOff = layout.diceYOffset;
                  drawY = layout.y + yOff * ps;
              }
              drawZ = 0;
              rx = 0;
              ry = 0;
              rz = 0;
            }

            // Draw Shadow - simplified for performance
            ctx.save();
            ctx.translate(drawX, drawY);
            const shadowSize = (30 * ps * ds * selectionScale) * (1 + (drawZ + selectionZOffset) / (150 * ps * ds));
            const shadowAlpha = Math.max(0, 0.3 - (drawZ + selectionZOffset) / (400 * ps * ds));
            ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${shadowAlpha})`;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.ellipse(0, 5 * ps, shadowSize, shadowSize * 0.6, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.restore();

            ctx.save();
            const screenX = drawX;
            const screenY = drawY - (drawZ + selectionZOffset);

            ctx.translate(screenX, screenY);

            const size = 30 * ps * ds * selectionScale;
            const vertices = cubeVertices.map((v) => {
              const p = { x: v.x * size, y: v.y * size, z: v.z * size };
              return rotate3D(p, rx, ry, rz);
            });

            const facesWithZ = cubeFaces.map((f) => {
              const avgZ = f.indices.reduce((sum, idx) => sum + vertices[idx].z, 0) / 4;
              return { ...f, avgZ };
            });
            facesWithZ.sort((a, b) => a.avgZ - b.avgZ);

            const cornerRadius = 8 * ps;

            facesWithZ.forEach((f) => {
              const facePoints = f.indices.map(idx => vertices[idx]);
              drawRoundedPolygon(ctx, facePoints, cornerRadius);

              const styleId = window.gameConfig.layout.diceStyle || 'classic';
              const style = DICE_STYLES[styleId] || DICE_STYLES.classic;
              
              const shade = 1.0 + f.avgZ / (size * 4);
              const r = Math.min(255, style.face[0] * shade);
              const g = Math.min(255, style.face[1] * shade);
              const b = Math.min(255, style.face[2] * shade);
              
              ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r}, ${g}, ${b})`;

              if (style.glow) {
                  ctx.shadowColor = style.glow;
                  ctx.shadowBlur = (d.active && gameState.isRolling ? 15 : 5) * ps * ds;
              } else {
                  ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
              }

              ctx.fill();
              ctx.strokeStyle = style.border;
              ctx.lineWidth = 1 * ps;
              ctx.stroke();

              ctx.shadowBlur = 0;

              if (f.avgZ > 0) {
                drawDiceDots(ctx, f.value, vertices, f.indices, ps);
              }
            });
            ctx.restore();
          });
        }

        function drawPawns() {
          const isRotating =
            mode === 'play' && Math.abs(gameState.targetRotation - gameState.rotationOffset) > 0.1;
          const ss = layout.spaceSize;
          const ps = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape ? 1.12 : (layout.scale || 1.0);
          const moveSpeed = window.gameConfig.layout.pawnSpeed || 0.05;

          // Determine if we should show highlights for human player
          const currentPlayerObj = window.gameConfig.players[gameState.currentPlayer];
          const isHumanTurn = mode === 'play' && currentPlayerObj && !currentPlayerObj.isAI && gameState.gameStarted && !gameState.isRuleActive;
          let activeSteps = null;
          if (isHumanTurn && gameState.hasRolled && !gameState.isRolling && !gameState.isSettling && gameState.movesAvailable.length > 0) {
              if (gameState.movesAvailable.length === 1) activeSteps = gameState.movesAvailable[0];
              else if (gameState.selectedMoveIndex !== null) activeSteps = gameState.movesAvailable[gameState.selectedMoveIndex];
          }

          // Calculate occupancy for stacking and sort players/pawns for correct overlap
          const occupancy = new Map();
          const drawOrder = [];

          for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < 5; pIdx++) {
            gameState.pawns[pIdx].forEach((pawn, iIdx) => {
              drawOrder.push({ pIdx, iIdx, pos: pawn.displayPos });
            });
          }

          // Reverse overlap sorting: tokens further back (smaller displayPos) overlap tokens further ahead.
          // Note: status 'home' pawns are effectively at pos -1.
          drawOrder.sort((a, b) => b.pos - a.pos);

          if (!isRotating) {
            for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < 5; pIdx++) {
              const path = getPawnPath(pIdx);
              gameState.pawns[pIdx].forEach((p, iIdx) => {
                if (p.status === 'on-path' && p.displayPos !== undefined) {
                  const idx = Math.round(p.displayPos);
                  if (idx >= 0 && idx < path.length) {
                    const sq = path[idx];
                    const key = `${sq.arm}-${sq.c}-${sq.r}`;
                    if (!occupancy.has(key)) occupancy.set(key, []);
                    occupancy.get(key).push({ pIdx, iIdx });
                  }
                }
              });
            }
          }

          drawOrder.forEach(({ pIdx, iIdx }) => {
            const pawn = gameState.pawns[pIdx][iIdx];

              if (pawn.displayPos === undefined) pawn.displayPos = pawn.pos;
              const diff = pawn.pos - pawn.displayPos;
              if (Math.abs(diff) > 0.001) {
                  if (diff > 0) pawn.displayPos = Math.min(pawn.pos, pawn.displayPos + moveSpeed);
                  else pawn.displayPos = Math.max(pawn.pos, pawn.displayPos - moveSpeed * 3); 
              } else {
                  pawn.displayPos = pawn.pos;
              }

              let target;
              if (pawn.displayPos < 0) {
                const basePos = getBasePawnPos(pIdx, iIdx);
                const path = getPawnPath(pIdx);
                const firstSquareData = path[0];
                const firstSquare = getSpaceCoords(firstSquareData.arm, firstSquareData.c, firstSquareData.r);
                const t = pawn.displayPos + 1; 
                target = {
                  x: basePos.x + (firstSquare.x - basePos.x) * t,
                  y: basePos.y + (firstSquare.y - basePos.y) * t
                };
              } else {
                const path = getPawnPath(pIdx);
                const idx1 = Math.floor(pawn.displayPos);
                const idx2 = Math.min(path.length - 1, Math.ceil(pawn.displayPos));
                const t = pawn.displayPos - idx1;
                const p1 = path[idx1];
                const p2 = path[idx2];
                const pos1 = getSpaceCoords(p1.arm, p1.c, p1.r);
                const pos2 = getSpaceCoords(p2.arm, p2.c, p2.r);
                target = {
                  x: pos1.x + (pos2.x - pos1.x) * t,
                  y: pos1.y + (pos2.y - pos1.y) * t
                };
              }

              let stackX = 0, stackY = 0;
              if (pawn.status === 'on-path' && !isRotating) {
                const path = getPawnPath(pIdx);
                const idx = Math.round(pawn.displayPos);
                if (idx >= 0 && idx < path.length) {
                  const sq = path[idx];
                  const key = `${sq.arm}-${sq.c}-${sq.r}`;
                  const shared = occupancy.get(key);
                  if (shared && shared.length > 1) {
                    const stackIdx = shared.findIndex(s => s.pIdx === pIdx && s.iIdx === iIdx);
                    if (stackIdx !== -1) {
                      const angle = (stackIdx / shared.length) * Math.PI * 2;
                      const dist = ss * 0.25 * ps;
                      stackX = Math.cos(angle) * dist;
                      stackY = Math.sin(angle) * dist;
                    }
                  }
                }
              }

              if (isRotating || pawn.renderX === 0) {
                pawn.renderX = target.x + stackX;
                pawn.renderY = target.y + stackY;
              } else {
                pawn.renderX += (target.x + stackX - pawn.renderX) * 0.25;
                pawn.renderY += (target.y + stackY - pawn.renderY) * 0.25;
              }

              ctx.save();
              ctx.translate(pawn.renderX, pawn.renderY);

              // Draw highlight ring if pawn is moveable by human player
              if (activeSteps !== null && pIdx === gameState.currentPlayer && canMovePawn(pIdx, iIdx, activeSteps)) {
                  ctx.save();
                  ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
                  ctx.lineWidth = 4 * ps;
                  ctx.shadowBlur = 15 * ps;
                  ctx.shadowColor = '#ffffff';
                  const ringRadius = (ss * 0.6 * ps) + Math.sin(Date.now() / 200) * 5 * ps;
                  ctx.beginPath();
                  ctx.ellipse(0, 5 * ps, ringRadius, ringRadius * 0.6, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                  ctx.stroke();
                  ctx.restore();
              }

              // Draw shadow at the base
              ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';
              ctx.beginPath();
              ctx.ellipse(0, 5 * ps, 15 * ps, 8 * ps, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
              ctx.fill();

              let bounce = 0;
              if (
                gameState.currentPlayer === pIdx &&
                gameState.hasRolled &&
                !gameState.isRolling &&
                gameState.movesAvailable.length > 0
              ) {
                bounce = Math.abs(Math.sin(Date.now() / 150)) * 25 * ps;
              }

              const img = assetCache[`pawn_${pIdx}`];
              if (img && img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
                const pw = ss * (layout.pawnScale || 1.15) * ps; // Scale pawn width relative to space size
                const ph = pw * (img.naturalHeight / img.naturalWidth);
                // Pawns are drawn upright (billboarded) for better visibility
                ctx.drawImage(img, -pw / 2, -ph + 8 * ps - bounce, pw, ph);
              } else {
                // Fallback vector pawn
                ctx.rotate(getArmAngle(pIdx));
                ctx.translate(0, -bounce);
                ctx.fillStyle = getPlayerColor(pIdx);
                ctx.strokeStyle = '#000';
                ctx.lineWidth = 2 * ps;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.moveTo(-12 * ps, 10 * ps);
                ctx.lineTo(12 * ps, 10 * ps);
                ctx.lineTo(8 * ps, -15 * ps);
                ctx.lineTo(-8 * ps, -15 * ps);
                ctx.closePath();
                ctx.fill();
                ctx.stroke();
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.arc(0, -20 * ps, 10 * ps, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                ctx.fill();
                ctx.stroke();
              }
            ctx.restore();
          });
        }

        function getMousePos(e) {
          const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
          const clientX = e.clientX ?? (e.touches && e.touches[0].clientX);
          const clientY = e.clientY ?? (e.touches && e.touches[0].clientY);

          // Detect whether the game container actually has a 90deg CSS rotation
          // applied right now, rather than trusting the isLandscape flag alone.
          // (The mobile build's landscape TOGGLE really does rotate the canvas;
          // the web/laptop build renders true landscape with no rotation at all,
          // so the old assumption that isLandscape always implies a 90deg
          // rotation broke click/tap hit-testing there.)
          let rotated90 = false;
          const containerEl = document.getElementById('game-container');
          const cs = containerEl && window.getComputedStyle(containerEl).transform;
          if (cs && cs !== 'none') {
            const mm = cs.match(/matrix\(([^)]+)\)/);
            if (mm) {
              const v = mm[1].split(',').map(Number);
              const angle = Math.round(Math.atan2(v[1], v[0]) * 180 / Math.PI);
              rotated90 = Math.abs(Math.abs(angle) - 90) < 1;
            }
          }

          let x, y;
          if (rotated90) {
            x = (clientY - rect.top) * (canvas.width / rect.height);
            y = (rect.width - (clientX - rect.left)) * (canvas.height / rect.width);
          } else {
            x = (clientX - rect.left) * (canvas.width / rect.width);
            y = (clientY - rect.top) * (canvas.height / rect.height);
          }
          const dx = x - layout.x;
          const dy = (y - layout.y) / (layout.tilt || 1.0);
          return { x: layout.x + dx, y: layout.y + dy, rawX: x, rawY: y };
        }

        function openEditMenu(type, index) {
          if (type === 'rollHint') {
            lib.editMenu.open({
              name: 'Turn Indicator',
              params: {
                'X': { key: 'layout.rollHintX', type: 'number' },
                'Y': { key: 'layout.rollHintY', type: 'number' },
                'Font Size': { key: 'layout.rollHintFontSize', type: 'slider', min: 10, max: 100 },
              }
            });
          } else if (type === 'adBanner') {
            lib.editMenu.open({
              name: 'Ad Section',
              params: {
                'X': { key: 'layout.adBannerX', type: 'number' },
                'Y': { key: 'layout.adBannerY', type: 'number' },
                'Width': { key: 'layout.adBannerWidth', type: 'number' },
