                'Height': { key: 'layout.adBannerHeight', type: 'number' },
              }
            });
          } else if (type === 'diceHud') {
            lib.editMenu.open({
              name: 'Dice Results Display',
              params: {
                'X': { key: 'layout.diceHudX', type: 'number' },
                'Y': { key: 'layout.diceHudY', type: 'number' },
                'Dice Size': { key: 'layout.diceHudSize', type: 'slider', min: 40, max: 150 },
                '3D Dice Y Offset': { key: 'layout.diceYOffset', type: 'slider', min: -1000, max: 1000 },
              }
            });
          } else if (type === 'path') {
            lib.editMenu.open({
              name: `Path - ${PLAYER_NAMES[index]}`,
              params: {
                'X Adj': { key: `players.${index}.xAdj`, type: 'number' },
                'Y Adj': { key: `players.${index}.yAdj`, type: 'number' },
                'Angle Adj': {
                  key: `players.${index}.angleAdj`,
                  type: 'slider',
                  min: -180,
                  max: 180,
                },
                'Radius Adj': {
                  key: `players.${index}.radiusAdj`,
                  type: 'slider',
                  min: -200,
                  max: 200,
                },
                'Space Width': {
                  key: `players.${index}.widthAdj`,
                  type: 'slider',
                  min: 0.5,
                  max: 2.0,
                  step: 0.05,
                },
                'Space Height': {
                  key: `players.${index}.spaceHeightScale`,
                  type: 'slider',
                  min: 0.5,
                  max: 2.0,
                  step: 0.05,
                },
              },
            });
          } else if (type === 'base') {
            lib.editMenu.open({
              name: `Home Base - ${PLAYER_NAMES[index]}`,
              params: {
                'Base X Adj': { key: `players.${index}.baseXAdj`, type: 'number' },
                'Base Y Adj': { key: `players.${index}.baseYAdj`, type: 'number' },
                'Base Rotation': {
                  key: `players.${index}.baseRotation`,
                  type: 'slider',
                  min: -360,
                  max: 360,
                  step: 1,
                },
                'Base Width': {
                  key: `players.${index}.baseWidthScale`,
                  type: 'slider',
                  min: 0.1,
                  max: 4.0,
                  step: 0.05,
                },
                'Base Height': {
                  key: `players.${index}.baseHeightScale`,
                  type: 'slider',
                  min: 0.1,
                  max: 4.0,
                  step: 0.05,
                },
                'Base Opacity': {
                  key: `players.${index}.baseOpacity`,
                  type: 'slider',
                  min: 0,
                  max: 1.0,
                  step: 0.05,
                },
              },
            });
          } else if (type === 'winPath') {
            lib.editMenu.open({
              name: `Home Path - ${PLAYER_NAMES[index]}`,
              params: {
                'Win Path X': { key: `players.${index}.winPathXAdj`, type: 'number' },
                'Win Path Y': { key: `players.${index}.winPathYAdj`, type: 'number' },
              },
            });
          } else if (type === 'winHome') {
            lib.editMenu.open({
              name: `Win Home - ${PLAYER_NAMES[index]}`,
              params: {
                'Win X Adj': { key: `players.${index}.winXAdj`, type: 'number' },
                'Win Y Adj': { key: `players.${index}.winYAdj`, type: 'number' },
              },
            });
          }
        }

        if (window._ludoCanvasDown) canvas.removeEventListener('pointerdown', window._ludoCanvasDown);
        window._ludoCanvasDown = (e) => {
          if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume();
          // Only block interaction if a major menu is visible (guide or tournament)
          // Notification rules like "Lucky 7" are now click-through.
          if (guidePopup.classList.contains('visible') || tournamentPopup.classList.contains('visible')) return;
          
          if (!soundBuffers['freesound_community-dice-95077.mp3']) loadSound('freesound_community-dice-95077.mp3');
          const m = getMousePos(e);
          if (mode === 'play') {
            // Dice Click Detection — checked BEFORE the nameplate zones so that,
            // in landscape layout, a player's nameplate hit-circle (which can
            // geometrically overlap the fixed on-screen dice position) never
            // swallows a click intended to roll the dice.
            // In an online room, only the client that owns the current seat may act.
            const canAct = !window.__roomSystem || !window.__roomSystem.isConnected() || window.__roomSystem.isMySeat(gameState.currentPlayer);
            if (canAct && !gameState.hasRolled && !gameState.isRolling && !gameState.isSettling) {
              const isLandscape = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape;
              const isDiceClick = [0, 1].some((i) => {
                const d = gameState.dice3D[i];
                const ds = layout.diceScale || 1.0;
                const spacing = 60 * ds;
                const dy = isLandscape ? (610 - (d.hoverOffset || 0)) : (layout.y + layout.diceYOffset - (d.hoverOffset || 0));
                const dx = isLandscape ? (830 + (i === 0 ? -spacing : spacing)) : (layout.x + (i === 0 ? -spacing : spacing));
                const hitRadius = 120 * ds;
                return Math.hypot(m.rawX - dx, m.rawY - dy) < hitRadius;
              });
              if (isDiceClick) {
                if (window.__roomSystem && window.__roomSystem.isConnected() && !window.__roomSystem.isHost()) {
                  window.__roomSystem.submitAction({ kind: 'roll', player: gameState.currentPlayer });
                } else rollDice();
                return;
              }
            }

            // Name Plate Click Detection
            const ps = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape ? 1.12 : (layout.scale || 1.0);
            const ss = layout.spaceSize;
            for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
                const p = window.gameConfig.players[i];
                const baseCenter = getCenterOfBase(i);
                const armAngle = getArmAngle(i);
                const totalBaseRot = armAngle + ((p.baseRotation || 0) * Math.PI) / 180;
                
                const baseHeight = 5 * ss * ps * (p.baseHeightScale || 1.0);
                const plateHeight = 40 * ps;
                const plateX = -baseHeight / 2 - plateHeight / 2 - 15 * ps;
                
                const wx = plateX * Math.cos(totalBaseRot);
                const wy = plateX * Math.sin(totalBaseRot);
                
                const plateCenterX = baseCenter.x + wx;
                const plateCenterY = baseCenter.y + wy;
                
                if (Math.hypot(m.x - plateCenterX, m.y - plateCenterY) < 60 * ps) {
                    openPlayerProfile(i);
                    return;
                }
            }

            if (!gameState.hasRolled && !gameState.isRolling && !gameState.isSettling) {
              // (dice check already handled above)
            } else if (canAct && gameState.hasRolled && gameState.movesAvailable.length > 0 && !gameState.isRolling && !gameState.isSettling) {
              const currentPlayerPawns = gameState.pawns[gameState.currentPlayer];
              for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < currentPlayerPawns.length; pIdx++) {
                const pawn = currentPlayerPawns[pIdx];
                // IMPORTANT: hit-test the pawn where it is ACTUALLY rendered.
                // The old code used the logical square coordinates, which can differ
                // from renderX/renderY because of tilt, board rotation and smooth pawn
                // interpolation. That made a visibly bouncing pawn impossible to tap.
                const effectiveScale = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape ? 1.12 : (layout.scale || 1.0);
                const hitX = (typeof pawn.renderX === 'number' && Number.isFinite(pawn.renderX)) ? pawn.renderX : getBasePawnPos(gameState.currentPlayer, pIdx).x;
                const hitY = (typeof pawn.renderY === 'number' && Number.isFinite(pawn.renderY)) ? pawn.renderY : getBasePawnPos(gameState.currentPlayer, pIdx).y;
                const hitRadius = Math.max(65, 52 * effectiveScale);
                if (Math.hypot(m.x - hitX, m.y - hitY) < hitRadius) {
                  let stepsToUse = null;
                  let usedIndex = -1;

                  // Resolve the die for the pawn that was actually tapped.
                  // A selected die may be legal for another pawn but illegal for
                  // this one; in that case automatically use the first legal die.
                  const selectedIndex = gameState.selectedMoveIndex;
                  if (selectedIndex !== null &&
                      gameState.movesAvailable[selectedIndex] !== undefined &&
                      canMovePawn(gameState.currentPlayer, pIdx, gameState.movesAvailable[selectedIndex])) {
                    usedIndex = selectedIndex;
                    stepsToUse = gameState.movesAvailable[selectedIndex];
                  } else {
                    const fallbackIndex = gameState.movesAvailable.findIndex(steps =>
                      canMovePawn(gameState.currentPlayer, pIdx, steps)
                    );
                    if (fallbackIndex >= 0) {
                      usedIndex = fallbackIndex;
                      stepsToUse = gameState.movesAvailable[fallbackIndex];
                      gameState.selectedMoveIndex = fallbackIndex;
                    }
                  }
                  if (stepsToUse !== null) {
                  if (window.__roomSystem && window.__roomSystem.isConnected() && !window.__roomSystem.isHost()) {
                    window.__roomSystem.submitAction({ kind: 'move', player: gameState.currentPlayer, pawn: pIdx, dieIndex: usedIndex });
                    return;
                  }
                  const possibleCaptures = getPossibleCaptures(gameState.currentPlayer, gameState.movesAvailable);
                  const isMovePartOfCapture = possibleCaptures.some(c => c.pawnIdx === pIdx && c.diceIndices[0] === usedIndex);
                  
                  const startPos = gameState.pawns[gameState.currentPlayer][pIdx].pos;

                  if (movePawn(gameState.currentPlayer, pIdx, stepsToUse)) {
                    if (!isMovePartOfCapture && possibleCaptures.length > 0) {
                        let penalizedAny = false;
                        const uniquePawnIndices = [...new Set(possibleCaptures.map(c => c.pawnIdx))];
                        uniquePawnIndices.forEach(pI => {
                            const p = gameState.pawns[gameState.currentPlayer][pI];
                            if (p.status === 'on-path') {
                                p.status = 'home';
                                p.pos = -1;
                                penalizedAny = true;
                            }
                        });
                        if (penalizedAny) {
                              gameState.pendingCaptureRule = true;
                              const capTitle = t('rule_capture_title');
                              const capSub = t('missed_capture_desc');
                              rollHint.innerHTML = `<div style='color:#ff4d4d; font-size: 0.9em;'>${capTitle}</div><div style='color:#fff; font-size: 0.7em;'>${capSub}</div>`;
                              rollHint.classList.add('visible');
                              setTimeout(() => { if(rollHint.innerText.includes(capTitle.substring(0,5))) rollHint.classList.remove('visible'); }, 2000);
                          }
                      } else if (isMovePartOfCapture && possibleCaptures.length > 0) {
                          // Check for stack split sacrifice
                          applySplitSacrifice(gameState.currentPlayer, pIdx, startPos, possibleCaptures, usedIndex);
                      }
                      gameState.movesAvailable.splice(usedIndex, 1);
                      gameState.selectedMoveIndex = null;
                      updateDiceHud();
                      if (gameState.movesAvailable.length === 0) {
                        gameState.pendingNextTurn = true;
                      } else if (!canAnyPawnMove(gameState.currentPlayer, gameState.movesAvailable)) {
                        rollHint.innerText = t('no_moves');
                        gameState.isWaitingToPass = true;
                        gameState.autoTurnTimer = 1.0;
                      } else {
                        rollHint.innerText = t('die_select');
                      }
                      break;
                    }
                  } else {
                    rollHint.innerText = t('die_select');
                  }
                }
              }
            }
            return;
          }
          if (mode === 'edit') {
            if (Math.hypot(m.rawX - window.gameConfig.layout.rollHintX, m.rawY - window.gameConfig.layout.rollHintY) < 60) {
              gameState.selectedItem = { type: 'rollHint' };
            } else if (Math.hypot(m.rawX - window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudX, m.rawY - window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudY) < 100) {
              gameState.selectedItem = { type: 'diceHud' };
            } else if (Math.abs(m.rawX - window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerX) < window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerWidth / 2 &&
                       Math.abs(m.rawY - window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerY) < window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerHeight / 2) {
              gameState.selectedItem = { type: 'adBanner' };
            }
          }
          if (gameState.selectedItem) {
            gameState.dragTarget = gameState.selectedItem;
            gameState.dragStartPos = { x: m.x, y: m.y };
            gameState.dragStartRaw = { x: m.rawX, y: m.rawY };
            if (gameState.selectedItem.type === 'rollHint') openEditMenu('rollHint');
            else if (gameState.selectedItem.type === 'diceHud') openEditMenu('diceHud');
            else if (gameState.selectedItem.type === 'path') {
              const p = window.gameConfig.players[gameState.selectedItem.index];
              gameState.dragInitialProps = { x: p.xAdj || 0, y: p.yAdj || 0 };
              openEditMenu('path', gameState.selectedItem.index);
            } else if (gameState.selectedItem.type === 'base') {
              const p = window.gameConfig.players[gameState.selectedItem.index];
              gameState.dragInitialProps = { x: p.baseXAdj || 0, y: p.baseYAdj || 0 };
              openEditMenu('base', gameState.selectedItem.index);
            } else if (gameState.selectedItem.type === 'winPath') {
              const p = window.gameConfig.players[gameState.selectedItem.index];
              gameState.dragInitialProps = { x: p.winPathXAdj || 0, y: p.winPathYAdj || 0 };
              openEditMenu('winPath', gameState.selectedItem.index);
            } else if (gameState.selectedItem.type === 'winHome') {
              const p = window.gameConfig.players[gameState.selectedItem.index];
              gameState.dragInitialProps = { x: p.winXAdj || 0, y: p.winYAdj || 0 };
              openEditMenu('winHome', gameState.selectedItem.index);
            } else if (gameState.selectedItem.type === 'center') {
              gameState.dragInitialProps = { x: layout.x, y: layout.y };
            } else if (gameState.selectedItem.type === 'background') {
              gameState.dragInitialProps = { x: layout.bgX, y: layout.bgY };
            }
          } else {
            lib.editMenu.close();
          }
        };
        canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', window._ludoCanvasDown);

        if (window._ludoCanvasMove) canvas.removeEventListener('pointermove', window._ludoCanvasMove);
        window._ludoCanvasMove = (e) => {
          if (!gameState.dragTarget || mode !== 'edit') return;
          const m = getMousePos(e);
          const item = gameState.dragTarget;
          const effectiveScale = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape ? 1.12 : (layout.scale || 1.0);
          const dx = (m.x - gameState.dragStartPos.x) / effectiveScale;
          const dy = (m.y - gameState.dragStartPos.y) / effectiveScale;
          if (item.type === 'path' || item.type === 'base' || item.type === 'winPath' || item.type === 'winHome') {
            const p = window.gameConfig.players[item.index];
            const angle = getArmAngle(item.index);
            const s = Math.sin(angle);
            const c = Math.cos(angle);
            const localDX = dx * c + dy * s;
            const localDY = -dx * s + dy * c;
            if (item.type === 'path') {
              p.xAdj = gameState.dragInitialProps.x + localDX;
              p.yAdj = gameState.dragInitialProps.y + localDY;
            } else if (item.type === 'base') {
              p.baseXAdj = gameState.dragInitialProps.x + localDX;
              p.baseYAdj = gameState.dragInitialProps.y + localDY;
            } else if (item.type === 'winPath') {
              p.winPathXAdj = gameState.dragInitialProps.x + localDX;
              p.winPathYAdj = gameState.dragInitialProps.y + localDY;
            } else if (item.type === 'winHome') {
              p.winXAdj = gameState.dragInitialProps.x + localDX;
              p.winYAdj = gameState.dragInitialProps.y + localDY;
            }
          } else if (item.type === 'rollHint') {
            window.gameConfig.layout.rollHintX = m.rawX;
            window.gameConfig.layout.rollHintY = m.rawY;
          } else if (item.type === 'adBanner') {
            window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerX = m.rawX;
            window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerY = m.rawY;
          } else if (item.type === 'diceHud') {
            window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudX = m.rawX;
            window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudY = m.rawY;
            window.gameConfig.layout.diceYOffset = m.rawY - layout.y;
          } else if (item.type === 'center') {
            layout.x = gameState.dragInitialProps.x + (m.rawX - gameState.dragStartRaw.x);
            layout.y = gameState.dragInitialProps.y + (m.rawY - gameState.dragStartRaw.y);
          } else if (item.type === 'background') {
            layout.bgX = gameState.dragInitialProps.x + (m.rawX - gameState.dragStartRaw.x);
            layout.bgY = gameState.dragInitialProps.y + (m.rawY - gameState.dragStartRaw.y);
          }
        };
        canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', window._ludoCanvasMove);

        if (window._ludoCanvasUp) canvas.removeEventListener('pointerup', window._ludoCanvasUp);
        window._ludoCanvasUp = () => { gameState.dragTarget = null; };
        canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', window._ludoCanvasUp);

        if (window._ludoCanvasHover) canvas.removeEventListener('mousemove', window._ludoCanvasHover);
        window._ludoCanvasHover = (e) => {
          const m = getMousePos(e);
          let isHovering = false;
          if (mode === 'play') {
            if (!gameState.hasRolled && !gameState.isRolling && !gameState.isSettling) {
              const isLandscape = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape;
              isHovering = [0, 1].some((i) => {
                const d = gameState.dice3D[i];
                const ds = layout.diceScale || 1.0;
                const spacing = 60 * ds;
                const dy = isLandscape ? (610 - (d.hoverOffset || 0)) : (layout.y + layout.diceYOffset - (d.hoverOffset || 0));
                const dx = isLandscape ? (830 + (i === 0 ? -spacing : spacing)) : (layout.x + (i === 0 ? -spacing : spacing));
                const hitRadius = 120 * ds;
                return Math.hypot(m.rawX - dx, m.rawY - dy) < hitRadius;
              });
            }
          }
          canvas.style.cursor = isHovering ? 'pointer' : 'default';
        };
        canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', window._ludoCanvasHover);

        function updateAIJudge() {
          const judgeBar = document.getElementById('ai-judge-bar');
          const rankingBar = document.getElementById('ranking-bar');
          if (!judgeBar) return;

          if (gameState.isTournament) {
            judgeBar.classList.add('tournament');
            rankingBar.classList.add('tournament');
          } else {
            judgeBar.classList.remove('tournament');
            rankingBar.classList.remove('tournament');
          }

          const progressValues = [];
          let totalSum = 0;

          for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            const path = getPawnPath(i);
            const pathLen = path.length;
            let playerProgress = 0;

            if (gameState.pawns[i]) {
              gameState.pawns[i].forEach((p) => {
                if (p.status === 'on-path') playerProgress += p.pos + 1;
                else if (p.status === 'finished') playerProgress += pathLen;
              });
            }

            const percentage = (playerProgress / (4 * pathLen)) * 100;
            progressValues.push(percentage);
            totalSum += percentage;
          }

          const reactionEmojis = ['😂', '❤️', '🔥', '😡', '👍'];

          const sortedProgress = progressValues.map((val, idx) => ({ val, idx }))
            .sort((a, b) => b.val - a.val)
            .slice(0, 3);

          for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            const seg = document.getElementById(`judge-${i}`);
            if (seg) {
              const rankItem = sortedProgress.find(p => p.idx === i);
              if (!rankItem) {
                seg.style.display = 'none';
                continue;
              }
              seg.style.display = 'flex';
              const val = Math.round(rankItem.val);
              const pIdx = rankItem.idx;
              const pConfig = window.gameConfig.players[pIdx];
              const playerColor = pConfig ? pConfig.color : getPlayerColor(pIdx);
              const playerName = pConfig ? pConfig.name : `P${pIdx+1}`;
              
              seg.style.backgroundColor = playerColor;
              const width = totalSum > 1 ? (rankItem.val / totalSum) * 100 : 33.3;
              seg.style.width = `${width}%`;
              
              seg.style.opacity = '1';
              
              if (!seg.querySelector('.emoji-tray')) {
                  const isAI = pConfig ? pConfig.isAI : false;
                  seg.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="judge-name"></div>
                    <div class="emoji-tray">
                        <span class="voice-icon" style="cursor:pointer; margin-right:4px; opacity:0.8; font-size:12px;">${isAI ? '📡' : '🎤'}</span>
                        <span class="chat-trigger-btn" style="cursor:pointer; margin-right:4px; opacity:0.8;">💬</span>
                        ${reactionEmojis.map(e => `<span class="react-btn" data-emoji="${e}">${e}</span>`).join('')}
                    </div>
                  `;
                  const btns = seg.querySelectorAll('.react-btn');
                  btns.forEach(btn => {
                      btn.onclick = (e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          const m = getMousePos(e);
                          spawnReaction(pIdx, btn.dataset.emoji, m.rawX, m.rawY);
                      };
                  });

                  const chatTrigger = seg.querySelector('.chat-trigger-btn');
                  chatTrigger.onclick = (e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      openPlayerProfile(pIdx);
                  };
              }

              const nameDiv = seg.querySelector('.judge-name');
              const trayDiv = seg.querySelector('.emoji-tray');
              
              const shortName = playerName.split(' ')[0].substring(0, 8);
              nameDiv.innerText = `${shortName}: ${val}%`;
              trayDiv.style.display = 'flex';
              trayDiv.style.fontSize = width < 20 ? '10px' : '14px';
            }
          }
        }

        function updateHandPointer() {
          const hand = document.getElementById('hand-pointer');
          if (!hand) return;

          // Only show for human players in play mode
          const currentPlayer = window.gameConfig.players[gameState.currentPlayer];
          if (mode !== 'play' || (currentPlayer && currentPlayer.isAI) || !gameState.gameStarted || gameState.isRuleActive) {
            hand.style.display = 'none';
            return;
          }

          let targetX = -100;
          let targetY = -100;
          let show = false;

          if (!gameState.hasRolled && !gameState.isRolling && !gameState.isSettling) {
            // Point to dice area
            const isLandscape = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape;
            const dy = isLandscape ? 610 : (layout.y + (layout.diceYOffset || 500));
            const dx = isLandscape ? 830 : layout.x;
            targetX = dx;
            targetY = dy;
            show = true;
          } else if (gameState.hasRolled && gameState.movesAvailable.length > 0 && !gameState.isRolling && !gameState.isSettling) {
            if (gameState.movesAvailable.length > 1 && gameState.selectedMoveIndex === null) {
              // Point to dice HUD to select a die
              targetX = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape ? 830 : window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudX;
              targetY = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape ? 610 : window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudY;
              show = true;
            } else {
              // Point to moveable pawns
              let steps = gameState.selectedMoveIndex !== null ? gameState.movesAvailable[gameState.selectedMoveIndex] : gameState.movesAvailable[0];
              const pIdx = gameState.currentPlayer;
              const moveablePawns = [];
              if (gameState.pawns[pIdx]) {
                gameState.pawns[pIdx].forEach((p, i) => {
                  if (canMovePawn(pIdx, i, steps)) {
                    moveablePawns.push(p);
                  }
                });
              }

              if (moveablePawns.length === 1) {
                // Focus on the only available pawn that can move
                const p = moveablePawns[0];
                targetX = p.renderX;
                targetY = p.renderY;
                show = true;
              } else if (moveablePawns.length > 1) {
                // Point back to dice area to indicate choice is needed among highlighted tokens
                const isLandscape = window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape;
                targetX = isLandscape ? 830 : window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudX;
                targetY = isLandscape ? 610 : window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudY;
                show = true;
              }
            }
          }

          if (show) {
            hand.style.display = 'block';
            hand.style.left = targetX + 'px';
            // Adjust pointer Y based on dice hover for precision
            const dIdx = (gameState.hasRolled && gameState.selectedMoveIndex !== null) ? gameState.selectedMoveIndex : 0;
            const hOff = gameState.dice3D[dIdx] ? (gameState.dice3D[dIdx].hoverOffset || 0) : 0;
            hand.style.top = (targetY - 50 - hOff) + 'px';
          } else {
            hand.style.display = 'none';
          }
        }

        function animateInner() {
          gameState.frameCount++;
          ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
          
          const rot = getCurrentRotation();
          
          // 1. Perspective Transformation Block
          ctx.save();
          ctx.translate(layout.x, layout.y);
          ctx.scale(1, layout.tilt || 1.0);
          ctx.translate(-layout.x, -layout.y);
          
          // 2. Draw Tilted Gradient (Floor) - only every frame for simplicity, but cached
          if (!gameState.cachedGradient) {
            gameState.cachedGradient = ctx.createRadialGradient(
              layout.x,
              layout.y,
              0,
              layout.x,
              layout.y,
              Math.max(canvas.width, canvas.height)
            );
            gameState.cachedGradient.addColorStop(0, '#2c3e50');
            gameState.cachedGradient.addColorStop(1, '#000000');
          }
          ctx.fillStyle = gameState.cachedGradient;
          ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

          // 3. Draw Rotated & Tilted Background Image (skip rotation calc if not needed)
          let currentBgId = layout.backgroundId || 'none';
          if (mode === 'play' && gameState.isTournament) {
              currentBgId = layout.tournamentBackgroundId || 'bg_neon_grid';
          }
          const bgImg = assetCache[currentBgId];

          if (bgImg && bgImg.complete) {
            ctx.save();
            ctx.translate(layout.x, layout.y);
            ctx.rotate((rot * Math.PI) / 180);
            ctx.translate(-layout.x, -layout.y);

            const ar = bgImg.width / bgImg.height;
            const bgScaleFactor = 2.5; 
            let drawH = canvas.height * bgScaleFactor;
            let drawW = canvas.height * bgScaleFactor * ar;
            if (drawW < canvas.width * bgScaleFactor) {
              drawW = canvas.width * bgScaleFactor;
              drawH = (canvas.width * bgScaleFactor) / ar;
            }
            ctx.drawImage(bgImg, (canvas.width - drawW) / 2, (canvas.height - drawH) / 2, drawW, drawH);
            
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)';
            ctx.fillRect((canvas.width - drawW) / 2, (canvas.height - drawH) / 2, drawW, drawH);
            ctx.restore();
          }

          // 4. Edit mode cover image (tilted & rotated)
          if (mode === 'edit' && assetCache.cover) {
            ctx.save();
            ctx.globalAlpha = layout.bgOpacity;
            ctx.translate(layout.bgX, layout.bgY);
            ctx.rotate(((layout.bgRotation || 0) * Math.PI) / 180);
            ctx.scale(layout.bgScale, layout.bgScale);
            const img = assetCache.cover;
            const ar = img.width / img.height;
            const h = 1280;
            const w = h * ar;
            ctx.drawImage(img, -w / 2, -h / 2, w, h);
            ctx.restore();
          }
          
          if (mode === 'play') {
            gameState.rotationOffset += (gameState.targetRotation - gameState.rotationOffset) * 0.05;
          }

          // 5. Draw Board & Pawns (Internally handle rotation)
          drawBoard();
          drawPawns();
          ctx.restore();

          // 6. Draw 3D Dice (Drawn in screen space but tilted Y positioning)
          drawDice();
          drawReactions();

          // Update and Draw Speech Balloons
          for (let i = gameState.speechBalloons.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const b = gameState.speechBalloons[i];
            b.timer -= 0.016;
            if (b.timer <= 0) {
              if (b.el.parentNode) b.el.parentNode.removeChild(b.el);
              gameState.speechBalloons.splice(i, 1);
              continue;
            }
            if (b.timer < 0.5) b.opacity = b.timer / 0.5;
            b.el.style.opacity = b.opacity;

            // Positioning: anchor to player's home base
            const baseCenter = getCenterOfBase(b.playerIdx);
            const anchorY = layout.y + (baseCenter.y - layout.y) * (layout.tilt || 1.0);
            
            b.el.style.left = baseCenter.x + 'px';
            b.el.style.top = (anchorY - 50) + 'px'; 
          }

          // Random AI Banter Loop
          if (gameState.gameStarted && !gameState.isRuleActive) {
            gameState.aiBanterTimer -= 0.016;
            if (gameState.aiBanterTimer <= 0) {
              const aiIndices = window.gameConfig.players.map((p, idx) => p.isAI ? idx : -1).filter(idx => idx !== -1);
              if (aiIndices.length > 0) {
                const randomAI = aiIndices[Math.floor(Math.random() * aiIndices.length)];
                triggerAISpeech(randomAI, 'banter');
              }
              gameState.aiBanterTimer = 15 + Math.random() * 20;
            }
          }

          // Wish Button visibility
          if (mode === 'play' && !gameState.hasRolled && !gameState.isRolling && !gameState.isSettling && !window.gameConfig.players[gameState.currentPlayer].isAI) {
              const p = window.gameConfig.players[gameState.currentPlayer];
              if (p && p.wish && !p.wishUsed) {
                  wishBtn.classList.add('available');
              } else {
                  wishBtn.classList.remove('available');
              }
          } else {
              wishBtn.classList.remove('available');
          }

          // Sync DOM UI elements with gameConfig
          if (rollHint) {
            let x = window.gameConfig.layout.rollHintX;
            let y = window.gameConfig.layout.rollHintY;
            
            if (window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape) {
                // Shifted slightly right to match board alignment
                x = 830;
                y = 680;
            }
            
            rollHint.style.left = x + 'px';
            rollHint.style.top = y + 'px';
            if (window.gameConfig.layout.rollHintFontSize) {
              rollHint.style.fontSize = window.gameConfig.layout.rollHintFontSize + 'px';
            }
          }
          if (diceHud) {
            let x = window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudX;
            let y = window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudY;
            
            if (window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape) {
                // Shifted slightly right to match board alignment
                x = 830;
                y = 610;
            }
            
            diceHud.style.left = x + 'px';
            diceHud.style.top = y + 'px';
            const diceVals = diceHud.querySelectorAll('.dice-val');
            if (window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudSize) {
              diceVals.forEach(dv => {
                dv.style.width = window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudSize + 'px';
                dv.style.height = window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudSize + 'px';
                dv.style.fontSize = (window.gameConfig.layout.diceHudSize * 0.5) + 'px';
              });
            }
          }
          const adBanner = document.getElementById('ad-banner');
          if (adBanner) {
            let x = window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerX;
            let y = window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerY;
            let w = window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerWidth;
            let h = window.gameConfig.layout.adBannerHeight;
            
            if (window.gameConfig.layout.isLandscape) {
                w = 300;
                // Judge bar now sits at top:20, height:70 -> bottom edge at 90.
                // Settings/music buttons sit around bottom:20, height:54 -> top edge at ~646.
                // Leave a clear ~28px gap on each side so the banner never touches either.
                const topMargin = 118;
                const bottomMargin = 100;
                h = 720 - topMargin - bottomMargin; // 502
                x = 1280 - w/2 - 20;
                y = topMargin + h / 2;
            }
            
            adBanner.style.left = x + 'px';
            adBanner.style.top = y + 'px';
            adBanner.style.width = w + 'px';
            adBanner.style.height = h + 'px';
            adBanner.style.opacity = (mode === 'edit' || gameState.hasRolled || gameState.isRolling) ? '1' : '0.8';
          }

          // Update 3D Dice physics
          gameState.dice3D.forEach((d, i) => {
            // Physics should always run if active to allow rolling and snapping
            if (d.active) {
              if (gameState.isRolling) {
                d.x += d.vx;
                d.y += d.vy;
                d.z += d.vz;
                d.vz -= 0.5;

                d.rx += d.vrx;
                d.ry += d.vry;
                d.rz += d.vrz;

                if (d.z < 0) {
                  d.z = 0;
                  d.vz *= -0.4;
                  d.vrx *= 0.8;
                  d.vry *= 0.8;
                  d.vrz *= 0.8;
                }

                if (gameState.rollAnimTime < 30) {
                  const t = 0.15;
                  d.rx += (d.targetRX - d.rx) * t;
                  d.ry += (d.targetRY - d.ry) * t;
                  d.rz += (0 - d.rz) * t;
                  d.x += (d.targetX - d.x) * t;
                  d.y += (d.targetY - d.y) * t;
                }
              } else {
                // Snap to targets once stopped to keep them perfectly still
                d.x = d.targetX;
                d.y = d.targetY;
                d.z = 0;
                d.rx = d.targetRX;
                d.ry = d.targetRY;
                d.rz = 0;
              }
            }
            
            // Pop-up logic for selection (blocked by modal UI)
            const isSelectable = gameState.hasRolled && !gameState.isRolling && !gameState.isSettling && 
                                gameState.movesAvailable.length > 0 && !gameState.isRuleActive;
            const isSelected = isSelectable && gameState.selectedMoveIndex === i;
            const targetOffset = isSelected ? 40 : (isSelectable ? 20 : 0);
            const targetScale = isSelected ? 1.2 : (isSelectable ? 1.1 : 1);
            
            d.hoverOffset += (targetOffset - d.hoverOffset) * 0.15;
            d.hoverScale += (targetScale - d.hoverScale) * 0.15;
          });

          updateAIJudge();
          updateMusic();
          updateHandPointer();

          // Safety check: if isRuleActive is true but no popups are displayed, reset it to prevent soft-locks
          const isRulePopupVisible = rulePopup.classList.contains('visible') || (rulePopup.style.display !== 'none' && rulePopup.style.display !== '');
          const isGuidePopupVisible = guidePopup.classList.contains('visible') || (guidePopup.style.display !== 'none' && guidePopup.style.display !== '');
          const isTournamentPopupVisible = tournamentPopup.classList.contains('visible') || (tournamentPopup.style.display !== 'none' && tournamentPopup.style.display !== '');
          
          if (gameState.isRuleActive && !isRulePopupVisible && !isGuidePopupVisible && !isTournamentPopupVisible) {
              gameState.isRuleActive = false;
          }

          // Failsafe Turn Timeout: If no moves made for 25 seconds after rolling
          if (gameState.gameStarted && gameState.hasRolled && !gameState.isRolling && !gameState.isSettling && !gameState.isWaitingToPass && !gameState.pendingNextTurn && !gameState.isRuleActive &&
              (!window.__roomSystem || !window.__roomSystem.isConnected() || window.__roomSystem.isHost() || window.__roomSystem.isMySeat(gameState.currentPlayer))) {
              if (!gameState.lastMoveTime) gameState.lastMoveTime = Date.now();
              if (Date.now() - gameState.lastMoveTime > 25000) { 
                  lib.log("Turn timeout failsafe triggered - forcing skip");
                  gameState.isWaitingToPass = true;
                  gameState.autoTurnTimer = 0.5;
                  gameState.lastMoveTime = Date.now();
              }
          } else {
              gameState.lastMoveTime = Date.now();
          }

          // Update Turn Timer UI and Logic
          const timerContainer = document.getElementById('turn-timer-container');
          const timerBar = document.getElementById('turn-timer-bar');
          const timerText = document.getElementById('turn-timer-text');
          if (timerContainer && timerBar) {
              if (window.gameConfig.layout.turnTimerEnabled && gameState.gameStarted && !gameState.isRuleActive && !gameState.isWaitingToPass && !gameState.pendingNextTurn &&
                  (!window.__roomSystem || !window.__roomSystem.isConnected() || window.__roomSystem.isHost() || window.__roomSystem.isMySeat(gameState.currentPlayer))) {
                  timerContainer.style.display = 'block';
                  
                  // Decrease timer
                  gameState.turnTimer -= 0.016;
                  if (gameState.turnTimer <= 0) {
                      gameState.turnTimer = 0;
                      lib.log("Turn timer expired - forcing skip");
                      gameState.isWaitingToPass = true;
                      gameState.autoTurnTimer = 0.5;
                  }

                  // Update UI
                  const percent = (gameState.turnTimer / gameState.turnMaxTime) * 100;
                  timerBar.style.width = percent + '%';
                  if (timerText) {
                      timerText.innerText = Math.ceil(gameState.turnTimer) + "s";
                  }
                  
                  // Color stages
                  timerBar.classList.remove('timer-warning', 'timer-danger');
                  timerText.classList.remove('timer-text-danger');
                  if (percent < 25) {
                      timerBar.classList.add('timer-danger');
                      timerText.classList.add('timer-text-danger');
                  } else if (percent < 50) {
                      timerBar.classList.add('timer-warning');
                  }
              } else if (window.gameConfig.layout.turnTimerEnabled && gameState.gameStarted) {
                  // Keep showing but don't count down if waiting for turn transition or rules
                  timerContainer.style.display = 'block';
                  if (timerText) {
                      timerText.innerText = Math.ceil(Math.max(0, gameState.turnTimer)) + "s";
                  }
              } else {
                  timerContainer.style.display = 'none';
              }
          }

          // Rule transitions: Handle turn logic even if a rule is active (visual only)
          if (gameState.pendingNextTurn && arePawnsSettled() &&
              (!window.__roomSystem || !window.__roomSystem.isConnected() || window.__roomSystem.isHost() || window.__roomSystem.isMySeat(gameState.currentPlayer))) {
            if (gameState.rotationDelayTimer <= 0) {
              gameState.rotationDelayTimer = window.gameConfig.layout.rotationDelay || 60;
            }
            gameState.rotationDelayTimer--;
            if (gameState.rotationDelayTimer <= 1) {
              nextTurn();
              gameState.rotationDelayTimer = 0;
            }
          } else if (gameState.pendingNextTurn &&
                     (!window.__roomSystem || !window.__roomSystem.isConnected() || window.__roomSystem.isHost() || window.__roomSystem.isMySeat(gameState.currentPlayer))) {
              // Forced settle if taking too long
              if (!gameState.settleFailsafe) gameState.settleFailsafe = 0;
              gameState.settleFailsafe++;
              if (gameState.settleFailsafe > 180) { // 3 seconds
                  nextTurn();
                  gameState.settleFailsafe = 0;
              }
          } else {
            gameState.rotationDelayTimer = 0;
            gameState.settleFailsafe = 0;
          }

          // We only return early if a major menu is active (Guide or Tournament).
          // Notification rules like "Lucky 7" should not freeze the game loop logic.
          if (isGuidePopupVisible || isTournamentPopupVisible) {
            return;
          }

          if (gameState.isRolling) {
            gameState.rollAnimTime--;

            // Hard timeout: a rendering exception or throttled frame must not
            // leave the dice in the rolling state forever.
            if (gameState.rollAnimTime <= 0 ||
                (gameState.rollStartedAt && Date.now() - gameState.rollStartedAt > 4000)) {
              gameState.isRolling = false;
              gameState.isSettling = true;
              gameState.settleTimer = 12;
              gameState.settleStartedAt = Date.now();
            }
          }

          if (gameState.isSettling) {
            gameState.settleTimer--;
            if (gameState.settleTimer <= 0 ||
                (gameState.settleStartedAt && Date.now() - gameState.settleStartedAt > 1500)) {
              gameState.isSettling = false;
              gameState.hasRolled = true;
              if (window.__roomSystem && window.__roomSystem.isConnected() && window.__roomSystem.isHost()) {
                window.__roomSystem.broadcastState();
              }

              // Store the original dice roll for 3D rendering
              const originalDiceRoll = gameState.dice.slice();

              // Check 7-roll rule condition - priority: Capture > Lucky 7
              let isRuleSeven = false;
              if (gameState.movesAvailable && gameState.movesAvailable.length === 2 && gameState.movesAvailable[0] === 6 && gameState.movesAvailable[1] === 6 && gameState.lucky7UsedThisTurn) {
                isRuleSeven = true;
                gameState.dice = [6, 6];
              } else if (typeof checkAndApplyLucky7 === 'function') {
                isRuleSeven = checkAndApplyLucky7(gameState.currentPlayer, gameState.dice);
              }
              if (!isRuleSeven) {
                if (!gameState.movesAvailable || gameState.movesAvailable.length === 0) {
                  gameState.movesAvailable = gameState.dice.slice();
                }
              }
              
              // Auto-select a playable die so a two-dice roll never leaves the
              // player stuck with highlighted pawns but no active move. The HUD
              // remains clickable and can be used to switch to the other die.
              gameState.selectedMoveIndex = null;
              if (gameState.movesAvailable.length > 1) {
                const firstPlayableDie = gameState.movesAvailable.findIndex(steps =>
                  canAnyPawnMove(gameState.currentPlayer, [steps])
                );
                if (firstPlayableDie >= 0) gameState.selectedMoveIndex = firstPlayableDie;
              }
              
              const isRolledSix = gameState.dice[0] === 6 || gameState.dice[1] === 6;
              if (isRolledSix) {
                gameState.consecutiveSixes[gameState.currentPlayer]++;
              } else {
                gameState.consecutiveSixes[gameState.currentPlayer] = 0;
              }
              
              if (isRuleSeven || (gameState.dice[0] === 6 && gameState.dice[1] === 6))
                gameState.extraTurnEarned = true;
              
              updateDiceHud();

              if (!canAnyPawnMove(gameState.currentPlayer, gameState.movesAvailable)) {
                rollHint.innerText = t('no_moves');
                gameState.isWaitingToPass = true;
                gameState.autoTurnTimer = 1.2;
              } else {
                rollHint.innerText = t('die_select');
                
                // Check if any move can capture
                let capturePossible = false;
                for (const mv of gameState.movesAvailable) {
                  for (let pI = 0; pI < 4; pI++) {
                    if (wouldMoveCapture(gameState.currentPlayer, pI, mv)) {
                      capturePossible = true;
                      break;
                    }
                  }
                  if (capturePossible) break;
                }
                
                if (capturePossible) {
                  playSound('capture_suspense_heartbeat');
                }
              }
            }
          }

          // Handle capture rule popup immediately
          if (gameState.pendingCaptureRule) {
            gameState.pendingCaptureRule = false;
            showRule('capture');
          }
          if (gameState.isWaitingToPass) {
            gameState.autoTurnTimer -= 0.016;
            if (gameState.autoTurnTimer <= 0) {
              rollHint.classList.remove('visible');
              gameState.pendingNextTurn = true;
            }
          }
          
          if (
            mode === 'play' &&
            window.gameConfig.players[gameState.currentPlayer].isAI &&
            (!window.__roomSystem || !window.__roomSystem.isConnected() || window.__roomSystem.isHost()) &&
            !gameState.isWaitingToPass &&
            !gameState.isSettling
          ) {
            gameState.aiThinkTimer -= 0.016;
            if (gameState.aiThinkTimer <= 0) {
              if (!gameState.hasRolled && !gameState.isRolling && !gameState.isSettling) {
                const p = window.gameConfig.players[gameState.currentPlayer];
                // AI uses wish if it has pawns home and hasn't used it yet (30% chance per turn to not be too predictable)
                const hasPawnsHome = gameState.pawns[gameState.currentPlayer].some(pn => pn.status === 'home');
                if (p.wish && !p.wishUsed && hasPawnsHome && Math.random() < 0.3) {
                    rollDice(p.wish);
                    p.wishUsed = true;
                    lib.log(`AI ${p.name} used their Wish Roll!`);
                } else {
                    rollDice();
                }
                gameState.aiThinkTimer = 1.0;
              } else if (gameState.hasRolled && gameState.movesAvailable.length > 0) {
                const moves = gameState.movesAvailable;
                let moved = false;
                
                // 1. Identify all legal moves
                const legalMoves = [];
                for (let mIdx = 0; mIdx < moves.length; mIdx++) {
                  const steps = moves[mIdx];
                  for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < 4; pIdx++) {
                    if (canMovePawn(gameState.currentPlayer, pIdx, steps)) {
                      legalMoves.push({ pawnIdx: pIdx, dieIdx: mIdx, steps });
                    }
                  }
                }

                if (legalMoves.length > 0) {
                  // 2. Prioritize moves
                  let bestMove = null;

                  // Priority 1: Captures (including combinations)
                  const possibleCaptures = getPossibleCaptures(gameState.currentPlayer, moves);
                  if (possibleCaptures.length > 0) {
                      const moveSelection = possibleCaptures[Math.floor(Math.random() * possibleCaptures.length)];
                      bestMove = legalMoves.find(m => m.pawnIdx === moveSelection.pawnIdx && m.dieIdx === moveSelection.diceIndices[0]);
                  }

                  // Priority 2: Bring out from home (only if entry square is safe)
                  if (!bestMove) {
                    const homeMove = legalMoves.find(m => gameState.pawns[gameState.currentPlayer][m.pawnIdx].status === 'home');
                    if (homeMove) {
                      // Only bring out if the entry square is not threatened by opponents
                      if (!isEntrySquareThreatened(gameState.currentPlayer)) {
                        bestMove = homeMove;
                      }
                    }
                  }

                  // Priority 3: Progress existing pawns
                  if (!bestMove) {
                    bestMove = legalMoves.sort((a, b) => {
                      const pawnA = gameState.pawns[gameState.currentPlayer][a.pawnIdx];
                      const pawnB = gameState.pawns[gameState.currentPlayer][b.pawnIdx];
                      return (pawnB.pos || 0) - (pawnA.pos || 0);
                    })[0];
                  }

                  if (bestMove) {
                    // Check for potential captures before moving (for penalty check)
                    const possibleCaptures = getPossibleCaptures(gameState.currentPlayer, moves);
                    const isMovePartOfCapture = possibleCaptures.some(c => c.pawnIdx === bestMove.pawnIdx && c.diceIndices[0] === bestMove.dieIdx);
                    
                    const startPos = gameState.pawns[gameState.currentPlayer][bestMove.pawnIdx].pos;

                    if (movePawn(gameState.currentPlayer, bestMove.pawnIdx, bestMove.steps)) {
                      // Apply penalty if AI missed a capture
                      if (!isMovePartOfCapture && possibleCaptures.length > 0) {
                          let penalizedAny = false;
                          const uniquePawnIndices = [...new Set(possibleCaptures.map(c => c.pawnIdx))];
                          uniquePawnIndices.forEach(pI => {
                              const p = gameState.pawns[gameState.currentPlayer][pI];
                              if (p.status === 'on-path') {
                                  p.status = 'home';
                                  p.pos = -1;
                                  penalizedAny = true;
                              }
                          });
                          if (penalizedAny) {
                              gameState.pendingCaptureRule = true;
                              rollHint.innerHTML = "<div style='color:#ff4d4d; font-size: 0.9em;'>AI MISSED CAPTURE!</div><div style='color:#fff; font-size: 0.7em;'>Penalty applied</div>";
                              rollHint.classList.add('visible');
                          }
                      } else if (isMovePartOfCapture && possibleCaptures.length > 0) {
                          // Check for stack split sacrifice
                          applySplitSacrifice(gameState.currentPlayer, bestMove.pawnIdx, startPos, possibleCaptures, bestMove.dieIdx);
                      }

                      gameState.movesAvailable.splice(bestMove.dieIdx, 1);
                      moved = true;
                      updateDiceHud();
                    }
                  }
                }
                
                if (moved) {
                  if (gameState.movesAvailable.length === 0) {
                    gameState.pendingNextTurn = true;
                  } else if (!canAnyPawnMove(gameState.currentPlayer, gameState.movesAvailable)) {
                    gameState.isWaitingToPass = true;
                    gameState.autoTurnTimer = 1.0;
                  }
                  gameState.aiThinkTimer = 0.6;
                } else {
                  gameState.isWaitingToPass = true;
                  gameState.autoTurnTimer = 1.0;
                }
              }
            }
          }

          // Consecutive 6 counter display removed - unlimited consecutive 6s now allowed in all modes
        }

        // A single bad frame (e.g. malformed data received from a synced online
        // room) must never permanently freeze the game on a blank screen — catch
        // per-frame errors, log them for diagnosis, and always keep the loop alive.
        let __lastShownError = null;
        function showDebugError(prefix, e) {
          const banner = document.getElementById('debug-error-banner');
          const textEl = document.getElementById('debug-error-text');
          if (!banner || !textEl) return;
          const msg = prefix + ': ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : String(e)) +
            (e && e.stack ? '\n' + e.stack.split('\n').slice(0, 4).join('\n') : '');
          if (msg === __lastShownError) return; // don't spam identical repeats every frame
          __lastShownError = msg;
          textEl.textContent = msg;
          banner.style.display = 'block';
        }
        const debugErrorCloseBtn = document.getElementById('debug-error-close');
        if (debugErrorCloseBtn) debugErrorCloseBtn.onclick = () => {
          document.getElementById('debug-error-banner').style.display = 'none';
        };
        window.addEventListener('error', (e) => showDebugError('Uncaught error', e.error || e));

        function animate() {
          try {
            // Turn-state watchdog runs outside the renderer so a single render
            // error can never permanently trap the game after a dice roll.
            if (gameState.isRolling && gameState.rollStartedAt && Date.now() - gameState.rollStartedAt > 5000) {
              gameState.isRolling = false;
              gameState.isSettling = true;
              gameState.settleTimer = 1;
              gameState.settleStartedAt = Date.now();
            }
            if (gameState.isSettling && gameState.settleStartedAt && Date.now() - gameState.settleStartedAt > 2500) {
              gameState.isSettling = false;
              gameState.hasRolled = true;
              gameState.settleTimer = 0;
            }
            animateInner();
          } catch (e) {
            console.error('animate() frame error (recovered):', e);
            showDebugError('Render error (recovered, game keeps running)', e);
          }
          requestAnimationFrame(animate);
        }
        animate();

      }
    

                  // ===== Real online "room" system (Supabase) =====
      // Switched from Firebase to Supabase: same features (host/join a room, live
      // shared chat, synced match state), but running on different infrastructure
      // than Google's — useful if a network specifically blocks Google services.
      (function() {
        // ⚠️ REPLACE THESE TWO VALUES with your own Supabase project's details:
        // Dashboard -> Project Settings -> API -> "Project URL" and "anon public" key.
        const SUPABASE_URL = "https://xiummtvknlfpwvrgchms.supabase.co";
        const SUPABASE_ANON_KEY = "sb_publishable_M2PpkO9SjERJVGlEcdH_5A_fcWWGsvJ";

        function roomToast(msg) {
          const toastEl = document.getElementById('toast');
          const msgEl = document.getElementById('toast-message');
          if (toastEl && msgEl) {
            msgEl.innerText = msg;
            toastEl.classList.add('visible');
            setTimeout(() => toastEl.classList.remove('visible'), 3000);
          } else {
            console.log('[room]', msg);
          }
        }

        let sb = null;
        let sbReady = false;
        function ensureSupabaseReady() {
          if (sbReady) return true;
          try {
            if (typeof supabase === 'undefined' || !SUPABASE_URL || SUPABASE_URL.indexOf('YOUR_SUPABASE') === 0) {
              return false;
            }
            sb = supabase.createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_ANON_KEY);
            sbReady = true;
            return true;
          } catch (e) {
            console.error('Supabase init failed:', e);
            return false;
          }
        }

        function withTimeout(promise, ms, label) {
          return Promise.race([
            promise,
            new Promise((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('timeout: ' + label)), ms))
          ]);
        }

        let roomCode = null;
        let amHost = false;
        let mySlot = 0;
        let seatOwners = {};
        let channel = null;
        let roomSnapshotTimer = null;
        let roomActionTimer = null;
        const localSessionId = 'p_' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);

        function localPlayerName() {
          // Prefer whatever the person actually typed into the room panel's own
          // "Your name" field — every device defaults its local human player to
          // the same generic config name, so without this everyone joining a
          // room from a fresh copy of the game shows up with the same name.
          const nameInput = document.getElementById('room-name-input');
          if (nameInput && nameInput.value.trim()) {
            const typed = nameInput.value.trim().slice(0, 20);
            const human = window.gameConfig.players.find(p => !p.isAI);
            if (human) human.name = typed; // keep nameplate/chat bubbles consistent too
            return typed;
          }
          const human = window.gameConfig.players.find(p => !p.isAI);
          return (human && human.name) || 'Player';
        }

        function genRoomCode() {
          const chars = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';
          let code = '';
          for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) code += chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)];
          return code;
        }

        function isMySeat(i) {
          if (!roomCode) return true;
          if (seatOwners[i] === localSessionId) return true;
          if (amHost && !seatOwners[i]) return true;
          return false;
        }

        const roomPanel = document.getElementById('room-chat-panel');
        const roomToggleBtn = document.getElementById('room-chat-toggle-btn');
        const roomCodeDisplay = document.getElementById('room-code-display');
        const roomPresenceDisplay = document.getElementById('room-presence-display');
        const roomJoinRow = document.getElementById('room-join-row');
        const roomJoinInput = document.getElementById('room-join-input');
        const roomJoinBtn = document.getElementById('room-join-btn');
        const roomHostBtn = document.getElementById('room-host-btn');
        const roomMessagesEl = document.getElementById('room-messages');
        const roomChatInput = document.getElementById('room-chat-input');
        const roomChatSendBtn = document.getElementById('room-chat-send-btn');
        const roomChatCloseBtn = document.getElementById('room-chat-close-btn');

        function appendRoomMessage(msg) {
          const div = document.createElement('div');
          const isMe = msg.sender === localPlayerName();
          div.style.alignSelf = isMe ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start';
          div.style.background = isMe ? 'rgba(46,204,113,0.25)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)';
          div.style.borderRadius = '10px';
          div.style.padding = '6px 10px';
          div.style.maxWidth = '85%';
          const safeSender = (msg.sender || 'Someone').replace(/</g, '&lt;');
          const safeText = (msg.text || '').replace(/</g, '&lt;');
          div.innerHTML = `<strong style="opacity:0.75;">${safeSender}:</strong> ${safeText}`;
          roomMessagesEl.appendChild(div);
          roomMessagesEl.scrollTop = roomMessagesEl.scrollHeight;
        }

        function updateRoomPresenceUI(names) {
          roomPresenceDisplay.innerText = names.length
            ? `${names.length} in room: ${names.join(', ')}`
            : 'Waiting for others to join...';
        }

        function applyMatchSnapshot(row) {
          if (!row) return;
          if (row.seats) {
            seatOwners = {};
            Object.keys(row.seats).forEach(idx => {
              const seat = row.seats[idx];
              seatOwners[idx] = seat.sessionId || null;
              const p = window.gameConfig.players[idx];
              if (p) {
                p.isAI = !!seat.isAI;
                if (seat.name) p.name = seat.name;
                if (typeof seat.gender === 'string') p.gender = seat.gender;
              }
            });
            const mine = Object.keys(row.seats).find(idx => row.seats[idx].sessionId === localSessionId);
            if (mine !== undefined) mySlot = parseInt(mine, 10);
          }
          if (row.started && row.match_state) {
            const ms = row.match_state;
            if (ms.pawns) window.gameState.pawns = ms.pawns;
            if (typeof ms.currentPlayer === 'number') window.gameState.currentPlayer = ms.currentPlayer;
            window.gameState.gameStarted = true;
            window.gameState.hasRolled = !!ms.hasRolled;
            window.gameState.movesAvailable = ms.movesAvailable || [];
            window.gameState.dice = ms.dice || window.gameState.dice;
            window.gameState.isWaitingToPass = !!ms.isWaitingToPass;
            window.gameState.pendingNextTurn = !!ms.pendingNextTurn;

            // The start screen is called "player-setup-overlay" in the markup.
            // Looking up "setup-overlay" left that full-screen layer in place for
            // guests, so they saw the turn timer but never the board.
            const setupOverlay = document.getElementById('player-setup-overlay');
            const mainMenu = document.getElementById('main-menu-section');
            const localSetup = document.getElementById('local-setup-section');
            const btnOnlineIngame = document.getElementById('online-friends-btn-ingame');
            if (setupOverlay && setupOverlay.style.display !== 'none') {
              setupOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
              setTimeout(() => { setupOverlay.style.display = 'none'; }, 500);
            }
            if (mainMenu) mainMenu.style.display = 'none';
            if (localSetup) localSetup.style.display = 'none';
            if (btnOnlineIngame) btnOnlineIngame.style.display = 'flex';
          }
        }

        function broadcastState() {
          if (!roomCode || !sb || !amHost) return;
          sb.from('rooms').update({
            match_state: {
              pawns: window.gameState.pawns,
              currentPlayer: window.gameState.currentPlayer,
              hasRolled: window.gameState.hasRolled,
              movesAvailable: window.gameState.movesAvailable,
              dice: window.gameState.dice,
              isWaitingToPass: window.gameState.isWaitingToPass,
              pendingNextTurn: window.gameState.pendingNextTurn,
              updatedBy: localSessionId
            }
          }).eq('code', roomCode).then(({ error }) => {
            if (error) console.error('broadcastState failed', error);
          });
        }

        function broadcastMatchStart() {
          if (!roomCode || !sb || !amHost) return;
          const seats = {};
          window.gameConfig.players.forEach((p, i) => {
            seats[i] = {
              sessionId: seatOwners[i] || (i === 0 ? localSessionId : null),
              name: p.name,
              isAI: p.isAI,
              gender: p.gender || 'male'
            };
          });
          sb.from('rooms').update({
            started: true,
            seats: seats,
            match_state: {
              pawns: window.gameState.pawns,
              currentPlayer: window.gameState.currentPlayer,
              hasRolled: false,
              movesAvailable: []
            }
          }).eq('code', roomCode).then(({ error }) => {
            if (error) console.error('broadcastMatchStart failed', error);
          });
        }

        function attachRealtimeChannel(code) {
          if (channel) sb.removeChannel(channel);
          if (roomSnapshotTimer) clearInterval(roomSnapshotTimer);
          roomMessagesEl.innerHTML = '';

          channel = sb.channel('room-' + code)
            .on('postgres_changes', { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'rooms', filter: 'code=eq.' + code },
              (payload) => applyMatchSnapshot(payload.new))
            .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'room_messages', filter: 'room_code=eq.' + code },
              (payload) => appendRoomMessage(payload.new))
            .on('presence', { event: 'sync' }, () => {
              const state = channel.presenceState();
              const names = Object.values(state).flat().map((p) => p.name);
              updateRoomPresenceUI(names);
            })
            .subscribe(async (status) => {
              if (status === 'SUBSCRIBED') {
                await channel.track({ name: localPlayerName(), session_id: localSessionId });
              }
            });

          roomCode = code;
          roomCodeDisplay.innerText = code;
          roomJoinRow.style.display = 'none';
          roomToggleBtn.style.display = 'flex';
          roomToggleBtn.style.alignItems = 'center';
          roomToggleBtn.style.justifyContent = 'center';

          // Realtime can miss updates when a phone browser is in the background.
          // Periodically read the saved room state as a synchronization safety net.
          const loadRoomSnapshot = () => sb.from('rooms')
            .select('started, seats, match_state').eq('code', code).maybeSingle()
            .then(({ data, error }) => {
              if (error) console.error('room snapshot failed', error);
              else if (data && roomCode === code) applyMatchSnapshot(data);
            });
          loadRoomSnapshot();
          roomSnapshotTimer = setInterval(loadRoomSnapshot, 1500);
          if (roomActionTimer) clearInterval(roomActionTimer);
          roomActionTimer = setInterval(processRoomActions, 350);
        }

        function submitAction(action) {
          if (!roomCode || !sb || amHost) return;
          sb.from('room_actions').insert({ room_code: roomCode, session_id: localSessionId, action });
        }

        async function processRoomActions() {
          if (!amHost || !roomCode || !sb) return;
          const { data, error } = await sb.from('room_actions').select('*')
            .eq('room_code', roomCode).eq('status', 'pending').order('id').limit(10);
          if (error || !data) return;
          for (const request of data) {
            const action = request.action || {};
            const validSeat = Number.isInteger(action.player) && seatOwners[action.player] === request.session_id;
            let accepted = false;
            if (validSeat && action.player === window.gameState.currentPlayer) {
              if (action.kind === 'roll' && !window.gameState.isRolling && !window.gameState.isSettling) {
                rollDice(); accepted = true;
              } else if (action.kind === 'move' && window.gameState.hasRolled && Number.isInteger(action.pawn) && Number.isInteger(action.dieIndex)) {
                const steps = window.gameState.movesAvailable[action.dieIndex];
                if (steps !== undefined && movePawn(action.player, action.pawn, steps)) {
                  window.gameState.movesAvailable.splice(action.dieIndex, 1);
                  window.gameState.selectedMoveIndex = null;
                  updateDiceHud();
                  if (!window.gameState.movesAvailable.length) window.gameState.pendingNextTurn = true;
                  else if (!canAnyPawnMove(action.player, window.gameState.movesAvailable)) {
                    window.gameState.isWaitingToPass = true; window.gameState.autoTurnTimer = 1;
                  }
                  broadcastState(); accepted = true;
                }
              }
            }
            await sb.from('room_actions').update({ status: accepted ? 'accepted' : 'rejected' }).eq('id', request.id);
          }
        }

        function showRoomError(message) {
          if (__connectingCountdownTimer) { clearInterval(__connectingCountdownTimer); __connectingCountdownTimer = null; }
          roomJoinRow.style.display = 'flex';
          roomCodeDisplay.innerText = '—';
          roomPresenceDisplay.innerText = 'Not connected';
          roomMessagesEl.innerHTML = `<div style="color:#ff6b6b; text-align:center; padding:20px 10px; line-height:1.5;">${message}</div>`;
          roomPanel.style.display = 'block';
        }

        let __connectingCountdownTimer = null;
        function showConnectingState(label) {
          if (__connectingCountdownTimer) clearInterval(__connectingCountdownTimer);
          let secondsLeft = 15;
          const spinnerFrames = ['⠋','⠙','⠹','⠸','⠼','⠴','⠦','⠧','⠇','⠏'];
          let frame = 0;
          const render = () => {
            roomMessagesEl.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align:center; padding:20px 10px; opacity:0.9;">
              <span style="font-size:20px;">${spinnerFrames[frame % spinnerFrames.length]}</span> ${label}
              <br><span style="font-size:11px; opacity:0.6;">still trying — about ${secondsLeft}s left before giving up</span>
            </div>`;
          };
          render();
          __connectingCountdownTimer = setInterval(() => {
            frame++;
            if (frame % 5 === 0 && secondsLeft > 0) secondsLeft--;
            render();
            if (secondsLeft <= 0 && frame % 5 === 0) {
              clearInterval(__connectingCountdownTimer);
              __connectingCountdownTimer = null;
            }
          }, 200);
        }

        async function hostRoom() {
          if (!ensureSupabaseReady()) {
            showRoomError("The room service isn't configured yet — this build still has placeholder Supabase credentials. Paste your real Project URL and anon key into the code where marked, then try again.");
            return;
          }
          const code = genRoomCode();
          amHost = true;
          mySlot = 0;
          seatOwners = { 0: localSessionId };
          try {
            const seats = { 0: { sessionId: localSessionId, name: localPlayerName(), isAI: false, gender: 'male' } };
            rowSeatsCache = seats;
            const { error } = await withTimeout(
              sb.from('rooms').insert({ code, host: localPlayerName(), started: false, seats }),
              15000, 'create room'
            );
            if (error) throw error;
            attachRealtimeChannel(code);
            roomPanel.style.display = 'block';
            const url = new URL(window.location.href);
            url.searchParams.set('room', code);
            roomToast(`Room ${code} created!`);
            return url.toString();
          } catch (e) {
            const rawDetail = JSON.stringify({
              message: e && e.message, code: e && e.code, details: e && e.details, hint: e && e.hint
            });
            console.error('hostRoom failed:', rawDetail);
            let msg = "Couldn't create the room.";
            if (e && e.message && e.message.indexOf('timeout') === 0) {
              msg = "The connection to the room database timed out after 15 seconds. Try a phone hotspot to test on a different network.";
            } else if (e && e.code === '42501') {
              msg += " This looks like a Row Level Security policy issue — check that you ran the full setup SQL script, including the policies.";
            } else {
              msg += " Check your internet connection and try again.";
            }
            msg += `<br><span style="font-size:10px; opacity:0.7; font-family:monospace;">${rawDetail.replace(/</g,'&lt;')}</span>`;
            showRoomError(msg);
          }
        }

        async function joinRoomByCode(codeRaw, explicitName) {
          if (!ensureSupabaseReady()) {
            showRoomError("The room service isn't configured yet — this build still has placeholder Supabase credentials.");
            return false;
          }
          const code = codeRaw.trim().toUpperCase();
          if (!code) return false;
          try {
            const { data, error } = await withTimeout(
              sb.rpc('claim_seat', {
                p_room_code: code,
                p_session_id: localSessionId,
                p_name: (explicitName && explicitName.trim()) || localPlayerName(),
                p_gender: (window.gameConfig.players[1] && window.gameConfig.players[1].gender) || 'male'
              }),
              15000, 'join room'
            );
            if (error) throw error;
            const claimedSlot = data;
            amHost = false;
            mySlot = claimedSlot;
            seatOwners[claimedSlot] = localSessionId;
            attachRealtimeChannel(code);
            roomPanel.style.display = 'block';
            roomToast(`Joined room ${code} as seat ${claimedSlot + 1}! Waiting for host to start...`);
            return true;
          } catch (e) {
            // Log every field explicitly — logging the raw object alone shows as
            // an unreadable "Object" when copied out of the console as plain text.
            const rawDetail = JSON.stringify({
              message: e && e.message, code: e && e.code, details: e && e.details, hint: e && e.hint
            });
            console.error('joinRoomByCode failed:', rawDetail);
            let msg;
            const emsg = (e && e.message) || '';
            if (emsg.indexOf('not-found') !== -1) msg = `Room ${code} not found. Double-check the code.`;
            else if (emsg.indexOf('already-started') !== -1) msg = 'That match already started without you — ask the host for a new room.';
            else if (emsg.indexOf('room-full') !== -1) msg = 'That room already has 4 friends in it (full).';
            else if (emsg.indexOf('timeout') === 0) msg = "The connection timed out after 15 seconds. Try a phone hotspot to test on a different network.";
            else msg = "Couldn't join the room. Check your internet connection.";
            // Always show the raw detail too, in plain text right on screen, so the
            // real cause is never hidden behind a generic message or a console
            // object that's hard to copy out of a phone browser.
            msg += `<br><span style="font-size:10px; opacity:0.7; font-family:monospace;">${rawDetail.replace(/</g,'&lt;')}</span>`;
            showRoomError(msg);
            return false;
          }
        }

        async function sendRoomMessage() {
          const text = roomChatInput.value.trim();
          if (!text || !roomCode || !sb) return;
          roomChatInput.value = '';
          const { error } = await sb.from('room_messages').insert({
            room_code: roomCode, sender: localPlayerName(), text
          });
          if (error) { console.error('sendRoomMessage failed', error); roomToast('Message failed to send.'); }
        }

        function leaveRoom() {
          if (channel && sb) sb.removeChannel(channel);
          if (roomSnapshotTimer) { clearInterval(roomSnapshotTimer); roomSnapshotTimer = null; }
          if (roomActionTimer) { clearInterval(roomActionTimer); roomActionTimer = null; }
        }
        window.addEventListener('beforeunload', leaveRoom);

        function hasEnteredName() {
          const nameInputEl = document.getElementById('room-name-input');
          return !!(nameInputEl && nameInputEl.value.trim());
        }

        if (roomHostBtn) roomHostBtn.onclick = async (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          if (!hasEnteredName()) { roomToast('Type your name first, so others can see who you are.'); return; }
          roomHostBtn.disabled = true;
          roomJoinBtn.disabled = true;
          showConnectingState('Creating room...');
          try {
            const shareUrl = await hostRoom();
            if (window.__afterHostRoom) window.__afterHostRoom(shareUrl);
          } finally {
            roomHostBtn.disabled = false;
            roomJoinBtn.disabled = false;
          }
        };
        if (roomJoinBtn) roomJoinBtn.onclick = async (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          if (!hasEnteredName()) { roomToast('Type your name first, so others can see who you are.'); return; }
          if (!roomJoinInput.value.trim()) { roomToast('Type a room code first.'); return; }
          roomHostBtn.disabled = true;
          roomJoinBtn.disabled = true;
          showConnectingState('Joining room...');
          try {
            await joinRoomByCode(roomJoinInput.value);
          } finally {
            roomHostBtn.disabled = false;
            roomJoinBtn.disabled = false;
          }
        };
        if (roomChatSendBtn) roomChatSendBtn.onclick = (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); sendRoomMessage(); };
        if (roomChatInput) roomChatInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') sendRoomMessage(); });
        if (roomChatCloseBtn) roomChatCloseBtn.onclick = (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); roomPanel.style.display = 'none'; };
        if (roomToggleBtn) roomToggleBtn.onclick = (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          roomPanel.style.display = (roomPanel.style.display === 'none') ? 'block' : 'none';
        };

        // ===== Invite Link & Guest Name Prompt/Modal System =====
        function askGuestNameModal(code) {
          const existing = document.getElementById('guest-invite-modal-overlay');
          if (existing) existing.remove();

          const overlay = document.createElement('div');
          overlay.id = 'guest-invite-modal-overlay';
