          overlay.style.cssText = 'position:fixed; top:0; left:0; width:100%; height:100%; background:rgba(4,7,15,0.88); backdrop-filter:blur(10px); z-index:999999; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; padding:16px; box-sizing:border-box; font-family:"Fredoka", sans-serif;';

          overlay.innerHTML = `
            <div style="background:linear-gradient(150deg, #182030 0%, #0c1019 100%); border:2px solid #2ecc71; border-radius:24px; padding:28px 22px; width:420px; max-width:92vw; box-shadow:0 25px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.9), 0 0 35px rgba(46,204,113,0.3); text-align:center; color:white; box-sizing:border-box; position:relative;">
              <button id="guest-modal-close-btn" type="button" style="position:absolute; top:14px; right:14px; background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1); color:#aaa; border:none; width:32px; height:32px; border-radius:50%; font-size:15px; cursor:pointer; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; font-weight:bold;">✕</button>
              <div style="font-size:46px; line-height:1; margin-bottom:8px;">🎲</div>
              <h2 style="margin:0 0 6px; font-size:24px; font-weight:700; color:#fff; letter-spacing:0.5px;">Join Multiplayer Room</h2>
              <div style="display:inline-block; background:rgba(46,204,113,0.18); border:1px solid #2ecc71; color:#2ecc71; font-family:monospace; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold; letter-spacing:2px; padding:4px 14px; border-radius:12px; margin-bottom:14px;">
                ROOM CODE: <span style="color:#2ecc71;">${code}</span>
              </div>
              <p style="margin:0 0 16px; font-size:14px; color:#cbd5e1; font-family:'Inter', sans-serif; line-height:1.4;">
                You've been invited to play! Enter your guest or player name below to join the match:
              </p>

              <div style="margin-bottom:14px; text-align:left;">
                <label for="guest-modal-name-input" style="display:block; font-size:11px; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:1px; color:#2ecc71; font-family:'Inter', sans-serif; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:6px;">
                  👤 Your Name
                </label>
                <input type="text" id="guest-modal-name-input" maxlength="16" placeholder="Enter your name..." autocomplete="off" style="width:100%; padding:12px 14px; border-radius:12px; border:2px solid #4a5568; background:rgba(0,0,0,0.6); color:white; font-size:16px; font-family:'Fredoka', sans-serif; outline:none; box-sizing:border-box; transition:border-color 0.2s, box-shadow 0.2s;">
                <div id="guest-modal-error" style="display:none; color:#ff6b6b; font-size:12px; font-family:'Inter', sans-serif; margin-top:6px; font-weight:600;">Please enter your name to join!</div>
              </div>

              <div style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:10px;">
                <button id="guest-modal-confirm-btn" type="button" style="width:100%; background:linear-gradient(135deg, #27ae60, #2ecc71); color:white; font-family:'Fredoka', sans-serif; font-weight:bold; font-size:17px; padding:13px; border:none; border-radius:12px; cursor:pointer; box-shadow:0 4px 15px rgba(46,204,113,0.4); letter-spacing:0.5px; transition:transform 0.15s;">
                  🚀 JOIN ROOM & PLAY
                </button>
                <button id="guest-modal-prompt-btn" type="button" style="background:transparent; border:none; color:#718096; font-size:12px; font-family:'Inter', sans-serif; cursor:pointer; text-decoration:underline;">
                  Or use browser prompt
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;

          document.body.appendChild(overlay);

          const inputEl = document.getElementById('guest-modal-name-input');
          const errorEl = document.getElementById('guest-modal-error');
          const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('guest-modal-confirm-btn');
          const promptBtn = document.getElementById('guest-modal-prompt-btn');
          const closeBtn = document.getElementById('guest-modal-close-btn');
          if (closeBtn) {
            closeBtn.onclick = () => {
              overlay.remove();
              window.__joiningViaInvite = false;
              if (roomPanel) roomPanel.style.display = 'block';
              roomToast('Type your name in the room panel and tap JOIN to enter.');
            };
          }

          if (inputEl) {
            inputEl.addEventListener('focus', () => {
              inputEl.style.borderColor = '#2ecc71';
              inputEl.style.boxShadow = '0 0 12px rgba(46,204,113,0.3)';
              if (errorEl) errorEl.style.display = 'none';
            });
            inputEl.addEventListener('blur', () => {
              inputEl.style.borderColor = '#4a5568';
              inputEl.style.boxShadow = 'none';
            });
            setTimeout(() => inputEl.focus(), 150);
          }

          async function doJoinWithName(entered) {
            if (!entered || !entered.trim()) {
              if (errorEl) {
                errorEl.innerText = 'Please enter your name to join!';
                errorEl.style.display = 'block';
              }
              if (inputEl) {
                inputEl.style.borderColor = '#ff6b6b';
                inputEl.focus();
              }
              return;
            }

            const cleanName = entered.trim();
            if (confirmBtn) {
              confirmBtn.disabled = true;
              confirmBtn.innerText = 'Connecting...';
              confirmBtn.style.opacity = '0.7';
            }

            window.__joiningViaInvite = false;
            localStorage.setItem('ludo_online_name', cleanName);
            const mainNameInput = document.getElementById('room-name-input');
            if (mainNameInput) mainNameInput.value = cleanName;

            overlay.remove();

            if (roomPanel) roomPanel.style.display = 'block';
            showConnectingState(`Joining room ${code} as ${cleanName}...`);

            const success = await joinRoomByCode(code, cleanName);
            if (success) {
              savePlayerName(cleanName);
            }
          }

          if (confirmBtn) {
            confirmBtn.onclick = () => doJoinWithName(inputEl && inputEl.value);
          }
          if (inputEl) {
            inputEl.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
              if (e.key === 'Enter') doJoinWithName(inputEl.value);
            });
          }
          if (promptBtn) {
            promptBtn.onclick = () => {
              const res = window.prompt(`Enter your name to join room ${code}:`);
              if (res && res.trim()) {
                doJoinWithName(res.trim());
              }
            };
          }

          window.__promptGuestName = (customCode) => {
            const targetCode = (customCode || code).toUpperCase();
            const res = window.prompt(`Enter your name to join room ${targetCode}:`);
            if (res && res.trim()) {
              doJoinWithName(res.trim());
            }
          };
        }

        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const inviteRoomFromUrl = (urlParams.get('room') || urlParams.get('invite') || '').trim();
        if (inviteRoomFromUrl) {
          window.__joiningViaInvite = true;
          const cleanCode = inviteRoomFromUrl.toUpperCase();
          // Clear preset / cached guest names
          localStorage.removeItem('ludo_online_name');
          if (roomNameInput) roomNameInput.value = '';
          if (roomJoinInput) roomJoinInput.value = cleanCode;

          // Ask guest to enter name via prompt/modal and sync to room
          askGuestNameModal(cleanCode);
        }

        window.__roomSystem = {
          hostRoom,
          joinRoomByCode,
          isConnected: () => !!roomCode,
          isMySeat,
          mySlot: () => mySlot,
          isHost: () => amHost,
          submitAction,
          broadcastState,
          broadcastMatchStart
        };
      })();
      // ===== end room system =====

      run(window.mode);
    </script>
</html>
